- [ ] The baker's bicycle (vehicles axis, run 85): a bicycle leaned on the bakery wall (two thin-torus wheels, a frame of three boxes, a wicker basket on the front); at 10:00 the baker loads two loaves into the basket and rides to the east village and back — a new `cycle` pose from the owner's board (seated high, knees pumping at the wheel's turn rate, hands on the bars, a 0.3s push-off windup and a 0.4s foot-down stop); the player can take it with `C` (×1.6 walking speed, no running, `C` again to lean it wherever you are), and a leaned bike knocked by a car or a punch falls over until someone (resident or player) stands it back up with `grab`.

- [ ] The tower door and its first band in Godot (Climb axis, run 86 — Climb has 26 open lines and every one is for the frozen web tower): `game/scenes/games/climb.tscn` + `climb3d.gd` — a stone core cylinder (r 2.5m) with twelve seeded box platforms spiralling up one band (24m), the town's own `Stick3D` and jump; falling below the first platform puts you back on it with a `land` squat; the top platform has a bench (`town_meals.sit_bench` shape) and a "Rest floor." toast. In the town: a narrow tower door on the east plaza (`_tower_door` in `town_places.gd`) — `C` enters, leaving the scene returns you to the door. Resident half: the jogger enters the door at 07:00, is gone 6 minutes, and comes back out saying "Twelve."
- [ ] A child at the end of the row (children axis, run 86, builds on Pass the plate; needs "Two children and a hand to hold"): a child seated at a bench end never passes an item on (`onward` 0) and eats it with a new `nibble` pose (both hands at the mouth, six small bites at 0.35s instead of three at 0.9s, shoulders hunched, feet swinging off the seat at 1.5Hz); a parent seated beside a child passes to the child first, and the child says "Mine?" before the first bite. The player handing the child something by hold-`C` gets a wave from the parent next morning.
- [ ] Empty cups go back (economy/props, run 86, builds on Pass the plate — the cup is the one thing that now travels a bench and then sits in a hand forever): `C` on a held cup drinks it in three sips like food's bites (meta `sips`, the cup tilts further each sip), then it is `empty` (lighter colour, no steam); a resident holding an empty cup walks it to a new cup crate beside the café counter (a slatted box, up to eight cups visible) and the café's resident clears the crate at 16:00 with a `carry` trip inside; the player returns one with `C` on the crate and the café resident says "Ta." — a cup is never thrown on the grass by anyone.
- [ ] A bus to the lakeside (vehicles axis, run 87 — the three open-world sites are reachable only on foot or by stealing a car, and the first transit route would make the world feel joined): the `van` becomes a resident-driven bus on a fixed loop hub bus stop (x −30, z 4.6) → main road east → the lakeside site's `from` road → a stop sign at the lakeside village → back, 10s wait at each end; `C` at a stop while it waits boards as a passenger (the `town_ride` passenger seat already exists), `C` again asks to get off at the next stop; two residents a day ride it to the lakeside and walk back (the reverse rule), and the driver says the stop name ("Lakeside." / "Square.") on arrival.
- [ ] A watermill at the cabin brook (places axis, run 87 — the open-world sites are three; the first one with a building that does work): a small timber mill on the forest cabin's flattened ring beside a new short brook (`Water3D.band`, 2m wide, from the cabin ring edge to the nearest lake), a wheel that turns with the brook flow (`rotation.x += delta * 1.2`), a millstone inside the door; `C` with a plot crop at the millstone plays a new `mill` pose (both hands on the crank, a 1.2s circle with the hips, a 0.3s wipe of the brow) — and hands back a flour sack item the bakery counter accepts (one restock); the woodcutter resident (owner priority item) carries its logs past it.
- [ ] Hills on the horizon (places/terrain axis, run 87, builds on the performance pass — streaming is now cheap enough to show more): beyond `RADIUS` draw a second ring of chunks at `RADIUS + 1 .. RADIUS + 3` as ground-only, 4×4-cell, no nature and no collision (one `ArrayMesh` each, built one per frame after the near ring is complete), so the lone green plane at y −2.5 is only seen past ~350m and hills and lakes read on the skyline from the lookout terrace; `probe_perf.gd` must still show a worst streaming frame < 4ms.
- [ ] A taxi fare (vehicles + economy axis, run 111 — the taxi has driven all day since 2026-09-30 and nobody has paid for a ride): a small dish on the taxi's dashboard (`Car3D` child, three disc slots like the till); a passenger who boards (the existing passenger seat from `_interact_check` "car" when `c.driver is Resident`) drops one coin in with `palm` played backwards (coin leaves the hand at PALM_AWAY) if they have one — with none the driver says `mind.line("fare")` and drives anyway; a resident whose picked spot is more than 40 m away and who has a coin waits at the road edge with `wait` and raises a hand (`lwave` family) when the taxi is within 12 m, the taxi `still`s 3 s for them to board and drops them at the waypoint nearest their spot; at the 17:00 edge the driver palms the dish into their pocket. Plain outcome: with no coin you walk, as today; the taxi never refuses.
- [ ] U-turn right of way (vehicles, polish run 93 — `probe_traffic` shows the taxi and the delivery van meeting at the east/west U-turns, x ±70..78, once or twice per 90s and sometimes 4–5 times with 9–20s stood still; run 87's code does it too, so it is old, not a recent regression): each U-turn box (|x| > 70, z −0.5..4.5) is claimed by the first AI car to enter it (`town.uturn[side] = car`, cleared when it leaves the box); a second car arriving sets `tspd` 0 at the box edge and waits, its driver says `mind.line("wait_turn")` once. Pin it: three `probe_traffic` runs with car-car crashes ≤ 1 each. (A blind-reverse rear ray was tried in run 93 and reverted: it did not move the numbers and could pin a car boxed in front and behind.)
- [ ] A road mender by name (jobs axis — the thinnest at eight open `game/` lines, run 113; builds on wages, which made every job pay but gave the roads nobody): one seeded jobless adult (`uid % 24 == 19`, job "mender", a grey cap) walks the main road's kerb 09:00–12:00 to pothole props that appear where `probe_traffic`'s wrecks happen (a 0.6 m dark disc flush with the road at x 31–65, at most three at once, one new per wreck, outside the lanes' raycast band so no car steers at it); at each they `tamp` — a new pose: 0.3 s lifting a rammer (a box on `hand_r`) to the chest, 1.6 s dropping and lifting it twice a second with the shoulders rolling and the knees giving on each drop, 0.3 s shouldering it — and the pothole fills (`wage.mend` 1 through `wage`, a `paid` line); the player can tamp with C at a pothole holding the rammer from the mender's barrow (same pose, same pay); an untamped pothole bumps a passing car (`_vy` 0.25, a short squash), and a `mind.score` term sends the mender back out after rain, when two new potholes open.

- [ ] (vehicles, run 115 — the thinnest axis) The woodcutter's handcart: a two-wheeled wooden cart (bed 0.9×0.5, two spoked wheels, two handles; `Car3D`-free — a pushed prop under `props3d.gd` with a `push` pose family from the owner's board: 0.3 s lean into the handles, a walk cycle with the torso 20° forward, 0.3 s straighten) that holds four logs at once; it stands by the stump, C on the handles grabs it (the figure walks at 0.8× with the cart in front, logs ride in the bed; C again lets go), C at the pile with a loaded cart stacks all four one `pile_put` per 0.4 s; the woodcutter uses it when three or more loose logs lie around the stump (`loose_logs` ≥ 3) and a resident with a "logs" card does too. One run.
- [ ] (vehicles axis, run 116 — the thinnest axis with Climb: the delivery van has driven the loop since 2026-09-30 and has never delivered anything) The van delivers to the general store: at the 09:00 edge the AI `delivery` van leaves its route, pulls onto the lane shoulder outside the open general store (nose to the kerb, two orange rear cubes blinking 0.5 s as hazard lamps), the driver gets out with a crate (`_box` 0.5×0.4×0.4 wood, two-hand `carry` pose), walks it to the store door, sets it down with `stoop`, and every sold-out `ShopKit` kind in that store refills +2 (the keeper says a new `restocked` line); the driver walks back and the van rejoins the route within 60 s (probe: stock before/after, van waypoint index). The player can carry the crate off first — then the shelves stay empty today and the driver says `that was yours?`. Plain outcome: one stop a day, the loop runs as before.

- [ ] Big pieces come by van (vehicles axis, run 117 — the sofa and the bed are carried home as a shoebox; the delivery car on the main road carries nothing): a sofa or bed bought at the catalogue table is not handed over but *ordered* — the keeper says "The van brings that.", the box goes on the shop's back shelf with your name, and the existing AI `delivery` car (town3d, `tc.route`) gets a detour: at its next pass of the square it leaves the loop to the kerb nearest your door (the U-turn points stay; lane rule: it stops in the verge z 4.1+, never in a lane), a resident driver steps out, carries the box with the two-hand `carry` pose to your doormat and drives back to the loop; the box waits on the mat as a ground item until you carry it in and `plonk` it. Residents' sofas and beds arrive the same way at their doors. Plain outcome: chairs and lamps still walk home in your hand; only the two big kinds wait for the van.

## Quests and relationships in `game/` (thin axes, 2026-09-28 polish run 67 — after the jobs/vehicles lines landed these two were the only axes with no `game/` line at all; the web has both, the 3D town has neither)
- [ ] Fetch the little ones (quests axis — ten open lines, the second-thinnest, run 108; builds on the nap corner): at 12:50 the storysitter stands at the lean-to door and, to a player within 3 m, says `mind.line("nap_ask")` (new category: "Find the small ones. Cots are made.") — the ask is the quest, no board and no marker; the children are wherever their parents are (the `follow` spot), and a player `C` on a child (the greet branch, `job == "child"`) sends them to a free cot through the rocker's existing `nap["call"]` path with `nap_call`; both children on cots by 13:10 gets `mind.line("nap_thanks")` from the storysitter and `mind.befriend(player, 0.15)` — a favour remembered per resident; residents do the half too: a parent whose child is called walks them to the door side by side (run 95 `pair_walk`) if within 20 m. Plain outcome: the children come on their own at 13:00 as now; the ask only lets you be early.
- [ ] Two children and a hand to hold (relationships-and-children axis, run 73 — the axis had no child in it at all): two roster residents by seed (`uid % 24 in [7, 19]`) are children — the rig at `scale = 0.75` (`Stick3D` is a Node3D, so the whole figure scales; the capsule collider shrinks to match), they *run* between spots (`RUN` with the run cycle, the first residents who run outside a chase), never sit longer than 3–5s, weight the playground's swing/slide spots ×4 and the terrace tree ×2, and skip the lamp lean, the counter and the plot. Their parent is the roster row before them (`uid - 1`): at dusk (`is_night()` flips) the parent's `_pick_spot` goes to the child first and the two walk home together in a `handh` pose pair (side by side 0.45m apart, the inner arms lowered toward each other and swinging in step, the child's steps shorter — `stick3d_poses.gd` + `stickman.ts`, the same pose on both), the child released at the door. Player half: a child you wave at waves back with both arms (`cheer` from the reaction family) and follows you for 8s at 1m the way the dog does, then runs home. No dialogue needed — the first relationship the town shows without a line.
- [ ] A resident asks for an apple (quests): a seated resident with an empty hand (`carrying_kind == ""`, on a bench or chair) sets a `want = "apple"` once per sit and says "An apple, if you see one." — the town's first ask. `C` on them while carrying an apple (the existing greet branch, but with something in hand) hands it over: they `hold()` it, say "Obliged.", and eat it in three bites with the same `eat` pose and shrink-per-bite the player has (the resident half of eating — today residents never eat), then drop the core as a loose item. They remember: a `favours` count per resident makes their next greeting "Hello again." instead of "Hello." — the first reputation effect in `game/`, no number shown. Refusing is the other branch: a resident who isn't hungry says "No, thank you." (plain outcome ≥ 40%: only seated empty-handed residents ask, everyone else refuses). This is the quest half of Items / "Give"; build it there or here, not twice.
- [ ] Fetch it back from the den (quests axis, run 69 — closes the fox slice of Scavengers): when the fox takes a resident's thing, the resident stores `lost = {kind, where: "den"}` and, once up, greeting them (`E`/`C`) gets "The fox has my cup. Past the pond." instead of "Hello."; `C` on them while carrying an item of that kind hands it over (the "Give" verb from Items, first use): they hold it again (`carrying_kind` restored), say "That is mine. Thank you.", clear `lost`, and remember you (`thanks += 1` on the resident — the seed of per-resident reputation: at 2 they greet you by handle). If you never bring it, they walk to the den themselves after 60s and pick it up (residents do what players do).
- [ ] Neighbours wave (relationships axis, polish run 71): residents whose `home_door`s are neighbours in `doors` (i and i+1 on the same street) wave to each other (`wave` pose, both, facing) when both are in `routine` within 4m, once per 90s per pair, with a one-word line ("Morning." / "Evening." by `is_night()`); the player's own `wave` near a resident gets a wave back and marks that resident `met` — the second time they add the player's name. Task: "Wave to three neighbours on the north lane."
- [ ] Waving at a pair (relationships — the player half left from the old seed-based "Two who walk together" line, which run 95 replaced with friend-tie pairs in `resident_pair.gd`): today greeting either walker of a linked pair breaks the link (the greet spot is not an outside kind). Instead: `greet` on a paired resident makes both stop and turn to the player (two `wave`s, the follower 0.3s later), one `mind.greet_line()` each, and the pair resumes side by side toward the same spot (keep `pair`, re-aim the follower) — a second greet inside 20s gets one wave from the lead only. Plain outcome stays: the greet always lands.
- [ ] Bring the punt over (quests axis, run 94 — the first ask that needs a vehicle): a resident who reaches a landing on a ferry route while the punt is tied at the other one re-plans over the stones (run 94) one time in two and otherwise waits on the deck and asks the nearest player within 8m, "Could you bring the punt across?" (`want = "punt"`, `mind.line("want_punt")`); if the player rows it to that landing within 90s and steps off, the resident says thanks, boards and rows themselves over (`board(r, true)`), and `mind.befriend` +0.08; past 90s they shrug (`shrug` fidget) and take the stones. Never more than one waiting asker per landing.
- [ ] The dropped umbrella (quests axis, run 76 — the rack is the first shared thing a resident can lose): a resident knocked down while holding a borrowed umbrella (`has_umb` → false in `hit`) sets `lost_umb = true` and, until it clears, greeting them gets "I dropped the café's umbrella. Somewhere by the stalls." (or "The fox has it." if the den holds an `umbrella` item); the player returning *any* umbrella to the rack (`rack_put`) while that resident is within 12m clears it and they say "Good of you." and wave — the first quest a weather slice hands out, no new synced state.
- [ ] A knock on a neighbour's door (relationships axis, run 77 — the chimneys now say who is home, so a visit can be a decision, not a coincidence): when a resident in `routine` passes within 3m of a house whose chimney is smoking (`_someone_home`) and the owner (`home_door` match) is a pair-neighbour (i and i+1 in `doors`), once per 4 minutes per pair they walk to the door, play `knock` (three raps from the elbow, 0.25s each, wrist only, then a step back and a wait — its own timing, in `stick3d_poses.gd` + `stickman.ts`), the owner leaves their chair/bed for the door spot, and the two exchange one seeded line pair ("Tea?" → "Not today.") before the visitor moves on; the player knocking (`C` at a closed door of a smoking house while nobody is out front) gets the owner to the door and "Yes?" — a door that opens for you because someone is home.
- [ ] A friend by name (relationships axis, polish run 79 — the minds now hold `friends[uid]` and `rel` friend/rival tags, but two friends only meet when a spot throws them together): once per 3 minutes a resident whose `mind.company` is under 0.35 picks their best-liked resident (`relation_k` highest, > 0.3) instead of a spot — `spot = {kind: "visit", who: r}` — walks to within 1.2m of wherever that resident is (re-targeting every 2s while they move, `via_bridge` for the route), and on arrival calls `_chat_force` with that partner regardless of the 2m-and-standing filter, so the two talk where the friend was (a bench, the lookout, the plot); if the friend is asleep or driving they turn back with "Another time." and `company` is not refilled. Player half: greeting a resident whose `fond` is over 0.5 twice within a minute makes them walk with you for 30s (the `walk-with` mode from "Two who walk together", 0.8m behind) — the first resident who follows the player without being pushed. Task: "Find who Iris is looking for."
- [ ] Saving a seat (relationships axis, run 85, builds on Halves on a bench): a resident seated on a bench whose friend (`relation_k` > 0.5) is walking within 12m lays a flat hand on the free slot beside them (a new `save` pose: 0.3s reach down, hold with a pat every 1.5s, 0.3s lift) and waves with the other hand; the friend's next pick is that bench. Anyone else sitting there gets "That one's taken." but sits anyway (plain outcome); the player gets a seat saved too once a resident's `fond` > 0.6, and can save one for a resident the same way (`C` held while seated).
- [ ] The woodcutter's kid (thin axis: children, polish run 93): a small resident (`fig.base_scale` 0.65, new handle in `minds.json` `people` with `rel` parent → the woodcutter) lives at the forest cabin; by day they carry one loose log at a time from around the stump to the pile with a new `haul` pose (log hugged to the chest with both forearms, quicker shorter steps, a lean back on every fourth step) and the woodcutter says `mind.line("kid_watch")`; after dark, if the stove is lit, the kid sits on the cabin bed facing it. The player can hand the kid a log (`give`) → `mind.befriend(player, 0.1)` and the kid stacks it; task "help the woodcutter's kid with the stack" (complete: 3 logs given).
- [ ] A hand to hold on the walk (children axis, run 95, needs "Two children and a hand to hold"): a child walking with its parent uses the pair link (`resident_pair.gd`) with the parent always the lead — `SIDE` 0.4 instead of 0.6, the child's pace clamp 0.9..1.6 (short quick steps), and the near hands joined: a new `hold` arm override (child's arm up 1.1 rad, parent's down-out 0.3 rad, both elbows soft) while both walk; a hit on the parent makes the child run behind the parent (`flee` from the hitter, 3m) instead of bickering — children do not quarrel with parents.
- [ ] A child watches a quarrel (children axis, run 95): a child within 5m of a `bicker` beat stops, faces it and plays a new `peek` pose (0.3 hands to the mouth → 1.0 still, head tilting between the two → 0.3 drop) and then copies the last jab once, small (`bicker` at half amplitude via `base_scale` 0.65 — the pose is shared), saying a `child_copy` line; the nearest adult says "Don't." — no other effect.
- [ ] Walk me there (quests axis, run 95 — builds on Two friends walk together; quests is the thinnest axis with four open lines and no quest system in `game/` yet): a resident with `mind.company` < 0.3 and `fond` > 0.2 who sets off to an outside spot ≥ 8m away while the player stands within 3m says "Walk with me?" (new `minds.json` `walk_ask`) and a mauve label `walk with` floats over them for 6s; if the player stays within 1.5m of them for 80% of the walk (sampled each 0.5s), on arrival they turn their head in (the `look_yaw` turn), say a `walk_thanks` line, `fond` +0.06 and `company` +0.3; drifting off says "Never mind." once and nothing else happens. The resident half already exists (friend pairs); this is the player's seat in it.
- [ ] Two logs for the woodcutter (quests axis, run 95 — the woodpile is the one workplace whose output another place uses up): at 16:00 when the stack holds fewer than 3, the woodcutter at the stump calls to a player within 6m ("Two to the stack?", `minds.json` `wood_ask`); carrying two split `log`s from the stump to the stack inside 90s gets "Obliged." and fond +0.05, and the woodcutter skips one ferry trip and sits on the stump in the `wait` pose for 20s instead — the first time the player's work changes a resident's routine. Ignoring it is fine; the woodcutter ferries as before.

## Economy in `game/` (thin axis, polish run 71 — the 3D town has counters that hand out bread and coffee for nothing and not one coin; the web's "Coins in play" never crossed over)
- [ ] Firewood for bread (economy axis, run 90 — the first barter, and it works before the coin exists): the bakery oven burns wood too — a stack of three logs beside it (`town_places._oven`) shrinks by one per bake; `C` at the oven holding a `log` refills it and the baker hands over a warm `bread` (`counter_take` bypass, one per two logs delivered, counted on the oven); the woodcutter carries two logs from the cabin stack to the oven once a day at 10:00 when the oven stack is under two, and when the oven is out of wood the baker says `mind.line("no_wood")` and does not bake.
- [ ] Sell a crop at the market stall (economy axis, grows from Garden plot): `C` at a market stall holding a `tomato`/`cabbage`/`pumpkin` hands it over for one coin (after the coin item above), the stallholder resident (the one whose routine spot is that stall) plays `grab` and puts it on the stall top as a visible item that the next resident to pass buys and carries off. Residents who picked a crop (`resident.gd` plot branch) walk it to the stall too — the same sale, the same coin.
- [ ] The telescope takes a coin (economy axis, polish run 75 — every coin sink so far is food; the town needs one that buys a *view*): once the coin item above and the terrace telescope (Grows from Lookout terrace) exist, the `scope` view costs one coin — a slot box on the post, the coin item leaves the hand with a `clink` (a 0.1s wrist flick on the `scope` step-in, no new pose) and the 4s far view runs; with no coin the eyepiece shows nothing (the pose still plays, the camera stays — the plain outcome is you looked and saw the rail) and the resident at the lookout says "It takes a coin." Residents carrying a coin (a fourth of the roster, the same `id % 4` trick as the apple/cup/paper carriers) spend it there one loop in three, so the slot fills through the day and the player can pry it open at dusk with `C` for what is inside — the town's first coin that comes back.
- [ ] Umbrella deposit (economy axis, run 76 — every coin sink so far is one-way; the rack is the first thing you get money *back* from): once the coin item above exists, borrowing from the rack costs one coin held as a deposit (a small `Label3D` on the bucket: "1 coin, back on return"), `rack_put` hands it back with a `grab` flip; residents pay and get it back the same way (a coin appears on the rim for 0.5s), and a resident who lost theirs (the quest line under Quests) is out a coin until someone returns it — the first coin that moves in both directions.
- [ ] A tip for the punt (economy axis, polish run 79 — every coin line above waits on the coin item; this one gives the jetty a reason to exist in the economy the day the coin lands): once the coin item above exists, a resident who rides the punt (`board`, run 78) leaves one coin on the thwart when they step off (`unboard` spawns a `coin` item at `boat_seat()` on the plank, 60% by seed — plain outcome: nothing left), visible glinting in the hull until someone rows or steps aboard and `C`s it up; the player rowing a resident as a passenger ("Rowing with a passenger's weight" under Grows from Jetty and punt) gets the coin handed over instead with "For the crossing." — the first coin earned by moving someone, not something.
- [ ] Tea for two is paid (economy axis, run 96 — builds on the pair's table slice): once the coin item exists, a pair that makes up at the café's pair table pays one coin from the one who started the quarrel (`_bicker_with` side) into the counter's till (a short stack of discs on the counter, up to five, emptied by the baker at dusk with a `count` pose: 0.3 lean → 0.8 two-finger sweep of the discs into a palm → 0.3 pocket); the player sitting with a fond resident pays the same coin from the HUD pocket, refused with "One coin." when empty (the plain tea without the table still costs nothing).
- [ ] The cobbler's tin (economy axis, run 80 — the first trade that could be *paid*, not just the first shop): once the coin item above exists, a small tin on the workbench (a short cylinder) shows its coins as stacked flat discs (up to five, then it empties itself into the cobbler's pocket at 17:00 with a `grab`); the player's resole costs one coin dropped in with `grab` (no coin → the cobbler still resoles, says "Next time." and remembers it — the `mind.fond` nudge down 0.05, never a refusal), and a resident customer drops one too (a visual coin from the hand; residents have no wallets yet, so it is minted, not spent). Hammering at the bench yourself puts nothing in the tin.
- [ ] Fish for a loaf (economy axis, polish run 97 — every coin line waits on the coin item; the bakery already takes firewood in barter, so a second barter works today): a `fish` (run 91's catch, carried, not yet bitten) handed at the bakery counter with `C` buys one loaf — the baker plays the existing counter hand-over, the fish goes into a wicker creel on the counter (0–4 tails shown), and at 12:00 the baker carries the creel indoors and the oven smokes one extra puff; a resident who catches a fish and is not hungry (`mind.full` > 0.6) takes it to the counter instead of eating it. Plain outcome is the swap every time; no stock rule, no price.
- [ ] Services have prices (economy axis — nine open lines, run 113; builds on wages, which pay the worker from the air): `prices.json` `buy` gets `resole` 2, `sharpen` 1, `mend` 2; at the stool/whet/fitting the player pays through `can_pay` (the refusal line is the worker's own: "Two coins, and I will have it done."), the coin moving pocket → worker's pocket with the `pass` beat instead of a `wage` from nowhere (the `wage` call stays for resident customers, who pay one from their own pocket when they have it); a broke resident customer gets a chalk line on the bench (the ledger's `tab` shape, a `tab_trade` key on the mind, settled on the next visit with a coin); hammering, grinding or sewing at the bench yourself still costs and pays nothing.

## Weather in `game/` (thin axis, run 68 — the 3D town has rain but nothing reacts to it except shelter)
- [ ] Wet wood (weather axis, run 90 — the split stack beside the cabin has no roof): while `weather == "rain"` the top row of the stack darkens (`log` material to `8a7356`), a log taken from a wet row carries meta `wet` and stays dark for two minutes; a wet log in the stove (slice 3 of the sites item) hisses — grey smoke from the cabin chimney instead of white and the stove glow at half — and the woodcutter says `mind.line("wet_wood")` and stops chopping in rain until the lean-to below exists.
- [ ] Mud on the soles after rain (weather axis, run 80 — the first thing rain leaves *on a person*; feeds the cobbler): walking on the meadow grass (`z > RIVER_S`) while raining or within 60s after counts double toward `walked` (player `_trades` and `resident_life._process` alike), and whoever came off the grass leaves brown footprints (two small flat boxes per stride, `8a6a4a` at 60% alpha, fading over 20s, at most 40 alive) on the cobbles and paths for the next 30m they walk; residents on the meadow in rain do the same — a wet afternoon fills the cobbler's stool. Nothing is blocked and no speed changes (that is "Wet and slow, dry and quick", not this).
- [ ] The fox dens up in rain (weather axis, run 69): while `weather == "rain"` the fox walks to its den, drops whatever it carries in front of the hole first, and lies (`lie`) in the entrance with only its head out (the quad rig's `lie` with `rump.position.z` pushed back into the mound), ignoring drops and prey until the rain ends — the first animal whose day changes with the weather; ducks meanwhile stop circling and sit on the pond rim (`fed`-style head tuck) so the park reads "rained out" at a glance.
- [ ] The river rises in rain: while `weather == "rain"` the river surface (`town_places.gd` water box) lifts 4cm over a minute and darkens toward `6f8fa0`, the glints speed up (already ×1.6) and the two `bank` spots are dropped from the rain pool; after rain it drains back over two minutes and leaves three puddles (flat discs) on the meadow path that dry one by one. A resident caught on the meadow when it starts crosses the bridge with the rain `hurry` speed and says "The river." once.
- [ ] A dry spell wilts the plot (weather axis, run 70 — rain already waters every row): after three minutes of `clear`, a ripe row left unpicked for two minutes wilts — leaf blobs squash to 0.7 height and go olive (`8a8a3f`), the fruit drops to the soil as a loose item anyone can pick, and the row falls back to stage 2; watering a wilted row restores it in one step. The first crop you can lose by neglect; a resident arriving at a wilted row says "Too late." instead of watering.
- [ ] Rain reaches the terrace (weather axis, polish run 75 — the terrace is the one place with no eave: a resident at the lookout or the terrace bench when rain starts re-picks from the door pool and walks all the way down the steps to a house front): `_terrace` registers a `canopy` spot 1.3m south of its apple tree (outside the tree's 1.0m `C` radius, so shaking stays shaking — 100%), listed in `_pick_spot`'s rain pool alongside `door` and weighted ×3 for anyone already on the shelf (`ground_y > 0`), where they stand in `storm` facing the town under the crown — the crown *is* the eave; `C` at that spot in rain plays `storm` for the player too (cleared by moving, like the door), and the crown's `_wind` amplitude doubles while someone stands under it in rain so the shelter reads as a tree, not a roof. The resident's arrival line: "Good enough."
- [ ] Morning fog on the river (weather-and-time axis, polish run 83 — the clock has a dawn but nothing happens in it): between 05:00 and 08:00 on the town clock a low fog lies over the river band — eight flat white boxes (alpha 0.35, unshaded, y 0.25) drifting west at the current's speed, fading out by 08:00 and never on a rain day; the punt's bow gets a small lantern (a mauve-capped glass box, `OmniLight3D` range 3) that lights whenever fog is up, whoever rows; residents who pick `bank` in fog stand in the existing `shade` pose (hand over the eyes) toward the bend and say "Can't see the far side." once; the player on the bridge in fog gets the same line from the nearest resident. Fog never slows or blocks anything (holiday rule) — it is something to see at dawn, and a reason to be out at dawn.

- [ ] Wet stone, no sparks (weather axis, run 81 — the cutler already stays home in rain via `_trade_pick`, but the wheel itself does not know it rained): while `weather == "rain"` the trough overflows into a flat puddle disc (`8fb8cc`, alpha 0.6) that grows 0 → 0.5m over the rain and shrinks back over 60s of sun; for those 60s the stone is dark (`6f6a64`), a `grind` loop throws no sparks and the first one shows the toast "Too wet to spark." — the loop, the spin and the treadle still play (nothing blocks `C`), and the residents' customer detour resumes only when the disc is pale again.
- [ ] Rain stops the set (weather-and-time axis, run 111 — the busker is the first job done in the open with a prop that must not get wet): when `weather` turns to `rain` during a set the busker goes straight into `strum`'s 0.4 s lowering (`strum_end`), steps off, stoops the cap's coins and walks the guitar to the nearest lane door eave, standing `storm` there with the guitar on their back until it clears; a set cut by rain does not mark the slot `played`, so if the sky clears inside the slot they go back and finish it. The player on the crate gets the same: rain clears `strum` and a toast 'Rain. The set is off.' Plain outcome: a dry day is unchanged.
- [ ] Rain on the glass front (weather axis, run 103 — the Sunroom has the only glazed wall in town and rain does nothing to it): while `weather == "rain"` each of the four panes gets eight thin unshaded quads (`dfe6ea`, alpha 0.35, 0.02 wide) sliding down the pane at 0.4m/s and respawning at the top at staggered x, cleared when the rain stops; story time in rain: children take an umbrella from the rack on the way (run 76 `take_umbrella`, `umbr` pose), lean it at a new `umbrellas` spot by the Sunroom door (three slots, visible as leaning sticks) and take it back on the way out; the player entering with an umbrella `C` at the door post leans it the same way, and a resident who finds the rack empty because of story hour says `mind.line("rack_empty")`.

- [ ] A wet box warps (weather axis, run 117 — a flat-pack is cardboard and rain does nothing to it): a `flatpack` carried for more than 20 s in `weather == "rain"` without an umbrella darkens (`c9a27a` → `8a6a4a`) and gets meta `wet`; a wet box `plonk`ed in a house becomes a piece that leans — its root yaws ±0.06 on a slow sine and the `sit` spot carries `wobble` so the sitter's idle sways with it — until the 08:00 edge dries it (the lean stops, the meta clears); a resident caught with a wet box says `mind.line("wet_box")` at the door. Umbrellas (run 76) keep it dry. Plain outcome: a dry day changes nothing, and a wet piece still works.
- [ ] (time of day, run 115) Morning post to the odd-jobs board: at the 08:00 edge, if no card is up and the letter room has stock (`letterwall.stock` > 0), the scribe walks from the letter room to the odd-jobs board with one letter (sort out of the wall, carry, `pin` on the board) and pins it as a mailed-in card — "Knead one" if the counter is 0, else "Two logs to the stove" — paid from the scribe's own pocket (needs a coin; wages for the scribe are a separate item); the card reads like the others and the town's needs reach the board on mornings when nobody walked by. At 17:00 the scribe takes it down like any poster. One run.

## Items, wearables and their verbs (owner, 2026-09-28; depth over breadth; `game/`)
- [ ] Try before you buy (items axis — the thinnest at five tagged open lines, run 114; builds on the general store): the Change Room hatstand (`town_props._hatstand`) stops handing out free hats, which today undercuts the store — `C` there lets you *try on* the next hat of `ShopKit.MIX["cap"]` (don, a small "try-on" label, meta `tryon`) for 20 s, then it lifts off by itself (doff) and vanishes back onto the stand; buying stays at the store, the wallet never moves. Residents passing the stand (the `keeper` role already lists `hatstand`) try one on the same way and say `mind.line("tryon")` (add to `minds.json` default). Probe: a tried-on hat is gone after 20 s, `coins` unchanged, `records["worn"]` unchanged.
- [ ] Hand a torn thing over the table (items axis, run 82 — today a torn backpack is only mended while worn): `C` at the tailor's stool *holding* a torn wearable (taken off with the hat-take-off slot or picked up after a knock-down) puts it on the table, the tailor plays one `sew` loop on it there instead of on your back, and hands it back with `grab`; a resident who finds a torn wearable on the ground (the fox's drops and knock-downs leave them) carries it to the stool the same way. The item keeps its `torn` meta wherever it lies, so the patch is visible on the ground too.
- [ ] Wearables v1: cap, beanie, top hat, straw hat, glasses, sunglasses, scarf, backpack — primitives on the rig's sockets; found in the world (a hat stand at the market stall), picked with C, worn with C while held, taken off with C long-press; residents wear a seeded set so the town has variety.
- [ ] Clothes: a shirt and trousers as colour bands on the capsule bones (two colours per person), shop that sells them (buy with coins), the web's figureColor stays as the "skin" ink colour.
- [ ] Café loop (partially shipped, 2026-09-28 — a `counter` spot at the market café hands over a free `cup` on `C`, carried, and `C` again plays the `drink` pose): **a real half-interaction** — unlike bread, drinking never finishes: there is no sip counter (`bites`'s cup equivalent), so the cup never empties, can never be binned, and playing `drink` again is the only thing `C` ever does with it. Needs a `sips` counter (same 3-and-gone shape as `bites`) that turns the cup into an ordinary bin-able empty-cup item on the third sip, per GROW.md's "interactions must be detailed all the way down" rule. No wait-timer (4–20s) either — order and cup-in-hand are instant.
- [ ] Give: C on a resident while carrying → offer; they take it (thank you line) or refuse (dry line); reputation +1 per resident when they take it.
- [ ] Umbrella: item that opens over the head in rain (pose `umbrella`), residents fetch theirs from home when it rains.
- [ ] Balls: a ball item that bounces (RigidBody3D), kick it with Z, dog fetches it, kids' resident job plays with it in the park.
- [ ] Balloon and small toys (thin axis: items, 2026-09-28 — the Assets backlog already lists a balloon-on-a-string and a small gift box as SVG entries but neither has a verb line here): a carried balloon tugs and bobs on its string instead of holding rigidly like other carried items and pops if thrown at a prop or a resident (a `pop` sound-and-burst, nothing left to pick up); a wrapped gift box can be given to a resident (reuses the "Give" line above) and unwrapped on the spot for a small reveal line, distinct from ordinary items in that giving it always gets a thank-you, never a refusal.
- [ ] The book that came back damp (items axis, polish run 105 — the book box, the letter room and the Sunroom all pass `book` around and a book never changes): a `book` dropped outdoors in rain, or knocked into the river and fished out (the run 91 rod path), gets meta `damp` and a wavy darker cover (`material_override` lerped toward `8a6f5a`); `C` on a damp book while held plays a new `fan` pose (0.2s open it face-down in both hands, 0.6s three quick flaps with the pages spread, 0.3s close and tuck) that dries it 30% per play; a damp book cannot go in the book box (the librarian says `mind.line("book_damp")` and props it open on the box lid to dry for 60s, the same lid slot the player can use) and residents carrying one in rain stop under the nearest eave (run 74) to `fan` it. Task: the storysitter refuses a damp book for the armchair ("Not that one. It smells of the river.") — a reason to dry it first.

## Grows from Bakery loop (run 72 — the first shop that runs out and restocks; deepen it before the café copies it)
- [ ] "Knead one" (quests axis): a resident who finds the counter sold out (`_arrive` "counter", the "Could someone knead one?" line) stays 8s at the counter with `want = "bread"` instead of leaving at 2s; if the counter's stock rises while they wait (player or baker kneaded) they buy it, say "Obliged." to whoever kneaded (the oven's `by`) and remember them (+1, the greeting becomes "You baked." for the rest of the day) — the game's first quest with a completion check, ported from the web's `questFor` shape.
- [ ] A queue at the counter: a second resident arriving at a taken counter slot does not pick another spot — they stop 0.7m behind the first, facing the same way, in the `wait` pose from "Standing room only" (Beds and seats), and step up when the slot frees; three deep at most, the fourth leaves with "Later."; the player in the slot counts (`_player_on`).
- [ ] Leftovers: an interrupted eater (greeted, or rain — `_leave` zeroes `bites` today and they carry the half loaf forever) finishes it at their next seat — needs a seated `eat` in `stick3d_poses.gd` (the 2D `eat` already sits; the 3D `eat` arm forces standing legs, which is why `_bite` is not allowed on a bench yet), then the bench, chair and bed spots resume `_bite` on arrival.
- [ ] Crumbs and the last bite: each bite emits six crumb particles (`CPUParticles3D`, `b48a5a`, 0.4s) at the hand for player and resident, the third bite leaves nothing; a pigeon within 4m walks to the crumb spot and pecks (the bird rig item first — until then the blob pigeons just turn toward it).
- [ ] Split `town_player.gd` before the next verb (code hygiene, run 72 — 467 lines; the `C` search-and-match is 230 of them): move `_interact_check` and its match arms into `town_interact.gd` in the chain (base → build → places → systems → player → **interact** → town3d), leaving movement, combat and throwing in `town_player.gd`; behaviour must not change — verify each arm against the original and with the headless run.

## Grows from Garden plot (run 70 — the first thing in the town that grows because someone tended it; deepen it before adding a second field)
- [ ] The can runs dry: the `can` item carries `water = 3` (a `full` meta); each watering spends one, an empty can pours nothing (no drops, the row stays dry, the pose still plays — the plain outcome is still "you watered"), and `C` at the pump while holding the can plays a new `pump` pose (grip the handle high, three downward strokes with the whole shoulder, a short lean back on each upstroke — the board's lever/pump family, `stick3d_poses.gd` + `stickman.ts` in the same run) and refills it. Residents pump before watering when their can is empty.
- [ ] Crops on the counter: `C` at the bakery counter while holding a `pumpkin` hands it over — the baker's line "Pie, then." and, thirty seconds later, the counter's bread stock shows one `pie` item (a flat cylinder, `d98a2a`, eaten in three bites like bread) for whoever comes next; residents who picked a pumpkin at the plot carry it there on their next spot pick (a `deliver` in `_pick_spot`: if `carrying_kind == "pumpkin"`, the counter spot is chosen). The first chain that turns a thing you grew into a thing someone else eats.
- [ ] "Water mine" (quests axis, in `game/`): a resident standing at a dry row without a can (the "carrying something else" branch of the plot routine) sets `want = "water:<row>"` and says "Could you water this one?" to a player within 3m; watering that row while they stand there gets "Obliged." and a +1 to that resident's memory of you (the first per-resident reputation key in `game/`; greeting them afterwards gets "You watered." instead of "Hello."). The web already has this exact quest (`goose.ts` `questFor` water) — same wording, same completion check, ported.
- [ ] Gates as data (grows from polish run 71 — residents never reached the plot because they walked straight into its back fence; fixed with a one-off `_gate_steps` in `crossings`): turn `garden_at` into a `gates: Array` of `{rect: Rect2 (xz), gate: Vector3, out: Vector3}` that `_garden` registers and `crossings` walks for both ends, so the next fenced place (a pen for the goats, the fox den's hedge, a café terrace) declares its rect and gate in one line instead of another special case; while there, give the plot's gate two posts and a hinged half-door that swings open (0.25s) when anyone passes the inner waypoint, like the house doors.
- [ ] Split `stick3d.gd` again before the action arms grow (code hygiene, run 70): `_process` is still ~230 lines because every `action` arm (punch jab/cross/hook, air punch, kick, jet kick, flying kick, throw, flinch, getup, grab) is inline; move them into `stick3d_actions.gd` (`class_name StickActions`, static `apply(f, a, k)`) the same way `StickPoses` took the pose arms — behaviour must not change, verify each arm by reading it against the original and with the headless run.

## Grows from Fox loot (run 69 — the first animal that takes something; deepen it before the next scavenger)
- [ ] Hats fall off: a heavy knock-down (`heavy` in `Resident.hit`, the third hit in `resident_hits_player`) also knocks the worn hat off — `take_off("hat")`, tossed 0.5m the way the blow went, stamped `dropped_at` like the carried item — so the fox's taste for "wearable textiles" from the wish is real: it takes hats too, and the den keeps a small pile (up to three things laid in a row in front of the hole instead of on one spot). Player half is identical: your own cap ends up at the den. Residents pick a fallen hat back up on getting up if it is still there (a `grab` at their feet).
- [ ] The den has a keeper: a second `C` use — crouch at the hole (`peek` pose: knees bent, one hand on the ground, head low and turned; `stick3d.gd` + `stickman.ts`, after the file split below) shows what the fox has stashed *inside* (a line above the head: "Two cups and a paper.") and the fox, if home, growls (`arch` reused for cats — the fox needs its own `growl`: head low, ears flat, tail straight) and comes out to stand between you and the hole for 3s. Nothing is taken from you; it is theatre that tells you where things go.

## Grows from North lane (2026-09-28 vision run — the first street since the pivot; deepen it before adding a third)
- [ ] House numbers on the lane: a small plaque beside each lane door (a box with one to three ink dots), and a resident whose `home_door` is on the lane answers a greeting with "Number two, if you're looking." instead of the generic "Hello." — the first line where a resident refers to their own home; pairs with the postie's round above.
- [ ] Lane light at dusk: the lane's single lamp is the only light on that street — add one more at the far end and make the three lane houses' door awnings glow (small emissive strip) when the owner is home, so the lane reads lived-in at night rather than dark.

## Grows from River and meadow (owner session, 2026-09-28 — the first water in the 3D town; deepen it before a second river)
- [ ] Fishing at the river bank (canonical for the web's "Fishing, later, as its own place" and Animal Crossing bar / "Water": build here): two rod stands on the south bank (`bank` spots become `rod` spots with a rod in a holder, a stool, a bucket); `C` starts `cast` → `angle` (seated, float bobbing) → `bite` (0.6 s window) → `reel` → `land` (a `fish` item held up, then loose, carry/give/bin) or `miss`; residents fish at the same stands with seeded outcomes; fish shadows under the water (dark flat ellipses drifting with the ripples). Poses `cast`/`angle`/`reel` in `stick3d.gd` and `stickman.ts` in the same run.
- [ ] Dive from the bridge: `SPACE` on the bridge deck while facing the water → a `dive` pose (arms forward, arc into the river, splash particles) landing in `swim`; residents on a hot clear afternoon (clock + weather) sometimes walk to the bridge middle and dive too, then swim to the bank and shake off (`shiver`-like `shake-off` pose, 0.8 s) — the river becomes a place, not a boundary.
- [ ] Rowing boat (vehicles axis, thinnest in `game/` — only the handcart line exists): a small wooden boat moored at the north bank under the bridge; `C` boards, ← → rows along the river (`row` pose: lean back, both arms pull), `C` at either bank moors and gets out; the boat drifts with the current when nobody rows; a resident takes it out once a day.
- [ ] Meadow picnic (relationships/economy axis): a picnic blanket prop on the meadow with a basket; `C` with bread or a cup sits on the blanket and eats (`seat`+`eat` on the ground); two residents sometimes meet there at noon and share bread from the bakery counter, which finally gives the bakery a customer who is not the player.
- [ ] Ducks visit the river: the pond ducks fly to the river at dawn and back at dusk (the `Bird3D` flying state over the town), paddle with the current, and a duckling line follows the mother — the first animal routine that spans two places.

## Owner session 2026-09-29 — the widening list (in the owner's order; each is one or more runs; external CC0 assets are now allowed and preferred where they beat our primitives)
- [ ] Dog affection clips (owner picked option b): no Blender on the build machine, so add bone-keyframed `Animation` resources generated by a GDScript tool (`tools/clips.gd`, saves `.tres` next to the model) for the Quaternius Shiba/Fox rig — play-bow (front legs down, rump up), sit, belly-up roll, scratch, bark hop — each verified with the motion sheet (`-- --sheet --only=`). Until then `Animal3D` fakes them with Idle clips + node tilt.
- [ ] Map travel: the world is one 92×48 m strip with streamed districts; add a real second map — a road off the east end with a sign and a fade-through portal that loads `scenes/countryside.tscn` (same town chain, different `_ready`), the car and the player carried across, residents staying home. Also needed for the racing track later.
- [ ] Umbrellas in rain: an `umbrella` item (Kenney/Quaternius CC0 or our primitive), residents fetch one from home when rain starts and walk with it open (`umbr` pose on the 2D side too), the player can pick one from the market stall; rain particles stop under the canopy (fake: no wet-slowdown while holding an open umbrella).
- [ ] Doors and interiors everywhere: houses already open with C and residents open/close them; the bakery and café need a real inside (counter, shelves, a baker behind it) and the door must be the way in; interiors get their own light at night.
- [ ] Builders (residents build): the web "Builders" chain ported to 3D — a staked plot appears, two builder residents (hard hat wearable) carry planks from a pile, the house rises in stages (frame → walls → roof) over a day, then a resident moves in (`home_door` assigned). Player can carry a plank to help.
- [ ] Lumberjack (residents cut trees): an `axe` item, a `chop` pose, the tree wobbles and falls (MegaKit model rotates over, then a stump + logs appear), logs are carryable and feed the builders' pile; a sapling grows back over 3 minutes.
- [ ] Repairers (residents repair buildings): wall cracks from `_crack_wall` are visible damage; a `repairer` job walks to the cracked wall with a bucket, plays `fix` for ~3 s per crack and the ink slivers vanish; the player can do the same with the bucket.
- [ ] Shops without money (owner: no currency yet): a shop is a building with an inside counter and a rack; C at the rack takes any item or wearable for free and equips it (hats, glasses, scarves, backpacks, umbrella, axe, bucket, ball); the shopkeeper resident stands behind the counter and greets; residents shop too and walk out wearing what they took.
- [ ] Multiplayer (owner asked 2026-09-29 — not built yet): `WebSocketMultiplayerPeer` to the existing Cloudflare room (`site/climb-room.js` relays `pos`/`ev`), other players as Stick3D with names, cars and knock-downs sent as events; the game is single-player today.

- [ ] Seesaw (owner 2026-09-29): the park seesaw is a prop only — make it work: C at either end sits you on it (`seat` pose), a second rider (resident or player) on the other end makes it tilt with weight, SPACE pushes off to bounce, a lone rider just sits at the low end; residents pair up on it like they do on the swing.
- [ ] Combat that feels dynamic (owner 2026-09-29: "싸움 자체가 좀 초라한데 맞고 싸우는 것도 역동적으로"): hit-stop (freeze both bodies 60–90 ms on contact, longer on heavy hits), knockback with a real arc (launch up and back, bounce once, slide, then the existing get-up), a hit spark + dust burst at the contact point and a short camera shake scaled by weight, stagger chains (third jab spins the target, the hook launches, the running kick sends them rolling), residents fight back properly — guard up (block pose, reduced knockback), sidestep/dodge on a seeded chance, and their own combo — hits on walls/props bounce the body off, and knocked-down bodies can be hit again once (juggle) before they recover. Built as a reusable `combat3d.gd` component so mini-games (a brawl arena) reuse it.
- [ ] Combat poses (owner 2026-09-29: "발차기 자세나 뭐 그런거"): today's kicks are one straight-leg swing. Add distinct moves, each with windup → strike → hold → recovery timing and hip/torso rotation: front snap kick, roundhouse (pivot on the standing foot, hips turn 90°), side kick (chamber the knee, body leans away), spinning back kick, low sweep (crouch, leg arcs along the ground — trips the target), flying knee; plus uppercut, body blow and a two-hand shove. Take reference timing from the Quaternius Universal Animation Library punch clips (`Punch_Jab`, `Punch_Cross`) where the joints map. Residents use the same set when they fight back.
## Grows from River and bridge (run 68 — the town's first water you cannot walk through; deepen it: things fall in, people lean on the parapet)
- [ ] Things fall in the river: an item thrown or dropped whose landing point is inside z RIVER_N..RIVER_S (`_fly`) does not rest on the bottom — it floats at y 0.03 and drifts east with the glints (same speed), bobbing; the player can wade nothing, so the one way to get it back is to be on the bridge when it passes under and `C` over the parapet (a `reach` down pose, both sides). A resident who sees their thrown thing go says "Gone east." and a bank resident fishes one out once a day. Past x = WORLD_X it is gone for good.
- [ ] Leaning on the parapet: a `parapet` spot on each side of the bridge deck (x ±0.8, facing east/west) where residents stop mid-crossing and lean on the stone with both forearms (`sill` pose — lean, elbows down, one foot crossed, a slow look downstream every ~3s; `stick3d.gd` + `stickman.ts`); the player does the same with `C` on the deck. Two leaners on one side trade a line ("Water."/"Still going.").

## Grows from Beds and seats (2026-09-28 polish run 67 — beds and shelves became reachable and the player's seat counts as taken; deepen before widening)
- [ ] Standing room only: a resident who walks up to a bench and finds every slot taken (by residents or the player, now that `_free_slot` sees the player) does not silently pick another spot — they stop 0.6m in front of it, say "Taken." once, and stand for 3–5s in a new `wait` pose (weight on one leg, arms folded, a glance at the seat every ~1.5s — its own timing, never a still frame; `stick3d.gd` + `stickman.ts` in the same run) before choosing again. Player half: the same `wait` is what the player plays when `C` on a full bench fails (today it does nothing), so the refusal is visible to both.
- [ ] Lights out: a resident who lies down in a bed at night turns that house's interior `_furniture("lamp")` off (`light.visible = false`) and the first one up in the morning turns it on — the lamp toggle already exists for the player (`C` on the lamp); residents get the same verb on the same object, and a house reads dark-because-asleep from outside once the windows glow (3D town / "Time of day").

## Grows from Lookout terrace (run 73 — the town's first second level; deepen it before adding a second cliff)
- [ ] A worn path to the steps: today the steps foot sits in bare grass and the way up does not read from the meadow path — add a `_path` from the meadow's east path end (x≈2, z 16.5) to the steps foot (14.5, 23.9), bending once at the flower cluster (x 3.5, z 21.5) so it is not one straight line, and a `dirt` patch (flat disc, `6b4a35`) at the foot where feet gather; a resident whose routine picks the terrace walks that path (`crossings()` adds its bend as a waypoint when the origin is west of x 8).
- [ ] Legs over the edge: the terrace's east lip (x 21, z 17..20) becomes a `ledge` spot with three slots like a bench — `C` there sits facing east with the legs dangling over the rock face (`dangle` pose: seated, both shins swinging out of phase at 0.8Hz, hands flat on the grass beside the hips, a lean forward to look down every ~5s; `stick3d_poses.gd` + `stickman.ts`), residents pick it like a bench (the same 6–14s), and two on the ledge trade "Long way down." / "Not really." — the first seat that is not furniture.
- [ ] A telescope on the rail: a brass tube on a post at the rail's centre (`scope` spot next to the lookout) — `C` swaps the camera for 4s to a far view of the north lane from the terrace (a second `Camera3D` on the tube, `current` toggled, the same 4s on both views) with the `scope` pose (one eye to the eyepiece, one hand on the tube, the other on the hip, a step-in windup and a step-back recovery); residents at the lookout use it one loop in three and say what they saw ("Someone is on the roof." if a resident is on the stair-house roof, "The bakery is open." if the counter has stock — the first lines that report the town's real state).
- [ ] Seats above ground as one rule (code hygiene, polish run 75 — standing up from the terrace bench dropped the player to y = 0.02 inside the rock and physics spat them out under the map; the furniture put-down had the same y = 0): every place that assumes the floor is at 0 — the player's stand-up tween, the furniture put-down, `_pick_furniture`'s socket return, the hatstand/counter/oven item spawns at `body + (0, 0.9, 0)`, and the residents' bench/chair step-off offsets — goes through one `floor_at(p) -> float` in `town_places.gd` (today's `ground_y` renamed and extended to the stair-house roof and the bridge deck, which `ground_y` still reports as 0), so the `ledge` seat above, a bench on the roof, or a second cliff cannot repeat the fall-through; verify with the same headless sit-then-stand probe on each raised seat.

## Grows from Under the eave (run 74 — the first time a person's body answers the weather; deepen it before slice 2's umbrella)
- [ ] Sheltering together: two residents under the same eave (both `storm` at `door` spots within 2m) offset their look-up cycle by `uid` (`pose_t` starts at `uid % 3`) so they never nod in unison, and once per 12s one turns to the other (`face`) for a two-line exchange ("Heavier than it looks." → "It will pass."), then back to the street; the player standing in `storm` beside one gets the first line said to them.
- [ ] The eave drips: a thin `CPUParticles3D` line along each house's front roof edge, emitting only while `weather == "rain"` — six fat drops per second falling 0.3m in front of the door, so a storm-stander reads as *just* inside the dry line; the drop line is where slice 3's wet rule draws its shelter boundary (inside = dry).
- [ ] The dog shelters too: in rain the dog (`quad3d`) trots to the nearest eave with someone standing under it, sits (`sit`) 0.6m beside them facing the street, and shakes off once on arrival (new quad act `shake`: a 0.6s whole-body wobble from the head back along the spine, ears flapping, six drops flung sideways) — the first animal that answers the weather beside a person, not just by hiding; a resident sat-beside says "Wet dog."

## Grows from Umbrella rack (run 76 — the first thing a person carries against the weather, and the first resident who walks in the rain instead of fleeing it; deepen it before slice 3's wet rule)
- [ ] Drips off the rim: a `CPUParticles3D` ring on the canopy (`umb` node, 12 fat drops/s falling 0.6m from the rim) emitting only while `weather == "rain"` and `umbr_k > 0.9`; a resident walking within 0.5m beside someone's open umbrella says "Room for one?" once per pair per rain — the first line said *to* an umbrella.
- [ ] Lost umbrellas come home: a thrown or dropped umbrella (or one the fox carried to the den) leaves the rack short until someone returns it; at 06:00 the café's resident (the one whose `home_door` is the café door, `job = "cafe"` stamped in `_market` like the baker's) walks to every loose `umbrella` item in `items`, `grab`s it and carries it back to the rack one at a time — visible, not a reset; if all three are in hands or racks nothing happens.

## Grows from Chimney smoke (run 77 — the first way to read from the street that someone is home; deepen it before the windows glow)
- [ ] A hearth inside: the smoke comes from nothing today — put a stone hearth on the back wall of each gabled house (`_interior`: a box frame, a dark opening, a log pair), a small `OmniLight3D` in orange (`e0a060`, range 2.5) that is on exactly when `_someone_home`, three ember `CPUParticles3D` sparks per second inside the opening, and a `warm` pose for anyone seated in that house's chair while it burns (hands held out to the fire, palms forward, a slow rub every 3s — its own 0.4s reach and 0.4s withdraw); residents at a home chair play it instead of the plain sit when it rains or at night; the player seated there gets it too and a nearby resident says "Warm enough."
- [ ] Smoke counts guests: the chimney puffs harder with more people home — `amount` 12 for one claimed slot, 20 for two, 28 for three or more (and the player counts as one), so a party reads from the street; a resident walking past a hard-smoking house says "Full house." once per pass — the first line said about a chimney.

## Grows from Jetty and punt (run 78 — the first thing that moves people over the water; deepen it before a second jetty)
- [ ] The wake and the ducks: a `CPUParticles3D` trail of six flat white streaks per second behind the moving punt (only while `|v| > 0.3`), the river glints within 1.5m of the hull scatter sideways (`_flow` gets the punt's x), and a duck that the punt passes within 1.2m flaps up and lands 3m off (the pigeon's `fly` arc on the duck) — a resident on a `bank` spot who sees it says "Mind the ducks." once per pass; the punt's rower gets the same line from a bank resident whether player or resident.
- [ ] Rowing with a passenger's weight: a second thwart at the stern (`SEAT_OFF` + 0.7 in x) lets one more person sit (`seat`-pose, facing the rower) — a resident who reaches the jetty while the player sits docked in the punt boards it as the passenger instead of saying "Taken." (and the reverse: the player `C`s a docked punt a resident is rowing to ride along), the punt then rows at ×0.8 and sits 3cm deeper (`n.position.y` − 0.03), and the passenger says "Steady." on the first stroke — the first ride two people share.
- [ ] A ferry bell at each landing (run 94, deeper — today the punt waits on whichever bank someone left it, so the far side can only walk): a small bell under a two-post gallows at each jetty (a cylinder bell on a crossbar, a dangling rope); `C` at it plays a new `ring` pose (0.2s reach up to the rope → two pulls 0.3s each with a knee dip → 0.2s let go) and the bell swings (`rotation.z` damped, ink clapper), and an *empty, untied-or-tied* punt across slips its rope and drifts to the ringing landing (`home` = that x) at the empty-boat speed; residents waiting at a landing ring it instead of re-planning one time in two. Ringing while someone rows does nothing but the sound — nobody is pulled out of a boat.

## Grows from Resident minds (owner session 2026-09-30 — the first residents with traits, needs, mood and a memory of the player; deepen it before adding the next 116 voices)
- [ ] Mood in the gait (motions axis): `mind.mood` and `mind.energy` reach the body — `Stick3D` gets `mood := 0.0` and `weary := 0.0` that `resident_life.gd` writes each frame; below −0.3 the walk drops its bob to 60%, the torso lean gains 0.08 forward and the arms swing 30% less (a slump), above 0.45 the stride phase runs 8% quicker with a 0.02 extra bob (brisk); `energy < 0.3` adds a 0.5Hz head droop of 0.12 rad while standing — no new pose, a modulation of the walk and stand that already exist, so every pose still reads. The player gets the same two knobs from their own `mind`-less state: `weary` rises after 60s of dash (`dash_until` accumulates) and drops while seated. A resident greeting a slumped one says "Long day?" — the first line said about how someone walks.
- [ ] The driver clocks off (jobs axis): the taxi and delivery drivers drive forever today — at 19:00 (`_hour`) the AI car finishes its lap, parks at the east lot's free slot (route ends there, `v` to 0), the driver leaves the car (`drive` → `routine` through a new `undrive()` that restores collision at `exit_pos()` and clears `car_seat`/`c.driver`), walks home to bed like everyone, and at 07:00 the first pick of the day is the car again (`spot = {kind: "car", car: c}` → `drive(c)` on arrival) — the player can then take the taxi at night (its `driver` is null) and a resident at the lot at 07:00 finding it gone says "Someone has my cab."

## Grows from Cobbler's bench (run 80 — the first trade: a craft on request, and residents who come because something of theirs actually wore out; deepen it before the next trade)
- [ ] Worn soles you can see (chain, run 80): past `SOLE_M` a figure's heel scuffs every sixth step — a small grey dust puff (`CPUParticles3D`, one-shot, 6 particles) at the back foot and a 0.1s toe drag in the walk (a new `scuff` gait blend in `stick3d.gd`, not a pose: the trailing knee stays 0.15 straighter for that step) — on the player and on residents by the same `walked` counter, so the reason a resident heads to the stool is visible before they go. Resoling clears it.
- [ ] The cobbler shuts the bench (chain, run 80): at 17:00 (or rain) the cobbler on the bench plays a new `tidy` pose — 0.3s reach, the three shoes lined up one by one (each shoe mesh tweened 0.1m into a row, 0.4s apiece), the hammer lifted onto the sign post hook (the laid-down hammer box reparented to the post), 0.3s straighten — and the stool gets turned upside down onto the table (a 0.5s tween); at 09:00 the cobbler reverses it. The player's `C` on a shut bench does the same `tidy` both ways. Nothing is locked: a shut bench still takes a `hammer` loop.

## Grows from Cutler's wheel (run 81 — the second trade, and the first one whose customers come because of work they did, not distance they walked; deepen it before the tailor)
- [ ] Sparks that catch the eye (chain, run 81): a resident in `routine` or `walk` passing within 4m of the wheel while `spark.emitting` turns their head toward it for the pass (a `neck.rotation.y` look-at like the dog-petting face, no new pose), and one in five stops for 2s at the customer spot's outer edge in `wait` to watch before walking on — the plain outcome (walk past) stays 80%; the player gets the same head-turn walking past while a resident grinds, so the wheel reads as a thing people notice.
- [ ] The trough runs dry (chain, run 81): every fourth grinding session lowers the trough's water box (the `8fb8cc` top) by 0.03 until at four it is empty — a dry stone throws twice the sparks and `spin` ramps no higher than 5; the cutler then fetches the plot's `can` (the garden's own item, `carry` walk to the pump and back) and pours it in with the existing `water` pose (drops on), refilling it in one `WATER_T`; the player can do the same with `C` at the trough holding the can. A dry trough never blocks `C`.
- [ ] Standing room only, now that `wait` exists (chain, run 81 — the Beds-and-seats item below asked for this pose and it landed here first): a resident who reaches a bench with every slot taken stops 0.6m in front of it, says "Taken." once and stands 3–5s in `wait` before choosing again; the player's failed `C` on a full bench plays the same `wait` for `WAIT_T` × 2 — build that line's behaviour, not a second pose.

## Grows from Tailor's stitchhouse (run 82 — the first trade whose customers come because someone knocked them down; the fight has a cost you can see and a place that fixes it)
- [ ] The mend shows (chain, run 82): `Wear.mend` no longer removes everything — the paper patch stays with a mauve cross-stitch on it (two 0.06m crossed boxes), so a mended backpack reads differently from a new one; a resident wearing a mended thing, greeted by the player, answers one time in four with "Mended. The tailor on the east plaza." instead of their usual line (word of mouth pointing at the place). A second tear on a mended thing replaces the cross-stitch with a fresh rip.
- [ ] Pinning the dress form (motions, chain, run 82): between customers the tailor stands every third minute and plays a new `pin` pose at the dress form (0.3s reach to the lips, a 0.4s press of the pin into the swatch with the thumb, a 0.3s step back to look at it with the head tilted; `PIN_T` 1.0, three pins per visit), the swatch mesh shifting 2cm each pin; the player can do the same with `C` at the dress form when the table is free (the same pose, same shift) — the tailor says "Not there." one time in three.
- [ ] A patch in the pocket (chain, polish run 83 — a car now tears the player's back item too, so torn things turn up after 17:00 when the table is empty): `C` at the fitting stool while the tailor is sewing and nothing of yours is torn buys nothing but gets a `patch` item (a paper-coloured square, "Take one for later."; one per visit, three at most in the world); holding a patch, `C` facing a torn resident (they stop, turn their back — `fig.face` away — and hold still for one `SEW_T`) plays `sew` standing (the seated legs swapped for a stand: add a `standing` flag to the pose, not a second pose) and mends them with a plain patch (the mauve cross-stitch is the tailor's alone — see "The mend shows"); residents with a `friend` tie (`resident_mind.gd`) do the same for a torn friend they pass after 17:00 if they hold a patch. The torn resident thanks the player by `mind` memory (+liking, the same call a gift makes).

## Grows from Stepping stones (run 84 — the second way over the river and the first one you balance on; deepen it before a third crossing)
- [ ] Feet in the water: the last stone on the south side becomes a one-slot seat — `C` there sits low facing downstream with both shins in the water (`dabble` pose: hands flat behind on the stone, shins swinging out of phase at 0.6Hz, a slow kick every ~2s that throws a ring with `water._ring`, a 0.3s lower-in windup and a 0.4s draw-up recovery); residents who reach the south `bank` spot on a hot clear afternoon take it instead one time in three, and someone crossing the stones while it is occupied has to step round — they stop on the stone before it and say "Excuse me." once, and the sitter draws their feet up (the same recovery, then back in).

## Grows from Climb direction (run 88 — the first mini-game now climbs the way the arrow points; deepen the climb before a second tower)
- [ ] Night climbs (thin axis: Climb, run 92 — the town has a day clock and the tower ignores it): `climb.gd` takes the town's `clock` when entered; if it is night the tower is dark blue, every 6th platform carries a small lantern (`OmniLight3D`, range 3, warm), the rest are lit only by the climber's own glow (a short-range light on the player), and the sign at the town tower keeps a separate `records["climb_night"]` best shown as a second line ("night 42 m"). Residents watching at the door at night hold a lamp (reuse the umbrella hand slot with a small lantern item).
- [ ] Gust bands on the game Climb (thin axis: Climb): above platform 24, every 12th band has a wind flag on the core; every ~6s a 1.2s gust pushes the climber outward at 1.6 m/s (`climb.gd`, a radial push added to `hv`), shown by the flag snapping straight and three thin streak lines; holding no arrow during the gust plays a new `brace` pose (knees bent, body leaning into the wind, arms in, a 0.2s set and a 0.3s release) and halves the push. `probe_climb.gd` still lands → jumps on bands 30 and 36 outside a gust.
- [ ] Someone waits at the tower door (thin axis: relationships): the resident with the highest `mind` affinity for the player walks to the Climb gate's `lookout` spot while the player is inside; on `finished` they clap (a `clap` pose: hands meet at chest height 3Hz for 1.2s, a small hop on the last) and say `mind.line("cheer")` (new category in `minds.json` `default`), and `mind.befriend()` runs once per day; nobody waits for a player they dislike.
- [ ] The best climb shows on the town tower: the decorative tower's flag sits at the height of the player's best (`records["climb"]` scaled to the 14m tower, eased over 2s on return), and the record sign beside the gate gets the height in chalk; a resident at the `lookout` spot looks up at the flag (`HeadLook`) when it moves.
- [ ] The storysitter's tower tale (thin axis: Climb, builds on run 103): on the first story hour after the player's best climb improves (`records["climb"]`), the storysitter reads "the tower" — the line comes from a new `tower_tale` category that names the height in metres (`"Someone went up %d metres. The wind was against them."` formatted from the record), and each child who heard it, on leaving the Sunroom, walks to the Climb gate and stands on the `lookout` spot for 8–12s with the `shade` pose before rejoining their parent; the player inside the Sunroom hears the same line.
- [ ] A rest at the checkpoints (thin axis: Climb, polish run 93): each `checks` ledge in `climb.gd` gets a seated resident Stick3D (`fig.base_scale` 1.0, one of the town's roster handles, sitting with a thermos — a capped `cup` item in the right hand) who turns their head toward the climber (`head_look`); landing on that ledge plays a new `pant` pose (0.25s bend → hands on knees, chest heaving at 1.2Hz with the neck bobbing, 0.3s straighten — interruptible by any move) and the resident says `mind.line("climb_rest")` once per ledge; a fall that respawns you there gets `mind.line("climb_again")` instead. Both categories go in `minds.json` `default`.
- [ ] A thermos at the top (thin axis: Climb, polish run 105 — reaching platform 48 ends with a HUD line and nothing in the hand): a capped `cup` item stands on the last platform of `games/climb.gd`; walking into it plays the existing `drink` pose with the climber facing out over the tower edge (0.3s lift, 1.0s sip with the head tipped back, 0.3s lower), and the cup comes back to the town in the hand when `_leave` returns (`carrying_kind` "cup", so the café's bin and rinse path takes it); the run's time is written on the cup's meta (`climb_t`) and a resident handed it (`give_to_resident`) reads it out — `mind.line("climb_cup")` naming the seconds — and +0.05 fond. Residents never enter Climb, so the resident half is the reader: the café owner says the same line when the player bins it at the café.

## Grows from Race (run 89 — the second mini-game and the first with residents in it; deepen it before a third game)
- [ ] Rivals remember the race (relationships): `race.gd` returns each rival's finishing order in `result["rivals"]`; back in town, a rival you beat with temper > 0.6 says `mind.line("sore")` (new `minds.json` category) the next time you meet and `fond` −0.05, a rival who beat you says `mind.line("gloat")`, a calm one `mind.befriend`s nobody but gets `fun` +0.3; the one who won walks to the garage's gate spot that evening and stands there with a new `trophy` pose (cup raised in both hands, a 0.3s lift, 1.0s hold with a small bob, 0.4s lower).
- [ ] A mechanic at the lakeside garage (jobs): a resident with job "mechanic" whose workplace is the garage — after any town crash (`Car3D._shaken`, `_debris`) the mechanic walks to the car, kneels at the wheel with a new `wrench` pose (crouched, right forearm cranking a 0.5s quarter-turn three times, left hand braced on the body) for 4s and the car's `_crash_at` scars (a dent decal) clear; the player can do the same with `C` at a crashed car's wheel; a task "fix the van's wheel" in the quest list.
- [ ] Wet track (weather): when the town's weather is "rain" on entry, `race.gd` lowers every car's `grip` by 30%, lays thin puddle discs on two corners that throw a spray (`CPUParticles3D`) and kill boost on contact, and the rivals' `cruise` drops 1.5; the HUD says "Wet." once.
- [ ] A pit lane and a pit stop (mini-games axis, run 96 — Race has four open lines and nothing to do on the track except drive): `race.gd` gets a short gravel lane cut inside the start straight (6 road boxes, white edge lines, a PIT board on a post); driving through it at under 6 m/s for 1.5s refills the boost bar and plays a resident pit hand (one Stick3D at the lane, a new `pit` pose: 0.2 crouch → 0.8 both hands at the wheel arch with a short jerk → 0.2 stand and a thumbs-up), costing about 3s against a lap; rivals take it once each on lap 2. The HUD stays a key legend.
- [ ] Ghost of your best lap (Climb/games): `race.gd` samples the player car's position and yaw every 0.1s; the best lap's samples are saved under `records["race_ghost"]` and replayed as a translucent car (alpha 0.35, no collision) on later visits, so the record on the sign is something you can see and chase.

## Grows from Woodcutting (run 90 — the first job in the open world, and the first thing a person makes that another place uses up; deepen it before a second open-world trade)
- [ ] Felling (places/jobs): two marked trees at the forest edge north of the cabin (a ring of mauve paint on the trunk); when the stump has no rounds left (a `rounds` count on the round-log pile, 6, −1 per split pair) the woodcutter walks to a marked tree with the axe and fells it — a `fell` pose (side swing at waist height, 0.35s back → 0.15s cut → 0.3s recover, ×6), the crown tips over on a 1.2s tween with `shake`, the trunk becomes three rounds rolled to the pile (+3) and a fresh stump stays where the tree stood; the player can fell a marked tree with `C` at it the same way. A sapling appears at the stump after a day.
- [ ] A lean-to over the stack (construction, a visible build chain): after the first wet-wood day the woodcutter builds a roof over the split stack in three visible stages over three mornings (four posts → two beams → plank roof), each stage a 20s `hammer` session at the stack; the player can do a stage with `C` at the stack while the woodcutter is away.

## Grows from Lakeside fishing (run 91 — the first thing in the open world that you wait for; deepen it before a second fishing spot)
- [ ] Roast it at the cabin fire (meals, builds on fishing + woodcutting — the two sites are 285m apart and nothing carries between them yet): carrying a raw `fish`, `C` at the cabin fire's `bank` spot = a new `roast` pose (0.3s crouch and hold a green stick over the flames, 4s slow turning of the wrist with a sway, 0.3s stand); the fish goes brown (`cooked` meta, darker material) and is eaten in two bites instead of three, a resident by the fire says `mind.line("roast")`; the woodcutter roasts any fish he is handed (`take_gift`) the same way before eating it.
- [ ] Fish bite in the rain (weather axis — rain only sends people under eaves): while it rains the bite wait at the jetty shortens to 2–6s and rain rings dot the lake around the float (`water._ring` at random points inside the disc, 3 a second); a resident with an open umbrella (run 76) at the jetty end fishes anyway — the rod in the left hand, the umbrella in the right (`cast` with sides swapped via meta `rod_side`).
