- [ ] A heron in the shallows (animals axis, on the bird rig `bird3d.gd`, Scavengers rules): a tall grey bird (long neck = three segments, dagger beak, stilt legs) stands in the lake's west shallows, stalks (`stalk`: neck coiled, one slow step every 2s), strikes at the water (0.15s neck shot, beak dips, a ring) and swallows; a fish left on the jetty planks for 10s it walks over and takes to the reeds (a stash spot you can walk to) — never one in a hand.

## Grows from Cabin stove (run 92 — the first thing in the town that is used up overnight and has to be fed again by hand; deepen it before a second hearth)
- [ ] Ash in the morning (chain: woodcutting → stove → ash → garden): each night the stove burns its logs it leaves an `ash` count (+1 per log); at 07:00 the woodcutter crouches at the stove with the same `stoke` crouch, scoops into a small tin `ash` bucket (a new item, verbs: carry, tip), and walks it to the garden plot where a `tip` pose (0.3s lift to the hip → 0.4s tilt with a grey puff `CPUParticles3D` → 0.3s back) gives that row one growth stage; the player can do the same with `C` at the stove empty-handed when ash > 0; ash left over three nights shows as a grey heap under the stove door and the stove only takes two logs until it is cleared.
- [ ] Warm hands on a cold night (weather/time): after 22:00 or in rain, a resident passing within 15m of a lit cabin stove or the cabin's campfire goes to it and plays a new `warm` pose (0.3s step in and crouch slightly → hands held out palms-forward at chest height, rubbing together at 2Hz with breath puffs every 3s → 0.3s straighten); the player does the same with `C` at the fire `bank` spot or the stove while it is lit; a resident already warming says `mind.line("warm")` to the next arrival (befriend once per night).

## Grows from Book box (run 98 — the first shelf where things are left for strangers; deepen it before the seed tin copies it)
- [ ] A book left on the bench (interaction/relationships, run 98 — builds on the book box): a resident who took a book and later sits on a bench reads it seated (`read` with seated legs, as `eat` already does on a bench) for the bench stay; one time in four they get up without it and the book stays on the bench slot as an item (`items`, spine up); the next resident who sits there picks it up and reads (`mind.line("found_book")`), the player can carry it back to the box (`shelve`) and the keeper says `mind.line("book_back")` once; plain outcome (book goes with the reader) 75%.
- [ ] The cat on the book box (animals axis, thinnest at one open line — the market cat has a home spot and nothing to do): between 12:00 and 15:00 in `clear` weather the market cat (Quad3D) walks to the bakery's book box, hops onto its roof board (0.97m, a two-beat crouch-and-spring on the rig: back legs fold 0.25s, launch, front paws land first) and lies in the sun (`lie`, tail tip flicking); a person who shelves under it makes it lift its head and watch the hand (look-at), one time in four it bats a spine with a front paw and that book drops to the ground as an item (the plain shelve still happens three times in four); the librarian shoos it at 16:00 (`wave` toward it, cat hops down and walks home).
- [ ] The reading room on the north lane (places axis, structural — a new building with a door and an interior, thinnest at two open lines): a narrow two-storey house at the lane's west end (x −14..−10, z −17..−14, behind the existing lane path, off the road band) built with `_house` and a distinct interior: two tall shelf spots (`shelf` with a `door`, so the existing read routine reaches them), a long table with two chairs, a lamp that the librarian turns on at dusk; the book box's overflow goes there — when the box is full (stock 6) the librarian carries one book to the room (`shelve` at the tall shelf) so the box always has room to leave one; a task "return a book to the reading room" for the player once the box is full.

## Grows from Letter room (run 99 — the first building with an interior made for one job, and the first paper that goes somewhere; deepen it before the noticeboard copies it)
- [ ] The scribe writes at dusk (time axis, builds on the letter room): from 18:00 to 21:00 the scribe lights the desk lamp (the room's `_furniture("lamp")` light on — its window glows from the lane) and sits at the desk writing (new `write` pose, seated: the right hand makes small loops, 0.25s per stroke, a 0.4s dip to the inkpot every fourth stroke, the head following the line) — each 20s of writing turns one `paper` from a desk stack (three, refilled at dawn) into a `letter` filed in the pigeonholes at 21:00; a player who sits at the desk chair holding a `paper` writes the same way and stands up with a `letter`.
- [ ] A post bicycle (vehicles axis — the town has cars and a punt and nothing in between): a bicycle leans on the letter room's mailbox (frame from thin capsules, two torus wheels, a front basket); `C` mounts it (new `cycle` pose: 0.3s swing the leg over → pedalling with the feet on a 0.18m crank circle locked to wheel rotation, knees rising alternately, hands on the bars → 0.3s step off); it rides at 4.5 m/s on paths and grass (a hit stops it dead with a wobble, no crash damage) and stays where it was left; the scribe rides it on the morning round when it is at the mailbox; a resident who finds it elsewhere rides it back one time in three.
- [ ] Rain on the letters (weather axis): while it rains, a letter lying outdoors goes darker over 10s (material lerped toward `cfc7c2`) and is `wet`; a wet letter cannot be posted (the scribe says `mind.line("letter_wet")` and lays it on the desk to dry for 60s, then files it); residents carrying a letter in rain tuck it under the arm (`storm`'s fold on one side only) instead of reading it.

## Grows from Story Sunroom (run 103 — the first house where people go to be read to, and run 104 — the first place a hurt body goes to feel better; deepen it before the nap corner)
- [ ] Books with titles (items axis — thinnest at ten open lines; builds on runs 98 and 106): `make_item("book")` draws a `title` meta from twelve deadpan titles in a new `game/data/books.json` ("Drains of the North Lane", "A Short History of the Long Bench", …) and paints the first word on the spine (a `Label3D` child, `pixel_size` 0.003, ink on the paper band); the book box and the Sunroom shelf keep a `titles` array beside `stock` so `unshelve_book`/`shelf_take` hand back the book that was actually there, in order; the storysitter's `story_open` names the title of the book in hand ("Chapter four of *%s*. Again." — a `%s` slot in two of the four lines); a `read` pose by anyone shows the title above the head for 1.5s. No new verbs — the same book, now remembered by name.
- [ ] A tab at the bakery (economy axis — twelve open lines, the second-thinnest; builds on runs 72 and 85, and "Coins in play"): a brass coin jar on the bakery counter (`_box` jar + three coin discs that show the stock the way `_show_stock` does); a resident taking bread drops a `coin` (new item: brass disc r 0.03, carried in the pocket, three at most, found as loose change under benches once a day) into the jar with the `pass` pose's hand-over beat; the player with no coin still gets the bread (the plain take is never blocked) but the baker says a new `mind.line("tab")` and the player's `mind` memory on the baker gets `tab += 1`; one knead at the oven (`oven_use`) clears the whole tab and earns `mind.line("square")`. Arithmetic: coin 1.0 when carried, tab otherwise — no refusal branch.
- [ ] The regular (relationships chain, builds on run 106): the storysitter's mind counts books brought (`books_from`: handle or "you" → n, saved with the memory); after the third, the player's `C` at the empty armchair outside story time makes the storysitter — if home — fetch the house book from the bed slot and hand it over to read (the `lend_book` path in reverse: `shelf_take` with a `house` meta so it comes back to the Sunroom, not the box), greeting with a new `story_regular` line; a resident who has brought three gets the same when they sit on a cushion outside story time (one in six picks, `mind.score` term "lonely"). The lent book's reverse trip is the same 10:00 walk.
- [ ] polish note for run 109 (found by run 106, pre-existing on the clean tree): `probe_calm`'s knocked-down adult sits on the cushion past the 6–10s `busy_until` (`sat_for −1`, still `busy`/`cushion` at the end of 1500 frames) while the player stands at the door — find what re-arms or skips `_leave` for a crosslegged adult (candidates: `_pair_pick` mates, `_notice`, the `cross_up` meta vs `busy_until`) and make the probe assert the exit; then confirm the player gets a free cushion afterwards as run 104 recorded.
- [ ] Bread for the reader (relationships-and-economy chain, builds on runs 72, 85 and 104): a resident who was settled on a cushion yesterday (a `calmed_day` on the mind, saved with the memory) goes to the bakery counter at 09:00 the next morning, takes a bread (the run 72 counter path, so the stock really drops), walks it to the Sunroom door and hands it over with the run 86 `pass` pose; the storysitter answers `mind.line("thanks_bread")` (new category), eats it on the doorstep with the existing seated eating chain and `mind.befriend`s them 0.2 — a tie you can see form from the lane; the player `C` holding a bread at the storysitter gives the same way and gets the same line, and a storysitter who already ate says `mind.line("full")` and keeps it for later on the two-book shelf (slice "Bring a book" adds that shelf; until then on the armchair arm).
- [ ] A cup for the rattled (relationships chain, builds on run 104): the storysitter keeps a teapot on a sill shelf by the armchair (`_box` pot + two cups as real `cup` items, restocked at 14:45); when a shaken resident sits on a cushion (the run 104 `shaken` path) the storysitter rises once with the existing `getup` transition, walks 1.5m, hands them a cup (`give_to_resident`, the cup counts as warm: `mind.ate()` with half the fullness of bread) and sits back down — one per story hour, so the reading is not a café. Player half: the same cup is offered when `cushion_use` sees `player_up_at` fresh; `C` takes it and `C` again plays `drink`. Empty cups go back on the sill shelf (`C` near it, residents on `_leave`), never into the street.

## Grows from Nap corner (run 108 — the first room the town watched being built, stake to wall; deepen it before the builders' tenth house copies the chain)
- [ ] Blankets on the cots (items axis — fourteen open lines, run 108): a folded `blanket` item at each cot's foot (a 0.4×0.05×0.2 box, two colours — `dfe6ea`, and `ad7096` on one only) that a child pulls up when they lie down (the box slides from the foot to the chest over 0.6 s and flattens to 0.02 — the `rest` pose with a `blanket` meta), and the storysitter folds back at 14:00 with slice 3's `fold` pose (built here first if slice 3 is still open: 0.3 pick a corner, 0.5 crease with the heel of the hand, 0.3 turn, twice); the player can take a blanket (`C` on the cot's foot), carry it to a bench and `C` wraps it round the shoulders (`wrap` pose: 0.3 both hands bring it over the head, 0.4 settle, then the hands hold it at the chest; walking keeps it) — the first wearable that is not on a socket, and the `shiver`-stopper for when "Weather people feel" brings cold rain; a resident caught in rain without an umbrella who passes the open lean-to takes one the same way and brings it back dry at 09:00.
- [ ] The rocker outside nap hours (relationships axis, run 108): the storysitter rocks in the empty lean-to 17:30–18:00 with a `hum` every 7 s and the door open; a resident whose `mind.energy` < 0.25 who passes within 8 m goes in and sits on the cot's edge (new `perch` pose: seated with the feet on the floor and the hands between the knees, 0.3 sit, hold with a slow 3 s sway matching the chair, 0.3 stand) for 20 s, `energy` +0.1 and `mind.befriend(storysitter, 0.1)`; the player `C` at a cot in that half hour perches the same way instead of lying down and hears one `story` line. Plain outcome: nobody is sent; the room is simply open.
- [ ] Rain on the lean-to (weather axis, polish run 109 — the nap corner is the town's only sloped roof that ends at head height, and rain falls through it as if it were not there): while `weather == "rain"` and `nap.stage == 3`, a row of five short drop meshes (0.02×0.08 boxes, `9fb4c4`) falls from the outer eave edge every 0.4 s and a wet strip (2.6×0.3 flat box `7a8a6e`, fading over 60 s after the rain stops) darkens the ground under it; a resident passing within 1 m of the eave line in rain steps around it (a 0.6 m sidestep on the `_route`, no new state), and the player standing under it gets the existing `shake` on leaving. Inside, a lying child sleeps through the player's door 50% of the time in rain (one variation on the door wake — the plain wake stays at 50%; clear weather unchanged at 100%) and the storysitter's hum switches to a new `hum_rain` category (three lines). Task: "Sit out a shower under the lean-to."
- [ ] The handcart brings the planks (vehicles axis, polish run 109; needs the canonical "A handcart" line under "Jobs and vehicles" first): on each nap-corner stage morning before 08:00 a resident (the builder by name once that line ships, else the nearest idle adult) pushes the handcart from the north lane to the pile with two planks in the bed (`push` pose, 1.1 m/s), tips them onto the pile with the existing `shelve` beat, and pushes it back; the stage then uses a plank from a pile that visibly grew first, so the plank count is a real stock, not a countdown. The player can push the cart there instead (same `push`, a `C` at the pile unloads); the resident's errand is skipped that morning and they say a new `cart_done` line. Task: "Bring the planks to the nap corner."

## Grows from Coins (run 107 — the town's first money, and the first thing that goes to the pocket instead of the hand; deepen it before a second currency or a bank)
- [ ] Loose change under the benches (economy + items, builds on run 107 — today a coin only exists after a knock-down): at 07:00 one `coin` spawns half under a random bench seat (`_bench` positions −0.45/0/0.45, the coin at z −0.25 behind the plank so only its rim shows); the player stoops it from the front; a resident who sat on that slot finds it when they stand (`_leave` checks under the seat and stoops it with `finders`); never more than three loose coins in the hub at once (count `items` of kind coin before spawning). The first coin source that is not violence.
- [ ] The fox takes coins (grows from Fox loot, runs 69 + 107): `_fresh_drop` already hunts dropped things; a coin lying still 20s within 10m of the den is taken too (`_fox_grab`, the fox carries it in its mouth), the den's hoard shows coins as discs beside the loot, and "Fetch it back from the den" under Quests covers the recovery — the fox's coins are the player's to stoop back, the resident who lost one says `mind.line("fox_coin")` (new category).
- [ ] Children find coins first (children axis, builds on runs 102 + 107 — the tick skips children today): a child within 1.5m of a coin (not in the road band) breaks its follow, skips to it and stoops at child height (`stoop` under `base_scale` 0.62, the same beats), then runs it to its parent and hands it over with the run 86 `pass` beat (`give_to_resident` path → the parent's pocket), saying `mind.line("kid_found")`; the player can hold out a hand (`C` at the running child) and get it instead one time in three — the parent frowns (`sulk`). Plain outcome: the parent gets it two times in three.
- [ ] Short by one (economy + relationships, builds on run 110's `stole`): a baker with `mind.stole > 0` who is paid a coin at the counter by the player (the `pay_counter` player branch) says `mind.line("paid_back")` (four lines, "That is one back.") and `stole` falls by one — amends are made through the same dish, not by apology; at `stole == 0` the greeting returns to the fond/cold lines. The resident half: a resident whose coin was knocked out of their pocket and pocketed by the player (`drop_coin` → `stoop` by the player within 6m of the fallen one) gets the same `stole` key on their own mind, and the same way back.

## Grows from Noticeboard (run 100 — the first paper in the town that anyone passing can read; deepen it before the rooftop drop)
- [ ] Notes that ask (quests axis — `game/` still has no task anywhere; this is the smallest real one): one resident in ten who pins a note pins a *want* — `"Lost: <item>"` for an item they owned (`apple`, `cup`, `umbrella`, `book`), stored on the note as meta `want` + `by`; `C` on the board with that item in hand pins nothing and instead hands it to the board ("Left for <handle>." toast), the asker walks over within a minute, takes it with the `pin` reach, says `mind.line("found_mine")` and is fond +0.05 of the finder; empty-handed `C` reads the newest want aloud as a toast. Residents who carry the wanted kind past the board do the same one time in three. Plain pin/take stay 100% when there is no want.
- [ ] Notes go yellow (time axis, builds on the board): a note's paper lerps from `f7f4ef` toward `e6d3a5` over one `DAY_LEN` (per-note material, updated every 5s, not per frame), so the board reads its own age from the street; `scan` readers stop longer (5–7s) at a board where every note is fresh, shorter (2–3s) when all are yellow; the scribe takes exactly the yellow ones — the 16:00 rule stays, the colour is just its sign.
- [ ] A path up the north slope (places axis, terrain — thin at five open lines): from the lane's fence gap at x 0 a 1.4m dirt path (`_path` segments following `town.gen.height`) climbs north to a flat 6×6m clearing at the first WorldGen rise (around z −34), with a rough log bench and a cairn; the clearing is a `lookout` spot (the `shade` pose already looks out) that residents pick on clear mornings by `mind.score` (curious), and the cairn takes one stone (a new `stone` item lying at the clearing's edge — none exists yet; `C` with it in hand: a `stack` reach, the cairn grows one stone, persisted only for the session).
- [ ] The dropped bundle goes home (quests axis, polish run 101 — a knocked-down scribe now drops the notes they took off the board as one `paper` with `count`, instead of losing them): a bundle lying on the ground 20s (`dropped_at`) makes the scribe, if within 25m and not in `drive`/`chase`, walk over, pick it up with the existing pick pose and file it at the letter wall on the next `_wall_pick`; if the player is holding it, the scribe says `mind.line("bundle_mine")` once, and the player who files it at the wall gets the scribe's `letter_in` thanks and `mind.gifted()`. Any other resident who picks one up already files it (the `carrying_kind` "paper" branch).
- [ ] Pigeons on the drip board (animals axis, on the bird rig `bird3d.gd` — two open animal lines in `game/`; the `game/` half of the web's "Pigeons on the square" under Grows from Animals, starting at one board instead of a flock): two pigeons land on the noticeboard's drip board at 07:00–10:00 in `clear` weather, bob and walk its length, peck at the pin heads; anyone playing `pin` within 1.5m (player or resident) makes them flap up (`flap`) and settle on the central house's ridge, returning 6s later; a resident in `scan` with a pigeon on the board says `mind.line("pigeon")` once. Scavengers rules: they never take a note.
- [ ] A garden behind the Sunroom (places axis — thin, and the Sunroom stands on bare grass): a 5×3m fenced yard on the house's north side (x −21.8..−17.2, z −21.6..−18.6, clear of the lane fence that starts at x −13) reached by a side gate on the west wall and a 1.2m gravel path round the house; inside, a low sandpit (`e6d3a5`, 0.1 high) with a bucket and spade (`spade` item: carry, `C` in the sand = a new `dig` pose — 0.3 crouch, three scoops 0.4 each tossing sand, 0.3 stand — that leaves a small mound mesh that flattens after 60s) and a clothesline with three small shirts; children play in the sandpit 10:00–11:00 instead of following, the storysitter sits on a yard stool watching; the player digs the same way.
- [ ] Waking up (time-and-motions axis — the clock has a dawn and nobody wakes into it): the first resident out of each house between 06:00 and 07:30 stops on the doorstep and plays a new `wake` pose (0.5s windup lifting both arms overhead, 1.0s hold rising onto the toes with the head tipped back in a yawn, 0.4s recovery dropping the arms and a shake of the shoulders) before their routine; the player plays the same `wake` when getting up from a `bed` spot after resting, and a resident watching from within 4m says `mind.line("morning")`.

## Grows from Busker's hat (run 111 — the first job paid by other residents, and the first time the town stops to watch something; deepen it before a second performer)
- [ ] A request (relationships + motions axis): a resident who has tipped the same busker twice (`mind.tipped` int, saved with the memory) does not walk on after the 3 s — they say `mind.line("request")` (new category, four lines) and the busker answers with the next two loops at a slower 1.6 Hz strum with a deeper off-beat nod (`strum` reads a `tempo` meta), the requester stays both loops in `wait` facing the crate and the two `befriend` by 0.1; the player gets the same by pressing E at the cap after two tips (the resident's line is the ask, the slower loops are the answer). Plain outcome: a first-time listener still gets the 3 s and walks on.
- [ ] The cap can be taken (economy + combat axis): a tap of C at the cap with an empty pocket still only earns a nod; holding C 0.45 s (the furniture long-press) grabs the cap with its coins — the busker cuts the set (`strum_end`), steps down and chases like a knocked resident (`_chase`, FightMoves), the cap is a carried item that drops on any hit and spills its coins one by one (`drop_coin` per disc, all of them), and the busker's mind gets `robbed()` like the baker's till. Residents who saw it (`witnessed`) walk on without stopping at the next set. Plain outcome: a tap is a tap; nothing changes for anyone who does not hold the key.
- [ ] A crowd that sits (places axis — the lane bench at (4, −11.4) is 5.6 m from the crate and faces the road): during the evening set a listener with `energy < 0.5` routes to that bench instead of walking on and stays until `strum_end`, facing the crate (a `face_at` on the bench spot), and a fourth listener with the bench full stands behind it with `wait`; the first time three sit at once the busker says `mind.line("crowd")` (three lines) and tips that set double (`randf() < 0.66` → `< 0.9` for the seated). The player on the bench during a set is counted too. Plain outcome: a fresh listener is unchanged.

## Grows from Ledger table (run 112 — the first debt in the town and the first page that remembers a person by name; deepen it before a second ledger)
- [ ] The baker reads the page (relationships axis): at 17:00, before collecting, the baker plays `scan` at the ledger for one SCAN_T and names the biggest debtor by handle (`mind.line("tab_worst")`, new category, the name substituted); a resident with `tab >= 3` who passes within 6 m while the baker is at the page gets `mind.score` ×0.3 on `counter` for the rest of the day (shy, never refused) and a `greet_tab` line from the baker at their next meeting; the player at `tab >= 3` hears `greet_tab` too, and one coin in the bowl clears the look.
- [ ] Paying for a friend (relationships + economy axis): a resident with `coins >= 3`, `social > 0.7` and a friend (`relation_k > 0.5`) whose `tab > 0` takes the friend's line instead of their own — the same three beats, the coin into the bowl, the friend's `tab −1` and `befriend(+0.3)`, and `mind.line("paid_for")` at their next meeting; the player: C at the ledger while a resident with a tab stands at the other slot pays theirs first (a toast names them).
- [ ] The tally on the board (letters + economy axis — run 100's board rotates note kinds by hour): at 09:00 the scribe pins a `tally` note (the `paper` item with three ink ticks) carrying the sum of every tab in town; `scan` at the board toasts "n loaves on the house."; when the sum reaches 0 the note comes down with `notice_down` and the scribe says `mind.line("books_clear")`.

## Grows from Wages (run 113 — the first time work pays in the 3D town; deepen it before the general store spends it)
- [ ] Pay in hand, not in the air: the wage becomes a coin that moves — at the end of a work unit a disc appears at the site's plank stack (builders) or the counter's left end (the baker) and the worker `stoop`s it with the busker's-cap chain, so a knocked-over worker leaves it on the ground for anyone; plain outcome: the worker pockets it ≥ 90% (the only loss is a hit inside the 0.9 s stoop). The player's pay does the same at the site.
- [ ] A shift has an end: four work units in a row by one person (a stage) end with a `wipe` beat (new pose: 0.3 s the forearm crosses the brow, 0.5 s the shoulders drop and the head tips back, 0.3 s the hands go to the hips) and a `shift_done` line from the nearest builder ("That is a wall."); the player gets the same beat and a toast naming the stage; `probe_wages` counts the beat once per four units, never on the fifth.
- [ ] The rich spend: a resident's `mind.score` for `counter` and the café rises with `coins` above three (wages make the counters busier by evening), and a resident with eight or more buys a second loaf to carry home and eat at the table (`_bite` on the house chair), so pockets empty by night and the till fills — `probe_coins` reads the till at 17:00 higher on a day builders worked.

## Grows from General store (run 114 — the first shop that sells things you keep, and the first coins residents spend on something that is not food; deepen it before a second clothes shop)
- [ ] Sell it back (economy + items, run 114): `C` at the store counter holding a wearable you own (no `unpaid`) sells it for half its price (`prices.sell` cap 2 · beanie 2 · straw 2 · tophat 4 · scarf 2 · backpack 3); the keeper puts it on the matching shelf as second-hand (meta `used`, colour darkened 0.25) and the next buyer of that slot gets it one coin cheaper; a resident who buys a new hat while wearing one sells the old one the same way instead of it vanishing (`town_store.don` keep_prev → sell path), so the shelves fill with the town's old hats.
- [ ] Glasses on the shelf (items, run 114): a fourth shelf for the store in `ShopKit` — kind `glasses` with MIX [glasses, sunglasses] (face socket, price 6 each in `prices.json`); residents with `curious` < 0.4 buy sunglasses on a clear afternoon and `doff` them at dusk (face slot — `doff` takes a slot already), the player wears them with the same `don`; the glasses sit on the shelf front-up (Wear origin is the face, so lift them 0.05).
- [ ] The store window (places/props, run 114): `HouseStyles.shop_front` gets a GENERAL STORE front — a hat on a post, a scarf over a rail, an umbrella bucket by the door, prices chalked on a small board — so the street says what is sold inside; a spot kind "window" in front of it where passing residents stop for 3 s with the `shade` pose and say `mind.line("window")`; the player's `C` there reads the board (toast with the price lines).

## Grows from Odd-jobs board (run 115 — the first task system in the 3D town: a resident's need becomes a card with a coin on it, and a stranger or the player earns it; deepen it before a second board)
- [ ] (motions, run 115 check) The card in hand: a resident who has taken a job card carries it visibly — a small paper (the `_card_node` paper, no coin disc) in the left-hand socket while walking to the woodpile or the oven, tucked into the shirt with a 0.4 s `tuck` pose (`stick3d` family with `pin`: hand to chest, flat press, drop) before the first chop or knead, and pulled out and waved once (0.5 s `wave_p`) when the coin is paid; the player's job paper uses the same `tuck` on the first work beat. Probe: `probe_jobs.gd` checks the paper node exists between take and pay and is gone after.
- [ ] (quests) The third card, "A book for the Sunroom": posted when the Sunroom shelf is 0 at story time (14:30–16:00) by a resident who is going there; the taker with the card follows the run 106 chain — `_swap_pick` to the book box for one book (shelve), then `_story_pick` to a cushion with it (lend_book) — and `job_count("book")` is the shelf stock, counted while the taker is inside the Sunroom (door within 4 m). The player does the same: take a book from the box, C on a cushion with it. One run.
- [ ] (relationships) Cards by name: the poster's handle is inked on the card (a short Label3D on the paper, mono, 10 px) and on the player's job paper toast ("Two logs to the stove — for <name>. A coin on it."); when paid, the taker greets the poster by name the next time they pass within 3 m ("Thanks for the coin, <name>." — `mind.line("job_thanks")`, once), and `mind.friends[by.uid]` rises 0.2 for both; a poster whose card was taken by the player greets the player with `greet_fond` on the next meeting. One run.
- [ ] (economy) The player's own card: C at the odd-jobs board with a coin in the pocket and nothing in hand while the top slot is free pins a card of the player's choosing (C cycles "Two logs to the stove" / "Knead one" on a 2 s toast, SPACE confirms) — the coin leaves the wallet (`_set_coins`) onto the card; a resident takes it like any other, does the task, and is paid the player's coin with `job_done`; untaken by 17:00 the player gets a toast "Nobody took your card." and the coin back at 08:00. One run.

## Grows from Pawn post (run 116 — the first place in the town that keeps a thing for you against a coin, and the first shelf that turns into a shop overnight; deepen it before a second booth)
- [ ] Pawn tickets as paper (economy + items — a pawnshop really gives you a ticket, and a ticket is a thing that can be lost): the hand-in ends with the broker passing a paper `ticket` (the `paper` item at 0.6 scale with an ink stamp, meta `pawn` = the shelf entry) into the customer's hand; redeeming with the ticket on you (hand or belt pocket) costs the tag price, without it one coin more; a knocked-down person drops the ticket like anything held, and whoever picks it up can redeem that item for the tag price — the first thing in the town worth stealing twice. Residents keep theirs in the belt socket and hand it over before the `put` beats. Plain outcome: nobody is refused for want of a ticket.
- [ ] Residents pawn what they carry, not only hats (economy + jobs): a resident walking with a `log` or `book` in hand, `coins == 0` and `mind.full < 0.3`, hands that over at the booth instead of the hat (same `pass`, value from `sell`; the woodcutter's log chain releases it — `carrying_kind` cleared); the shelf then holds logs and books anyone can buy after 08:00 for `sell + 1`, and the Sunroom reader and the stove-feeder each get a `mind.score` term for a booth with stock of their kind — the first second-hand shelf, feeding two existing chains.
- [ ] Rain shuts the booth early (weather axis — the booth has a roof and no walls): when `weather` turns to `rain` during the shift the shutter comes down, the broker steps back under the roof with `wait` (arms crossed) until it clears or 16:00, a customer mid-`pass` finishes (the broker's answering `pass` still plays), a customer mid-`put` keeps paying; residents heading to the booth re-pick. Plain outcome: the booth is closed a little more often on wet days, nothing else changes.
- [ ] (code hygiene, check of run 116) Split `resident.gd` (510 lines, over the 500 cap — the cap itself is met since the check of run 117: the swing/seesaw helpers went to `resident_play.gd`, 481 lines left; what follows is still open): move the random spot-pool exclusion list (the kinds list `probe_mind.gd` copies by hand and has drifted from twice, runs 112 and 116) into one `const SPOT_SKIP` on `ResidentBase` that both the resident and the probe read, and move the spot-pick/schedule block into a new `resident_pick.gd` layer in the chain. No behaviour change; probes mind/fight/traffic identical.
## Grows from Your own house (run 117 — the first room the player fills by hand, and the first furniture that is bought, carried and saved; deepen it before a second shop sells things for the home)
- [ ] Residents' furniture survives the night (economy + homes, run 117 — a resident's bought piece lives only for this session while the player's is saved): write `_extra` (door → pieces) to `user://furnish.json` on every `furn_add` and read it in `_growth_init` after the doors exist (door index is stable per lot order; key by lot order, not by index, so a house built out of order keeps its chairs), and let a home with three pieces ask for a fourth only when `mind.social > 0.6` (a sofa for guests) — the room's `_place` already clamps anything the layout's walls would swallow. Plain outcome: the same three pieces, now still there tomorrow.
- [ ] Guests sit on what you bought (relationships + homes, run 117 — the Homes section's "Guests" line, now with a reason to visit): a resident with `mind.fond > 0.3` for the player walks to your door once a day when you are inside (the proxy system already stands residents in rooms), takes the nearest placed `sit` spot with the same sit pose, says `mind.line("visit")` (four new lines) and leaves after 40 s; with no chair placed they stand on the doormat, say "No chair.", and go — the first thing in the town a resident says about your taste. Plain outcome: a visit a day at most, and only when you are home.
- [ ] Things for the wall and a `hang` pose (motions + items, run 117 — the catalogue sells only floor pieces; `RoomKit.PIECES` already has `frames`, `posters`, `mirror`, `star_chart`): a second catalogue table row sells wall boxes (frames 3 · posters 2 · mirror 5 · star chart 4); inside your house C with one in hand near a wall (within 0.7 m of the shell, facing it) plays `hang` (0.3 s both hands lift the frame to eye height, 0.15 s hold with the head tilting to judge it, 0.4 s one hand taps a corner level and both drop) and the piece appears flat on that wall at 1.4 m; long C takes it down. Residents hang theirs too (`furn_wish` adds frames for `curious`). Plain outcome: the floor pieces are untouched; the wall is a second place to put things.
- [ ] (relationships + code hygiene, check of run 117) The playground helpers speak with minds: `resident_play.gd`'s `go_push`/`go_seesaw`/`seesaw_launch` still pick lines with `[...][uid % 3]` — give them `mind.line("push")`, `mind.line("seesaw")`, `mind.line("launch")` (four lines each in `minds.json` `default`, deadpan: "Hold on.", "I will take the other end.", "That was not in the plan."), and a finished push (the rider gets off while this resident is still the pusher) calls `mind.befriend()` on the rider when the rider is a resident — a shared moment, per the fit checklist. Extend `probe_mind.gd` with one PUSH line (befriend 0 → >0).

## Grows from Updraft vents (run 118 — the first Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that lifts you instead of catching you; deepen it before the next pack)
- [ ] (animals + Climb, run 118 — the thinnest axis at five tagged lines) Swifts in the columns: a `bird3d.gd` rig swift per vent pair that circles the lit column in a 1.2 m helix (rising with the dust, flapping on the beat), dives to the dark tile's rim and perches there (bob, preen) while that vent is off, and startles off the rim when the player lands within 1 m; it never blocks a jump (no collision). Lives in `climb_updraft.gd` (`build` adds it, `_process` moves it off `active`), probe checks the perch/helix swap at t 0.5 and 2.5.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 118 — the second-thinnest axis, and the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `glide`) A wind pavilion at the tower foot: beside the Climb door on the plaza a four-post open pavilion with a bench, a signboard ("Mind the vents.") and one `ClimbUpdraft` tile built in town metres (x 16, z −70 outside the road band; the same builder with `K` 1) over a `vent` spot — standing on it when it is lit (same 4 s beat) lifts the player 2 m with the `glide` pose and sets them down on the pavilion's rim seat; residents walking to the tower plaza (`busy 1`) step on it in their routine via a `mind.score` curiosity term and say `mind.line("vent")` (four lines). Lives in `town_sites.gd`; `town_player` applies `step_vy` in metres; plain outcome: the tile is off half the time and you can walk round it.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, run 118) The vent tells before it swaps: 0.4 s before a pair changes the hum's pitch slides up a fifth (a second looping WAV swapped in) and the glow blinks twice, so a climber watching the dark vent knows when to jump; loose items (a hat, a dropped ice axe — "Things to carry up") inside a lit column ride it up and settle on the first platform above instead of lying on the tile.

- [ ] (code hygiene, check of run 119) `stick3d_poses.gd` is 453 lines, past the ~450 split line, and every Climb pack pose adds two more lines to `lean()`, `owns_right_arm()` and the `limbs()` fallthrough chain. Move the per-family dispatch (the `if p == …: lean = XPoses.lean(f)` lines and the `or XPoses.limbs(f, s)` chain) into a small `stick3d_families.gd` with one table `{pose: script}` so a new family is one line; `stick3d_poses.gd` drops under 420 and `probe_mushroom`, `probe_updraft`, `probe_fight` print the same numbers before and after.

- [ ] (code hygiene + vehicles, check of run 118) `probe_traffic.gd` is too noisy to be a gate: the same tree gives taxi/delivery 0, 1, 3 and 4 crashes on back-to-back runs (HEAD gave 1/1 twice), so a real regression and a lucky run look alike. Seed it — `seed(118)` at the top of `_init`, residents' `randf` routines and the cars' spawn jitter included — and print a fixed summary line (`TRAFFIC total crashes=N wrecks=M`) with a stated want (≤ 2 per car), so the next check reads a number, not a band. Check of run 127 (2026-10-09): one probe run gave a hatchback 26 crashes and 8 wrecks, the next 33 wrecks; HEAD without the run gave 9 and 2 wrecks with an SUV at 22 — the band is now 2..33 and says nothing.

## Grows from Bouncy mushrooms (run 119 — the second Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that throws you higher than you can jump; deepen it before the ghost platforms)
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, run 119) The cap remembers who stood on it: a cap that is bounced on three times inside one beat (4 s) goes limp — the dome droops to 0.7 on a tween and refuses the next 3 s (a body falls through to the cliff ledge below, no launch, a dull `thud` WAV instead of the boing) — so bouncing in place is not a free elevator; the probe bounces four times at one x and checks the fourth falls. Plain outcome stays: a single bounce always throws (3 of 4).
- [ ] (Climb + motions, run 119) Caps that throw sideways: a `tilt` field per planned cap (data: the gap's direction, ±1) so the dome is drawn leaning 0.25 rad toward the upper hop and the launch carries `vx` = ±200 px/s with it; the `bounce` tuck gets a 0.2 rad roll toward the flight side (meta `bounce_tilt`) so the body reads as thrown, not popped; the probe checks the x drift lands inside the upper hop's width for every cap of floors 1..37.
- [ ] (places + motions + children, run 119 — the motion-library rule's 'a use in the town' for `bounce`, and the relationships/children axis, second-thinnest at ~83 open lines) A play mushroom at the tower foot: beside the Climb door on the plaza (x 19, z −72, outside the road band) one `ClimbMushroom` cap built in town metres (the same builder with `K` 1, sunk into a sand ring) as the kids' trampoline — `resident_kid.gd` kids within 12 m queue at it (a `mind.score` play term), step on and bounce once with the `bounce` pose (launch 6 m/s in metres, `town_player`'s gravity), land in the sand with the brace, and say `mind.line("bounce")` (four lines in `minds.json`); the player can bounce too (walk onto the cap), and a kid who bounces right after the player calls `mind.befriend()` and follows the player to the Climb door once. Lives in `town_sites.gd`; probe: two kids bounce in turn, befriend fires once.
- [ ] (quests + economy, run 119 — the quests axis, thinnest at ~78 open lines) A spore errand on the odd-jobs board: a card "Bring back a mushroom spore from the tower" (coin from `prices.json` `prize.spore`) posted by a gardener resident; the first bounce on a cap of floor ≥ 5 in a visit drops a `spore` item (a cream puffball, one per visit, saved in records so it is not farmed), which comes down with the player at a camp exit like any carried thing; handing it to the card's resident pays the card and the resident walks to the garden plot and plants it with the `water` pose — a kid-size cap grows there over one in-game day (three tween stages, decorative, bounce-less). Probe: card posted, spore granted once, hand-in pays and the plot shows a cap the next morning.

## Grows from Ghost platforms (run 120 — the third Climb content pack and the first platform in the tower that is only there half the time; deepen it before the rope bridge)
- [ ] (economy + Climb, run 120 — the economy axis, thinnest after Climb itself at ~103 open lines) A phantom coin on the slab: every ghost slab carries one real coin item (run 107's coin builder, in `climb_ghost.gd` `build`) that stands on the slab only while it is solid and dissolves with it; a body that lands on the slab takes it with the `stoop` beat (`take(x, y, z)` in the pack, the owner session wires the call next to `land`), one per slab per visit and saved in records so the tower is not farmed; it comes down with the player at a camp exit like any carried thing and is paid into the wallet through the existing Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.ghost_coin`, 2). Mini-game entry stays free; the plain climb past the slab is untouched. Probe: coin present at t 0.5, gone at t 2.5, granted once across two landings.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, run 120 — the animals axis at ~117 open lines) A moth on the slab: a `moth3d.gd` rig (two paper wings, a thread body, two antennae) that ships with the full small-rig minimum — flap (12 Hz), rest (wings folding over 0.6 s, antennae twitching), spiral (a 0.6 m helix) and startle — and lives in `climb_ghost.gd`: while the slab is ghost the moth rests on the front outline dot; on the chime it lifts off and spirals round the slab while it is solid; a body landing within 0.5 m scatters it for 2 s. Never blocks a jump (no collision). Probe checks the rest/spiral swap at t 2.5 and 0.5 and the scatter.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, run 120) Ghost pairs from floor 18: the two longest gaps of a ghost floor get slabs with phases 0 and 0.5 (the second drawn with a cooler plum rim) so one is always solid and the route alternates on the beat instead of waiting; the fade also gets a sound — the chime's lower octave (330 Hz) at the start of the 0.4 s warning — so a climber mid-jump hears which slab is about to go. Data only (`pieces[].max` 2, `pair` phases like the vents) plus one WAV; the probe checks that at every t exactly one of the pair holds.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 120 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `lurch`) A rotten plank on the jetty (run 78's jetty): one plank near the punt that creaks (WAV) and gives the `teeter` warning after a body has stood on it 1.5 s, then drops it 0.4 m into the shallows with `lurch` and a `Water3D` splash; the builder (road 1) replaces it the next morning with the `hammer` pose, so it is a trap on only some days; residents who saw someone fall step round it for a day (`mind` memory, `mind.line("plank")` three lines) and the player can step over it. Plain outcome: the jetty stays walkable — the plank is one of twelve.

## Grows from Rope bridge (run 121 — the fourth Climb content pack and the first way across a gap in the tower that you walk instead of jump; deepen it before the windmill blades)
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 121 — the quests axis, thinnest after Climb itself at ~79 open lines) The missing plank: on bridge floors from 8 one plank of the deck is gone (two loose rope ends where it was, a one-pitch hole a body falls through — jumpable, so the plain crossing stays) and the spare plank leans on the lower hop; carrying it (two-hand carry, the existing carry pose) to the hole and C lays it with the `plonk` beat (run 117), the deck closes and creaks. The odd-jobs board (run 115) carries the card "Lay the plank on floor 8" with a coin prize paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.plank`, 3), once per floor per player, saved in records. Data: `kinds.bridge.hole_from` 8; the pack exposes `hole(on, x)` for the loader's standing test and `lay(x, y, z)` for C. Probe: a hole on floor 8, none on 4, closed after `lay`, paid once.
- [ ] (weather + Climb, run 121 — the weather axis at ~104 open lines) Wind on the deck: from the tower's wind floors (`wind_of`, 40 up) the bridge swings even when empty — amplitude and roll follow the band's wind (6 → up to 14 px, period 1.2 → 0.9 s, `kinds.bridge.wind_swing`/`wind_period`), the handrail threads stream downwind, and `sway` leans into the gusts (the pack writes `sway_k` from the wind too, so the body braces before it steps on). Data plus a `wind(k)` setter the loader calls with `wind_of(band)`; nothing changes below floor 40. Probe: swing amplitude at wind 1.0 is double the loaded calm swing and the pose's roll follows it.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, run 121 — builds on the ghost pack's `lurch`) Worn planks: from floor 24 every third plank is `worn` (greyer, an ink crack); standing on one for 0.8 s gives the `teeter` warning, then it drops out with `lurch` (run 120) and a snap WAV, leaving a one-pitch hole for 6 s before it swings back up; at most one worn plank drops per crossing and the hole is jumpable, so the plain crossing stays the most likely outcome. Data (`kinds.bridge.worn_from` 24, `worn_every` 3), `worn(on, x)` for the loader's standing test. Probe: no worn planks on floor 4, one drop per crossing on floor 24, the hole closes at 6 s.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 121 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `sway`, and a structural-growth item under the strict definition) A hanging footbridge over the river at the meadow (run 68's river; the stepping stones of run 84 were the second crossing — this is the third): move the catenary and `_lay` out of `climb_bridge.gd` into a shared `rope_kit.gd` (the town-is-the-engine rule: one builder for the tower and the town), hang a 7 m deck between two posts on the banks upstream of the stone bridge, give residents' river routes a `mind.score` term for it so some cross there with `sway` (the player too, with the same dip, swing and creak), and add the task "Cross the rope bridge" to the odd-jobs board for the first time. This is a new traversable connection in the map graph — the first structural run after the owner-priority sections should take it.

## Grows from Windmill blades (run 122 — the fifth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that carries you somewhere instead of holding you; deepen it before the icicles)
- [ ] (economy + Climb, run 122 — the economy axis, thinnest after Climb itself at ~123 open lines by keyword) A crate in the cup: on wheel floors from 15 one of the three cups carries a paper crate of the general store's goods (run 114's crate builder, in `climb_windmill.gd` `build`, riding level in the cup); a body that rides that cup takes the crate with the existing carry pose (`take(on)` in the pack, the owner session calls it when `landed["arm"]` is the crate's sail), the cup is empty until the wheel is rebuilt, once per wheel per visit saved in records so the tower is not farmed; the crate comes down with the player at a camp exit like any carried thing and sells at any staffed counter for `prices.json` `sell.crate` (2) through the existing selling path in `town_growth.gd`. Probe: false for a cup left early, true for the full half turn, paid once.
- [ ] (weather + Climb, run 122 — wind on the wheel) From the tower's wind floors (`wind_of`, 40 up) the wheel is a real windmill: `rev_s` scales with the band's wind (0.2 → up to 0.35 at |wind| 180, `kinds.wheel.wind_rev`), it turns the way the wind blows when that side rises for the lower hop and against it otherwise (the geometry wins — the lower hop's side always rises), the clack pitch rises with the speed and the sails get a 6 Hz paper flutter (`arm.rotation.y` ±0.04); nothing changes below floor 40. Data plus a `wind(k)` setter the loader calls with `wind_of(band)`. Probe: period 5.0 s at wind 0, ≈ 2.9 s at wind 180, flutter only above 40.
- [ ] (places + motions + children, run 122 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `ride`, and depth for run 102's children) A roundabout in the park: a wooden merry-go-round (hub, four paper cups like the tower's, a push bar) on the park gravel near the seesaw (run 108's `seesaw3d.gd` is the model — one `roundabout3d.gd` with `spin(k)`, `tip(k)`, `ride(fig)`); a child or the player in a cup gets `ride` with the real `ride_k`/`ride_v` from the spin; whoever stands at the bar pushes with the existing `push` pose and the spin decays over 12 s; children pick it through a `mind.score` term (curiosity + a free cup), two children at once befriend (`mind.befriend()`), a pushed-too-fast rider says `mind.line("dizzy")` and sits on the gravel 4 s. Plain outcome: pushing and riding both always work; the dizzy line is one in four spins. Probe: spin decays, a child boards within 60 s of daytime, the player's push adds speed.

## Grows from Falling icicles (run 123 — the sixth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that comes down at you; deepen it before the gears)
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 123 — the quests axis, thinnest after Climb at ~89 open lines by keyword) "Through the Wind cliffs without a scratch": the odd-jobs board (run 115) carries that card with a coin prize paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.cliffs`, 3); the pack counts its own hits per floor (`hits[n]`, reset when a band is rebuilt) and exposes `clean(from, to)` — true when floors `from..to` were crossed with zero strikes — and the loader reports it once per theme block (20..24, 30..34, …) per player, saved in records; the card's resident says `mind.line("cliffs")` when paid (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`). Plain outcome untouched: the card only pays, it never changes the icicles. Probe: a strike on floor 22 → false for 20..24, none → true, paid once.
- [ ] (Climb, run 123 — the Climb axis, thinnest at ~81 open lines by keyword) Knock them down first: X (punch) while standing under a hanging icicle within reach (tip at most 50 px above the feet + 40, |dx| < 24) calls `knock(x, y, z)` — the icicle shatters early (glass, shards, regrow) and the way is clear for its whole cycle; a trembling one knocked down still counts as a hit on nobody; residents in the tower (the bot climbers of the multiplayer item) knock too through the same call. Plain outcome: knocking always works when in reach; jumping through a lip without knocking stays ≥ 94% safe as today. Probe: knock on a growing one → gone; nothing for one 60 px above or a falling one.
- [ ] (weather + Climb, run 123 — the fall answers the floor's weather) Wind bends the drop, stars light it: from the tower's wind floors (`wind_of`, 40 up — the second Wind cliffs block is 55..59) a falling icicle drifts with `wind_of(band)` px/s like a body does, so its shatter point moves and `_floor` is re-read at the drifted x (a `wind(k)` setter the loader calls); on Starlit floors the ice carries a faint pulse of the theme's `f2c84b` (emission 0..0.3 with the tremble) and the glass sits a fifth higher (`glass_hz` 2700 under `themes_glass`), so the two seasons sound different. Nothing changes below 40 or off the two themes. Probe: drift 60 px/s → tip x after 0.39 s moved 23 px; glow only on 30..34; pitch per theme.
- [ ] (places + motions + economy, run 123 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `duck`, and road 4's orchard made usable) Shake the apple trees: the orchard trees on the tower road (road 4) and the garden-plot fruit trees carry 3 apples in the crown; C at the trunk is a `shake` family (two hands on the trunk, a 0.3 s shove, 0.6 s rattle with the crown swaying — Animal Crossing's shake) and 2–3 apples drop over 0.8 s; everyone under the crown within 1.6 m — the shaker and bystanders — gets `duck` (`DuckPoses.hold` while apples are in the air, the 0.3 s release after), an apple that meets a head bounces off with `mind.line("ouch")` (one in four shakes); the apples are the general store's `apple` item (`prices.json` `sell.apple` 1, eat with the existing `eat`), residents shake when hungry near an orchard through a `mind.score` term and share through run 101's hand-over, the crown regrows its apples at dawn. Plain outcome: the shake always drops apples. Probe: three apples, a bystander ducks, the bonk line one in four, regrowth at dawn.
- [ ] (relationships + Climb, 2026-10-08 check of run 123) Shout a warning: a climber who is standing under a trembling icicle while another body (player or bot climber) is within 120 px below gets a new `warn` pose (0.15 s arm snaps up to point at the lip, 0.4 s hold with two jabs of the finger, 0.25 s drop — `stick3d_warn.gd`) and a deadpan line over the head ("Overhead.") sent as an `ev` so others see it; the warned one ducks through the existing `ClimbIcicle.near` path a beat earlier (tremble warning +0.2 s for them only), and a resident who was warned that way calls `mind.befriend()` on the warner once per climb. Pure helper `warn(x, y, z, others) -> Array` on the pack; probe extends `probe_icicle.gd`.

## Grows from Conveyor gears (run 124 — the seventh Climb content pack and the first floor in the tower that moves you while you stand still; deepen it before an eighth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 124 — the Climb axis, thinnest at ~72 open lines by keyword) A lever on the cliff: each belt gets an iron lever on the rock behind its upper gear (C within 30 px of it, standing on the belt) that throws the gears the other way for everyone at once — `throw(id)` on the pack sets an override `until` the cycle's next stall (at most 5 s), the clank plays, the lever's arm swings 0.3 s and springs back when the override ends; sent as an `ev` so other climbers see the lever move and feel the same belt (the clock stays shared — the override is a dict keyed by belt id that `dir_at` consults first); bot climbers throw it when the belt runs against the way they want to go. Plain outcome unchanged: without a throw the belt is exactly today's. Probe: throw at t 1 → dir −1 until the stall at 5.0, then the clock's own −1 run continues; a throw during a stall does nothing; lever node swings and returns.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 124 — the quests axis, thinnest after Climb at ~90 open lines by keyword) "Against the current": the odd-jobs board (run 115) carries a card paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.belts`, 3) for crossing every belt of one Clockwork floor against its direction without leaving a hop — the pack records per belt the end a body stepped on from and the belt's direction then, and `crossed(id)` turns true when the same body steps off at the far end having walked against it the whole way (`rode` spans, reset when the band is rebuilt); `floor_done(n)` is true when all of a floor's belts are crossed; the loader reports once per player and floor, saved in records like the Climb camps; the card's resident says `mind.line("belts")` when paying (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`). Plain outcome untouched: the card only pays, nothing refuses.
- [ ] (places + jobs + motions, run 124 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `tread`, and road 1's construction chain made visible) A treadwheel crane at the tower-road lot: the builder's lot (road 1) gets a wooden treadwheel (a 2.4 m drum on a frame, a rope over a jib, a plank hook — `props3d.gd` builder) and from the walls stage on, one plank per shift goes up by the crane instead of by hand: the builder steps into the drum and walks it in `tread` (the pack's metas written from the drum's speed, always "against", so the short bent steps show) for 4 s while the plank rises on the rope to the frame, then hammers as today; the player can walk the drum with C (same pose, same 4 s) and that counts as a plank of progress — the second way to help build a house; residents within 8 m stop to watch (spot "watch", `mind.line("building")`). Pure helper `town_lots.crane_tick(lot, dt)`; probe: drum turns only while someone treads, the plank reaches the frame at 4 s, both the builder and the player use `tread`.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, run 124 — the animals axis at ~106 open lines, and the tower has not one living thing) Pigeons on the gears: each stalled gear seats a pigeon (the existing bird rig `bird3d.gd` — bob, peck, flap, hop — the same one road 4's fence-post birds will use) that pecks at the slats, hops to the other gear when the belt runs the other way, and flaps off 0.4 s before the clank (it hears the escapement) to land again when the gears stop — so the birds are the tell for the reversal; on floors 50+ a crow instead, which stays put and turns with the gear for a quarter turn before it steps off; a body landing within 40 px makes it flap to the cliff and back. No mechanics, only life — the gears are read by their birds. Probe: a bird exists per stalled gear, flaps 0.4 s before a start, none on a running gear.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-08 check of run 124) Things ride the belt too: a coin or hat dropped on a running belt (`ClimbGears.carry_item(id, on, dt)`, same 90 px/s and the same clock as bodies) travels to the end, tips off with a 0.2 s wobble at the gear and falls to the hop below under the tower's G; a stalled belt holds it still; the climber who picks it up off a moving belt uses `stoop` timed to the slat it sits on (miss → it slides past, plain pick-up still ≥ 60% because the belt only runs 45% of the time toward you); a resident climber does the same for a dropped coin on its way up; probe the item's x after 1 s, the tip-off at the end, and the stall.

## Grows from Pendulum weights (run 125 — the eighth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower you hang from that carries you; deepen it before a ninth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 125 — the pack's own next step) Pumping the swing: holding ← or → toward the way the bar is going for a whole half-swing adds 0.1 to that weight's amplitude factor (`pump` per bob, 1.0..1.6, −0.05 per idle half), so the ends reach further out and higher by L(1 − cos) — a pumped weight lifts a hanging body's hands above the upper hop's edge and the release jump needs no charge; everyone sees the same amplitude (the factor goes out as an `ev`; bot climbers pump when the upper hop is above their hands). Plain outcome unchanged: not pumping is exactly today's swing, and `clear` still holds at the cap because the extra reach is checked in `plan`. Probe: the factor after four pumped halves, the decay, the cap, the end height.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 125) "Keep time": the odd-jobs board (run 115) carries a card paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.swings`, 3) for hanging from one weight through three full swings without letting go — the pack counts `swings` per body while `hang_on` runs and `held_for(id)` reports it; the loader reports once per player and floor, saved in records like the Climb camps; the card's resident says `mind.line("swings")` when paying (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`). Plain outcome untouched: the card only pays, nothing refuses.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 125 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `dangle`) A swing-bar at the tower road's rest stop (road 2): a wooden frame with an iron bar at 2.1 m beside the bench; C under it jumps, grabs and hangs in `dangle` (the pose's own 2.4 s beat, no meta), three swings and a drop with the landing squat; children (run 102) take it in their play routine through a `mind.score` term "bar", adults with `brave` > 0.6 try it once a day and say `mind.line("bar")`; residents within 6 m stop to watch (spot "watch"). Pure helper in `town_places.gd`; probe: the player and a child both reach `dangle`, the drop lands on the path, the watcher count.
- [ ] (vehicles, run 125 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at ~53 open lines by keyword) The clock cart: once a day at noon a resident horologist drives a small cart (`Car3D` kind "cart", a new spec: slow, open bed, no doors) from the square to the Climb tower door with a crate of brass weights, parks on the plaza, carries one weight in (`carry` pose, through the door — a 3 s scene) and drives back; the cart's bed shows the weights, one fewer after each delivery, refilled overnight; the player can ride along as a passenger (existing passenger code) and get off at the plaza. Probe: the cart leaves at 12:00 town time, reaches the plaza, the crate count drops, the cart is back by 12:10.
- [ ] (animals, run 125 — the animals axis, second-thinnest at ~59 open lines by keyword) A bat under the bracket: on Clockwork floors 50+ each weight's bracket shelters a bat (a new small rig `bat3d.gd` beside `bird3d.gd`: wings fold, hangs upside down, drop-and-flap, ear twitch) that hangs from the beam and, when a body grabs the bar, drops, circles the gap once (1.5 s) and hangs again; two bats on floors 85+; a body landing within 40 px of the beam makes it flap to the cliff and back. No mechanics, only life — the weight is read by its bat. Probe: a bat per weight on 50+, none on 16..18, the drop on grab, the return.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-08 check of run 125) Two on one bar: a second body that grabs a weight already held (`ClimbPendulum.grab` does not check who else holds it, so today two bodies snap to the same point under the rod) takes the other half of the crossbar (hands 10 px either side of the rod, the bar width 24 already fits two), the rod tilts 0.03 rad toward the heavier side and the swing's period stays the clock's 2.4 s so both still ride the same shared beat; when one lets go the other keeps hanging; a bot climber waits on the hop for a free bar unless it is a friend (`mind.befriend()` when two share a full swing, `mind.line("bar_share")`, three deadpan lines in `minds.json`). Plain outcome unchanged: a lone body hangs exactly as now. Probe: two grabs on one bob both succeed, both positions follow the bar, a let-go leaves the other held.

## Grows from Cloud rafts (run 126 — the ninth Climb content pack and the first platform in the tower that gives under you; deepen it before a tenth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 126 — the pack's own next step) Two on a raft: a second body landing on a raft already loaded sinks it twice as fast (`stand` counts bodies per tick through a `riders` set the loader fills, `sink_s` / riders) and the raft lists 0.08 rad toward the heavier side (the side with more bodies, or the one nearer the edge when equal), so two climbers cannot both wait on one cloud — the first to jump is fine, the second jumps from lower; bot climbers (planned in the backlog's multiplayer lines) never land on a raft a friend is on unless the upper hop is closer than 40 px. Plain outcome unchanged: one body sinks exactly as now. Probe: load after 0.75 s with two riders is 1.0, the list angle and its side, the empty raft levels.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 126 — the quests axis, thinnest at ~92 open lines by keyword) "Light feet": the odd-jobs board (run 115) carries a card paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.raft`, 3) for crossing a Cloud sea floor's raft without sinking it past a third (`stand` reports `load` per body and the loader keeps the max for the floor; the pack exposes `touched(id)` so the card only counts a floor whose raft was actually landed on); once per player and floor, saved in records like the Climb camps; the card's resident says `mind.line("raft")` when paying (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`). Plain outcome untouched: the card only pays, nothing refuses.
- [ ] (vehicles, run 126 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at ~72 open lines by keyword) The cloud ferry: on Cloud sea floors from 60 the rest band's shelter (`%dr`) moors a flat cloud barge (the raft's puffs scaled to 180 px, a rope rail, a lantern) that a resident ferryman poles (`moor` and a new `pole` beat from the `push` family — a 1.2 s plant, lean and recover) along the rest band from its left edge to its right every 20 s and back; the player and bots board by walking on (the barge is a wide raft that does not sink), ride standing in `wade`, and step off at either end — the first thing in the tower that is driven by a resident. Pure helper in `climb_raft.gd` (`barge(n)`), visuals in `build`; probe: the barge leaves at 0 and arrives at 20 s, a body on it is carried the band's width, the ferryman's pose cycles.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 126 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `wade`, and a structural-growth item under the strict definition: a new traversable connection) The ford: downstream of the stepping stones (run 84) the river widens to a knee-deep gravel shallows 5 m across between two mud banks with reed tufts — a fourth river crossing after the stone bridge, the stones and the punt. Walking in is allowed anywhere on the shallows: the body sinks to the knee (`wade`, the same pose, `wade_k` from the water depth at that x), walks at 0.6 speed, and comes out with wet boots the cobbler (run 80) comments on for a minute; residents' river routes get a `mind.score` term "ford" (children of run 102 prefer it, elders never take it), the dog (animals section) splashes through it after its walker, and the odd-jobs board carries "Cross the ford" once. Pure water-depth helper in `town_places.gd`, the shallows mesh in `town_build.gd`; probe: a resident path through the ford reaches `wade`, the speed drop, the dry path on the bridge unchanged.
- [ ] (motions + Climb, 2026-10-08 check of run 126) The shake-off: a body stepping from a raft (or later the ford) onto a dry hop with `wade` load above 0.3 plays a new `shake` pose before walking on — 0.15 s the back foot lifts behind with the knee bent, 0.4 s two flicks of the shin (the foot ±0.25 rad, a few cotton puffs falling from the boot via the raft's puff stream), 0.2 s the foot sets down — movable throughout at 0.8 speed so it never costs a jump; bot climbers and, once the ford ships, residents leaving the shallows play the same beat with the same timings. Probe: shake only after load > 0.3, none from a raft barely touched, none on a hop-to-hop landing.

## Grows from Rolling ladder (run 127 — the tenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower you ride on purpose instead of jumping; deepen it before an eleventh set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 127 — the pack's own next step) Books on the rungs: while the ladder is parked, a body standing on the upper hop within reach can C a second time to take a book from the shelf behind the rail (a new `reach` beat inside the `rail` family — 0.3 s the front hand stretches to the shelf, 0.2 s it comes back with a paper block held, the `carrying` socket), carry it one floor and leave it on the next rest band's shelter bench (`plonk`), where it stays for the session and the Climb sign reads "1 book returned" per floor; bot climbers do the same on one floor in five. Plain outcome untouched: a mount still takes priority when the ladder is at this end, and the reach only exists when it is parked at the far end — nothing refuses, nothing costs. Probe: reach only from the upper hop, only while parked away, the block in hand, the bench count.
- [ ] (Climb, run 127 — the Climb axis, thinnest at ~76 open lines by keyword) Two hands on the rail: the ladder carries two bodies — a second climber (a friend over `poz_net`, or a bot) who presses C at the same end while the first is still in the mount phase rides on the rung below (`ride_on` takes a `rung` slot 0 or 1, the lower body 22 px down and 6 px behind, the same `rail` pose with `rail_c` and `rail_v` shared), the roll takes 1.5× as long with two aboard (the wheels groan — the clunk's tone a fifth lower, `clunk_hz` × 0.67), and both step off in order. Plain outcome: one body rides exactly as now. Probe: slot assignment, the slower roll, two step-offs, no third rider.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 127 — the quests axis, thinnest at ~87 open lines by keyword) "The long way round": the odd-jobs board (run 115) carries a card paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.ladder`, 2) for crossing every Library floor's gap by ladder instead of jumping on one tower run (the pack exposes `rode(n)` per floor — true once `ride_on` returned `done` there — and the loader keeps a set the card reads at the next camp door); once per player, saved in records like the Climb camps; the card's resident says `mind.line("ladder")` when paying (three deadpan lines in `minds.json` about librarians and patience). Plain outcome untouched: the card only pays, nothing refuses, jumping stays free.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 127 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `rail`) The reading room's ladder: the Story Sunroom (run 103) gets a two-metre bookshelf wall on its blind side with the same brass rail and rolling ladder (`ClimbLadder` reused as a town prop through `props3d.gd` — a `ladder(parent, w, h)` builder that returns the car node and a `roll(to)` tween), the reader's resident climbs it in `rail` to fetch the evening's book before the reading starts (a `mind.score` term "fetch" on the reader) and the player can C the rail to ride it for a book of their own (a paper block item, readable with `story` on the bench); the ladder in the town rolls on its own clock, not the tower's. Probe: the resident fetches once per reading, the player's ride, the book in hand.
- [ ] (motions + Climb, 2026-10-09 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: ten set-pieces each ship a pose, none ships a transition between two) Pose seams in the tower: when the loader swaps one pack pose for another within 0.3 s (`wade` → `rail`, `dangle` → `tread`, `duck` → `sway` …) `stick3d.gd` plays a 0.2 s `settle` blend instead of its default snap — the arms come down through the sides, never through the body, and the pelvis height eases — with a table of allowed pairs in `stick3d_poses.gd` so a pair that never meets costs nothing; probe: the shoulder never passes rotation.x 0 going the short way between `dangle` (−2.9) and `tread` (0.3) in under 0.2 s.
- [ ] (motions + Climb, check of run 127) The `rail` step-off: `stick3d_rail.gd` has no release, so on `ride_on`'s done tick the body snaps from both hands up the rungs to standing on the far hop. Give it `RailPoses.done(fig)` like `DuckPoses.done` — a 0.25 s beat where the back foot leaves the last rung after the front one lands, the rail hand lets go with a small push down (shoulder −2.8 → −0.3 eased, never through the head) and the pelvis drops 0.05 and recovers; the loader calls it when `riding` empties without a jump. Probe in `probe_ladder.gd`: shoulder at 0.1 s between −2.0 and −0.8, pose back to idle by 0.3 s, and a SPACE let-go skips it (a jump is its own exit).

- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 128 — the quests axis, thinnest at ~94 open lines by keyword) "Ring the hall": the odd-jobs board (run 115) carries a card paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.bell`, 2) for ringing every Old hall bell on floors 1–4 in one tower run (the pack exposes `rang(n)` per floor — true once a hoist started there — and the loader keeps a set the card reads at the first camp door); once per player, saved in records like the Climb camps; the card's resident says `mind.line("bell")` when paying (three deadpan lines in `minds.json` about bell-ringers and the hour). Plain outcome untouched: the card only pays, nothing refuses, jumping past a rope stays free.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 128 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `haul`) The well's bucket rope: the stone well of the tower road's rest stop (road 2) — or, until it exists, the cabin — gets a bucket on a hemp rope over a wooden wheel; a resident drawing water stands at the rim in `haul` (one pull beat per arm's length, four pulls for a full bucket, the pelvis dip on each, a `mind.score` term "water" when the trough is low), pours it into a trough the animals drink from (`Quad3D` drink), and the player can C the rope to draw a bucket of their own (a `bucket` item: carry, pour on a garden plot — counts as watering, `water` pose).
