- [ ] (motions + Climb, 2026-10-09 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: eleven packs each own one pose and seven of them end in a SPACE jump that borrows the plain jump's launch) Pack take-offs: a `wind` beat each hanging pose (`dangle`, `haul`, the `rail` roll) plays while the SPACE charge grows — knees tuck, the arms stay up, the pelvis loads 0.04 — so a jump from a rope reads as a swing-off, not a hop; the loader calls `XPoses.wind(fig, charge / CHARGE)` while `charge > 0` and the pose file owns the numbers; probe: the pelvis dips during a charge on a rope and not on a hop, and the tuck clears by 0.1 s after release.

## Grows from Vine curtain (run 129 — the twelfth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that is soft: it slows you on foot and catches you when you fall; deepen it before a thirteenth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 129 — the pack's own next step) Fruit in the vines: every third strand of a curtain carries a hanging fruit (the theme's amber, a 6 px ball) that a parting body knocks loose with a 0.3 s `pluck` beat inside the `part` family (the near hand closes on it as it sweeps) — the fruit drops into the hand, restores 0.25 stamina when eaten with C (`eat` pose, the fruit shrinks in two bites), regrows after 40 s on the loader's clock, and bot climbers pluck one when their stamina is under 0.5; the floor's Climb sign counts fruit per run. Plain outcome untouched: a body without C keeps parting and walking as now, nothing refuses, nothing costs. Probe: the pluck on the third strand, the regrow clock, the stamina, no pluck on a caught body.
- [ ] (Climb, run 129 — the Climb axis, thinnest at ~97 open lines by keyword) A curtain over a gap: a second piece kind `span` on Hanging garden floors hangs a wider curtain (96 px) across the floor's longest gap that no other pack took (bridge and ghost floors give up one, mushroom floors two) from a bar 150 px over the upper hop, with no platform under it — a body jumping into it from the lower hop is caught the same way and then slides down only to the vines' hem, hanging there over the drop (`part` caught metas) until SPACE jumps toward the upper hop with the swing's sideways speed added or Down drops into the gap; the strands carry a body for at most 4 s before they give (`lost`, a tearing rustle). Plain outcome: the ordinary crossing jump passes over the bar's shadow untouched — the span starts below the upper hop's height, so a full jump clears it; the span is the slow sure way for a short charge. Probe: the span's plan on a bridge floor, the catch over the drop, the hem hang, the 4 s give.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 129 — the quests axis, thinnest at ~100 open lines by keyword) "Through the garden": the odd-jobs board (run 115) carries a card paid through the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.vine`, 2) for reaching the floor-10 camp door without being caught by any Hanging garden curtain in one tower run (the pack exposes `caught_any()` since the run's first camp and the loader keeps the flag the card reads at the door); once per player, saved in records like the Climb camps; the card's resident says `mind.line("garden")` when paying (three deadpan lines in `minds.json` about keeping to the edges). No refusal, no cost: the card is a prize for a route choice already available.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 129 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `part`) Bead curtains in doorways: the bakery's back door and the general store's stockroom door (run 114) get a bead curtain (a `curtain` prop builder in `props3d.gd`: a lintel and twelve strands of paper beads with an ink bead every fourth) that any body walking through parts with the `part` pose (hands up, head bowed, the halves swinging 0.25 s and a rustle of beads instead of leaves); residents on their routines part it the same way; a body carrying a crate parts it with one hand (the `part_k` meta at 0.5 so the off hand holds the crate). Probe: the parting on a routine, the one-handed carry, the halves closing.
- [ ] (motions + Climb, 2026-10-09 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twelve packs and the vine is the first one that saves a fall) Soft landings everywhere: a body caught by the vines, the cloud raft or a rope bridge from a fall over SPLAT (330 px) gets a 0.4 s `relief` beat (the `part`/`wade`/`sway` family — the shoulders drop, a hand goes to the chest, the head tips back once) and a flat line from the loader's `_say` ("That would have hurt."), the floor's sign counts saves per run, and a bot climber saved this way says it too; the three packs expose `saved(on)` for the tick a catch replaced a splat. Probe: the beat fires only above SPLAT, once per catch, and never on a walked-in parting.

## Grows from Dumbwaiter (run 130 — the thirteenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that climbs only as fast as your hands work; deepen it before a fourteenth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 130 — the pack's own next step) Books in the crate: the dumbwaiter's two book blocks are real items — C while standing in the parked crate at the top takes one (`stoop` then carry, the block leaves the crate and the counterweight visibly drops 0.1 m lighter), the floor-15 camp door has a returns slot beside its sign that takes it (C with a book in hand, `put` pose) and stamps the sign "Returned: n"; the crate restocks one block each time it parks at the bottom empty. Probe: take, crate count 2 → 1, counterweight −0.1, return, sign count.
- [ ] (Climb, run 130 — the Climb axis, thinnest at ~116 open lines by keyword) Two-crate dumbwaiter on the long Library shafts: when the two hops are more than 280 px apart a second piece kind `pair` hangs two crates on one rope over the pulley, one at each end — stepping into either makes it the loaded one, its heaves lift it while the other crate descends as the counterweight (a body in the lower crate heaves the upper one down to itself when someone has left it parked above), and a second climber in the shared room can ride the other crate at the same time (positions through `pos.pose = "heave"`). Pack + probe only; the loader's second `lifting` slot is the owner session's.
- [ ] (economy + Climb, run 130 — the economy axis, thinnest at ~133 open lines by keyword) Porter's wage: the Climb prize path in `town_growth.gd` (`prices.json` `prize.porter`, 1 coin) pays once per tower run for riding a dumbwaiter up with a book block from the Library returns item in hand — the pack exposes `carried_up()` (true when a body stepped off at the top holding one); the town's general store (run 114) sells a reading lamp for the player's house (run 117) at 6 coins so the porter's coins have a place to go.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 130 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `heave`) A hand hoist on the lookout terrace (run 73): a beam with a pulley over the terrace edge and a crate on a rope down to the street; the baker's apprentice hauls the morning crate up with `heave` on the 09:00 beat (the crate rises 36 px a pull with the same click and clack, a resident on the terrace unloads two loaves into the terrace table's basket), and the player can take the rope at either end — C steps to the rope, C again is a heave, residents waiting at the top say a line from `minds.json` `hoist`.
- [ ] (Climb + relationships, 2026-10-09 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirteen packs each make a sound, and nobody else hears them) Sounds that carry in the shared room: the dumbwaiter's click and clack, the ladder's clunk, the bell's ring and the vine's rustle are sent as room events (`ev k "snd"` with the pack id and the floor) and played at the other climber's ghost position on every screen in the room, so you hear someone working the floor above you and residents at the camp doors turn their heads toward it (`look` pose). Pack side: each pack exposes `sounds_since(t)` → [{id, x, y}]; the loader and `poz_net` halves are the owner session's.

## Grows from Kite line (run 131 — the fourteenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that carries you on the wind instead of against it; deepen it before a fifteenth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 131 — the pack's own next step) Two kites on one floor's wind: when a Wind cliffs floor with wind has two qualifying downwind pairs, the second kite (`max` 2, the plan already sorts by gap) is pinned higher and flies the longer tether, and a body hanging from the first can catch the second's toggle in passing (Up while hanging, hands within reach of the other toggle) to chain two gaps without touching the hop between — the first kite returns empty; the pack exposes `swap(riding, x, y, z)` returning the new riding dict or empty; probe: two kites planned, swap at the overlap, the first's `rel` set, the second's `held`.
- [ ] (Climb, run 131 — the Climb axis, thinnest at ~116 open lines by keyword) Wind that shows: on floors with `wind_of` the Wind cliffs pack draws what the wind does — a wind sock on a short pole at every camp door of a windy floor (a cloth cone that points downwind and stands out straighter the stronger the wind, 60..180 px/s mapped to 20°..80°), drifting leaves or snow grains that cross the screen at the wind's speed (CPUParticles3D, 12 at a time, GL Compatibility), and a kite on a windless Wind cliffs floor (20..24) hangs limp with its tail straight down until a body catches it — then the cliff's gust picks it up over 0.3 s. Pack + probe only (the sock's angle, the particle count, the limp kite's tail).
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 131 — the quests axis, thinnest at ~129 open lines by keyword) The kite-maker's errand: a resident at the tower plaza's camp door 20 (the first Wind cliffs camp) asks, through `mind.line("kite")` (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`), for a lost kite — one kite per lap (the floor-24 one) has a torn tail bow (`torn` flag in the plan, drawn as a hanging thread) and riding it to the far hop once mends nothing but counts as "flown"; `town_growth.gd`'s odd-jobs board (run 115) gets a card "Fly the torn kite on floor 24" for 2 coins paid at the plaza when `ClimbKite.flown("24.K0")` is true in the saved records (`climb_kites` list); the resident says a second line when paid. Card + line + record only; the pack exposes `flown(id)`.
- [ ] (places + motions, run 131 — the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `kite`) Kites on the meadow: a kite stand by the river meadow (run 68) with three kites on pegs (the pack's builder reused as a prop, `props3d.gd`), where on windy days (`weather` wind > 0) one resident per hour takes a kite out — walks to the meadow's open patch, runs 6 m into the wind with the string (existing run pose, the kite climbing behind), then stands with `kite` (both hands on one line overhead, leaning back against the pull — the same pose read with `kite_v` from the town's wind) for 20..40 s while the kite bobs and the tail waves, then reels it in (`moor` hand-over-hand) and pegs it; the player can take a kite with C and fly it the same way, SPACE lets it go — a let-go kite drifts downwind over the river and the resident on the other bank says `mind.line("lost_kite")`; children (run 102) follow a flying kite with their heads.
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-09 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: fourteen packs and the tower still has one weather, the wind's number) Weather in the tower: `wind_of` becomes a mood the packs read — a gusty floor (one in four windy floors by the seed) has its wind swell and ebb on a 7 s cycle (±40%), so the kite's drift speeds and slows mid-gap (hang_on reads `gust(t)` from the pack), the icicles tremble in the gusts before they fall, the rope bridge (run 121) sways wider on the swell, and the cloud rafts (run 126) drift faster; a shared `climb_weather.gd` helper the packs call with the floor and `_t`, a probe that reads the swell at three times, and a wind sound (a low noise band) that rises with it. Packs + helper + probe only; the loader's `x += wind_of(...) * dt` stays the owner session's.
- [ ] (weather + interactions, 2026-10-09 — wider, from run 131: the kite reads the wind and the town never shows it) A weathervane on the post office roof: a cockerel cut-out on a spindle with N/E/S/W arms (`props3d.gd` builder, paper-and-ink) that turns to the town weather's wind direction with a damped swing (0.6 s lag, a 1 Hz wobble in gusts) and spins idle when the wind is 0; a resident leaving home glances up at it (`look` pose, 0.8 s) and `mind.score` raises the umbrella/stay-in terms when it points from the rain side; the player can C at the post office steps to read it (`say` toast "Wind: from the west. Moderate.") and the task "Check the wind for the kite stand" in the odd-jobs board pays nothing but unlocks the meadow kite item's first loan; probe: vane yaw follows a set wind within 0.6 s, idle spin at 0.

## Grows from Sliding bookcase (run 132 — the fifteenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower you move with your hands to make a jump smaller; deepen it before a sixteenth set-piece)

Card + lines + record only; the pack exposes `shelve(id)` and `shelved(id)`.

## Grows from Star lantern (run 133 — the sixteenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower you climb with your knees, and the first light in it; deepen it before a seventeenth set-piece)

Pack + record + probe only (dark before, lit after, the count saved, the light's range).
- [ ] (interactions with props + Climb, run 133 — the interactions-with-props axis, thinnest at ~25 open lines by keyword) Shaking the pole: C while standing on the foot plate of an empty pole rocks it 0.15 rad for 0.8 s with a new `jog` pose (two hands on the pole at chest height, a hip push on each of three beats — the board's shaking-a-tree sheets), the lantern swings 0.4 rad for 3 s and sheds three paper sparks (CPUParticles3D, the accent colour, 1.2 s); if the floor's icicles hang within 60 px of the pole's tip the shake knocks one down (the icicle pack's own fall); a body from the room on the same floor does the same when it passes the plate (`ev` "jog"). Pack + pose + probe (the rock, the swing, the sparks, the icicle, no effect while someone clings).
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, run 133 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at ~39 open lines by keyword) The lamplighter's cart: a two-wheel hand cart (built from the bookcase's caster code, no `Car3D`) parked at the Climb tower plaza with a stack of paper star lanterns; a lamplighter resident (door job like the baker, from the roster) pushes it down the tower road at dusk (`shunt` at 0.8 speed) and hangs a lantern on each road lamp (`pin`), so the road to Climb glows at night, and takes them down at dawn; the player can take a lantern from the cart (C, `stoop`) and carry it — the first light you carry, a small glow at the hand — and hang it on a lamp (`pin`); `mind.line("lamplighter")` (three lines), an odd-jobs card "Hang the road lanterns" for 2 coins when the player hung three. Probe: at dusk the cart moves and every lamp gets a lantern, the player's hung lanterns count, dawn clears them.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-09 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: sixteen packs and every grab is the same Up in the air; nothing in the tower is boarded from the ground) Grounded mounts: the lantern's rung, the ladder's rails and the bell rope get a second way in — standing beside them and pressing Up with the hands within reach steps the body onto the piece with a 0.3 s `mount` pose (one hand up, a knee lift, the board's climbing-onto sheets) instead of the jump; each pack exposes `mount(x, y, z, on)` returning the same dict as its `grab`, and the loader calls it in the grounded branch where Up now walks in z, only when a piece is in reach (the z-walk stays everywhere else). Packs + pose + probe (the mount from the hop, no z-walk when a piece is reachable, z-walk elsewhere); the loader line is the owner session's.
- [ ] (places + Climb, 2026-10-09 — wider, from run 133: the Starlit floors now have icicles and lanterns and are still the only theme with nothing on its rest floors) The observatory on the Starlit rest floors (30, 65, 100): beside the camp door a brass telescope on a tripod (C: a 2 s `peer` pose — one eye to the eyepiece, a hand on the tube, the other hip cocked; the pack returns the next three floors' hop positions as faint star dots the loader draws for 2 s — the first time the tower tells you what is coming), a star chart on the cliff (`scan`, reading it names the next theme in the banner), a cushion bench (`crossleg`); a body from the room peers when it arrives (`ev` "peer"). A pack (`data/climb/observatory.json`, `climb_observatory.gd`) with its own probe (the dots, the chart's theme, the bench taken).
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-09 — wider, from the run 133 check) Moths at the lit lanterns: once "Lighting the way" ships, each lit lantern draws three moths (a small bird-rig-style flapper — two paper wings on a body bead, a 14 Hz flap, a looping orbit 0.3..0.6 m round the lantern on smoothed seeded noise); a body riding the pole scatters them for 2 s (they flee outward, then drift back), a dark lantern has none; `probe_lantern` counts moths per lit pole (3) and per dark pole (0).

## Grows from Revolving door (run 134 — the seventeenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that keeps moving after you let go of it, and the first set-piece on the first theme; deepen it before an eighteenth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 134 — the pack's own next step) Spin-out: entering a door that is still coasting skips the 0.15 s ease and the half turn runs at the coasting speed plus the push (`angle_at` already knows the speed — store it as `spd` on entry), and on `done` `push` also returns `vx` = dir × the leaf tip's speed (ω × r = 98 px/s at full speed, more on a door pushed twice in a row) with `fling` true; the loader keeps that vx for 0.3 s instead of the walk target so the body comes out of a fast door at a trot and a jump pressed in that window starts with it — the first set-piece that sends you off faster than you came in. Pack + probe only (a coasting door → no ease, the vx on done, nothing on a slow door); the loader's 0.3 s window is the owner session's.
- [ ] (interactions with props + Climb, run 134 — the interactions-with-props axis, thinnest at 1 open line by keyword) Polishing the brass: C while standing still beside a stopped door with the hand within reach of a push bar (not walking into the drum — the ride needs a step toward it) rubs the bar — a `buff` pose (the right hand circling at chest height, the left on the hip, three strokes over 1.2 s, the board's wiping sheets) — and the bars brighten for the lap (albedo toward the Old hall gold, emission 0.3, Compatibility-safe) while the whirr rises 10 Hz on a polished door (`_loop_wav` rebuilt); `town_growth.gd` counts polished doors in the saved records as `climb_polished` and the plaza sign (run 112's signs) lists "Doors polished: n"; a body from the room sees the shine (`ev` "buff"). Pack + pose + record + probe (the strokes, the brightened bar, the pitch, the count, no polish while someone rides).
- [ ] (animals + Climb, run 134 — the animals axis, thinnest at 37 open lines by keyword with vehicles) The hall pigeon: on Old hall floors with a door, a pigeon (the bird rig — bob, peck, flap, hop) roosts on the canopy's lip; a body riding the door whirrs it off — it flaps one loop 1.5 m round the drum over 3 s and lands again on the far side of the lip; a body standing on the canopy makes it hop along the lip away from the feet and peck at the stone; a body walking under it slowly (no C) gets a bob and a two-note coo (`_tick_wav` twice, 310/260 Hz); a pack (`data/climb/pigeon.json`, `climb_pigeon.gd`) reading `door.canopy_at(n)`, with its own probe (the roost, the loop, the hop, the coo, none on a floor without a door).
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: seventeen packs and every one forgets its state when the band is rebuilt; nothing in the tower outlives a fall) Doors remember: the leaves' angle and the coasting state are kept per floor across a lap (`state(n)` the loader hands back to `build` when a band is rebuilt — a door you left spinning is still spinning when you fall back down to that floor) and every ride adds one to the pack's `turns`; a camp-door sign on the next rest floor (run 112's signs) reads "Door turns: n" and `town_growth.gd` saves the lap's count as `climb_turns`. Pack + record + probe (rebuild a band mid-coast and the angle continues, the count survives the rebuild); the `state` hand-back is one loader line, the owner session's.
- [ ] (places + Climb, 2026-10-10 — wider, from run 134: the Old hall is the first theme and its rest floors (5, 40, 75) still have only the camp door) The hall cloakroom on the Old hall rest floors: beside the camp door a row of brass hooks under a shelf with three numbered tags, a stool and a counter bell (`_tick_wav`) — C at the counter with a hat on (run 114's wearables) hangs it on a hook (`doff` exists, a tag goes to the pocket) and C with the tag takes it back; a hat left on a hook is still there next lap (saved records `climb_cloak`); a body from the room sees the hat on the hook (`ev` "cloak"); a pack (`data/climb/cloakroom.json`, `climb_cloakroom.gd`) with its own probe (the hook, the tag, the return, the save, the bell).
- [ ] (weather + Climb, 2026-10-10 — wider, from the run 134 check: the door is the first thing in the tower that moves air) The hall draught: on Old hall floors with a door, three paper scraps (a ticket, a torn programme, a leaf — 10 × 6 px ink-edged quads, seeded per floor) lie on the door's hop; while the drum spins faster than 2.0 rad/s the open arc blows a draught 60 px out of each side for as long as it turns — scraps in it lift 6..14 px, tumble on smoothed seeded noise and skitter downwind at 40 px/s, settle when it stops, and one that leaves the hop's edge drifts down to the floor below and lies there (kept per band like the doors in "Doors remember"); a body standing in the draught gets a 0.4 s `brace` lean already in the library; extend `probe_door` with the lift, the drift and the settle.

## Grows from Hammock net (run 135 — the eighteenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that catches a fall and gives it back as height; deepen it before a nineteenth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 135 — the pack's own next step) Net chain: a body that falls back into a net it was just thrown from (the same net, within 2.5 s of its `let_go`, missed the far hop) lands on a net still carrying its last sag — `catch` adds the new |vy| × 0.07 on top of whatever `sag_at` still holds instead of starting from zero (capped at sag_max 56), so the second SPACE throws higher than the first and a third reaches the full 1037 px/s from a one-hop fall: the hammock as a trampoline for a body that times it. Pack + probe only (the leftover sag, the cap, a late return that gets the plain catch); the net's own `bounce` in `_place` already shows the rebound.
- [ ] (interactions with props + Climb, run 135 — the interactions-with-props axis, thinnest at 2 open lines by keyword) Taking the pillow: C while lying in the net takes the linen pillow into the arms — a `hug` pose (the board's lying sheets with a cushion hugged to the chest: both arms crossed over it, the knees drawn up, the head turned into it) — and a throw with a hugged pillow lands soft once (the landing squat skipped and no splat above SPLAT speed for that one landing, `soft` true on `let_go`), after which the pillow lies where the body landed, can be carried like any two-hand item and set down on any hop's edge where it stays for the lap (`pillow_at(n)` in the pack, kept per band); a net without its pillow sways 20 % more and the next lap puts it back. Pack + pose + probe (the hug, the soft landing, the pillow on a hop, the emptier sway).
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, run 135 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 38 open lines by keyword with animals, which took run 134's item) The garden trolley: on the Hanging garden rest floor (the n % 5 == 0 floor whose theme is the garden — 5, 40, 75 …) a wooden watering trolley (two spoked wheels, a tin tank, a brass tap) on a 300 px rail along the rest slab; C at its handle pushes it at 110 px/s in a `trundle` pose (the board's wheelbarrow sheets: two hands low on the handle, the body leaning into it, short steps, the head up) to the rail's ends where it stops against a buffer with a clank; a body standing on the rail in front of it is nudged along at the trolley's speed (no shove, no fall — `push` returns the vx the loader applies); the tap drips three drops while it moves; a pack (`data/climb/trolley.json`, `climb_trolley.gd`) with its own probe (the rail, the push, the buffer, the nudge, the drips).
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: eighteen packs with their own sounds and not one sound tells the player *when* — the throw height depends on timing and only the eye can read the sag) Creak that tells the time: the net's creak is rebuilt per catch at creak_hz + sag × 2 Hz (a deep fall groans lower, `_tick_wav` with the pitch) and a second, softer creak plays at the deepest point (sag_s after the catch — the moment a SPACE throws highest), then one short rope tick every 0.5 s while the sag relaxes so the ear hears the throw draining away; on the wind floors (40+) the empty net's swing adds a rope whisper at the swing's ends (`_rustle_wav` at 600 Hz, volume with the wind). Pack + probe (the pitch per sag, the second creak at 0.4 s, the ticks, silence at rest).
- [ ] (places + Climb, 2026-10-10 — wider, from run 135: the Hanging garden has a curtain, nets and vents but no place a person made) The potting shed on the Hanging garden rest floor: beside the camp door a lean-to of three posts and a moss roof over a bench with three clay pots, a watering can on a hook and a seed packet on a nail — C with the can over a pot waters it (`water` pose exists; the can tilts, four drops) and the pot shows a sprout next lap, a leaf the lap after, a flower the third (saved records `climb_pots`, the gardener's own `prices.json` sale: a flower pot sells for 2 coins at the plaza counter); a body from the room sees the sprout (`ev` "pot"); a pack (`data/climb/shed.json`, `climb_shed.gd`) with its own probe (the shed, the watering, the three stages across laps, the sale).
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-10 — wider, from the run 135 check: a body in the net has one thing to do with its hands and the board's lying sheets have five) Lying is a family: while lying in the net a tap of Left/Right rolls the body onto its side (`curl` — the knees up, both hands under the cheek, the net's sway read through the hip), Up sits it up on the edge with the legs over (`perch` — hands on the rungs, feet swinging, the head turned to the far hop) from which SPACE is a plain hop to that hop with no throw, and a body left lying 8 s yawns and lets the hanging arm swing (`doze` — the yawn from the idle fidgets, the arm at 0.4 Hz); each a `pose_request` with its own timing, written by the pack through `cling` (`loll_pose` meta), each with a probe line.
- [ ] (interactions with props + Climb, 2026-10-10 — wider, from the run 135 ship check: the net catches bodies but nothing else, and the tower already drops things — run 114's hats knocked off on a splat, the pillow above) The net keeps what falls: a loose item (a hat lost in a splat, a dropped pillow, a paper scrap from the hall draught) whose fall crosses a net's box stops on the net's line instead of the floor below — it rocks with the net's sway, dips with a body's sag and is thrown up with it (the same throw ×0.6, landing where the arc meets a hop or back in the net); a body lying in the net reaches it with C (`loll` arm to the item, 0.4 s) and holds it as usual; an item left in a net is still there next lap (saved records `climb_netted`, at most three per net, the oldest drops through). Pack + probe only (an item over the box stops, one beside it falls past, the throw-up arc, the reach, the cap of three).

## Grows from Weathervane (run 136 — the nineteenth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that turns about a post and carries you out in front of the cliff; deepen it before a twentieth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 136 — the pack's own next step) Riding the return: a body that grabs the ribbon while the vane is winding back (today refused for 1.5 s) rides the return swing to the lower hop instead — `grab` accepts during the return with `dir` flipped and `hang_on` plays the arc backwards from the current angle (the remaining angle's share of turn_s), setting the body down on the lower hop's top; a body still hanging when a second grab comes is untouched (the held refusal stays). Plain outcome untouched: an empty, home vane behaves exactly as today. Pack + probe only (the grab mid-return, the backward arc, the set-down on the lower hop, the `rtheta` it starts from).
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, run 136 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 81 open lines by keyword) The sail-cart: on Wind cliffs rest floors (20, 55, 90 …) a wooden box cart with a square sail on a mast runs a 320 px rail along the rest slab, the floor's wind (or the cliff's own gust below 40) filling the sail; C at its tail puts the body standing in the box (`ride` pose exists) and the cart runs downwind at a speed that reads the wind (60..180 px/s → 90..220, 70 in still air) to the rail's buffer, where the sail luffs and the cart stops with a clank; pushing it back upwind is a `shunt` walk at 60 px/s against the sail; a bot climber from the room rides it when heading that way. Pack (`data/climb/sailcart.json`, `climb_sailcart.gd`) + probe (the speed per wind, the buffer, the push back, the luff, the ride pose).
- [ ] (relationships + Climb, run 136 — the relationships axis, second-thinnest at 95 open lines by keyword) Two on the vane: a second body (the room's others, or a bot climber) that grabs the ribbon while one hangs (today `grab` refuses a held vane) takes the ribbon's other side — the spindle takes 0.4 s instead of 0.25 and the half-turn runs 20 % slower under two; when both are set down on the far hop the two faces turn to each other for 0.6 s (`nod` from the greeting family) and bot climbers call `mind.befriend()` with `mind.line("vane_share")` (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`, e.g. "Shared a weathervane. The forecast held."); a body that lets go mid-turn leaves the other to finish at the single-body speed. Pack + probe (the second grab, the slower turn, the nod, the line, the single-body speed after one lets go).
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: three Wind cliffs packs read `wind_of` as one number and nothing on a floor shows the wind before a body commits to it) The vane tells the wind: an empty vane's fin on a windy floor rests pointing downwind (today it rests back over the lower hop regardless) and quivers in proportion, so a body on the hop reads the floor's wind before the kite or the vane carries it; on a windless floor it drifts slowly round the post (0.1 rad/s) until a body grabs it, when the cliff's gust takes over; the kite's limp tail (the "Wind that shows" item under Kite line) and the icicles' tremble read the same gust through one `climb_weather.gd` helper (`gust(n, t)`) the packs share. Pack + probe (the rest angle per wind sign, the drift, the helper's value at three times, the vane still carrying a body on a windless floor).
- [ ] (places + Climb, 2026-10-10 — wider, from run 136: the Wind cliffs have a kite, icicles and a vane and no place a person made) The signal hut on the Wind cliffs rest floor: beside the camp door a stone hut with a semaphore mast — two painted arms on a post that a body sets with C (four positions, a `haul` on the halyard for each) to spell the floor's wind for the room (the arms go out as an `ev`; others' huts show the same), a brass telescope on a tripod (C: a 2 s `peer` pose — one eye to the glass, the other hand shading — while the camera zooms 1.5× toward the next floor), and a wind log on the wall that keeps the strongest wind a body ever rode a kite or a vane in (saved records `climb_wind_best`, shown on the camp sign). Pack (`data/climb/hut.json`, `climb_hut.gd`) + probe (the arms and their ev, the peer zoom, the log).
- [ ] (motions + Climb, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the run 136 check: the `trail` set-down is one plain landing whatever the speed the arc carried) The vane's landing reads its speed: a set-down from a fast half-turn (the wind-assisted 0.91 s turns, speed over 200 px/s) runs out in two skipping steps along the far hop (`skid` — the trailing hand still up as if holding the ribbon, the heels scuffing, 0.5 s, the body slides 18 px) while a slow still-air turn keeps today's plain set; a skid that runs past the hop's edge is the loader's own fall, so the vane only plans skids on hops with 40 px to spare. Pack + `stick3d_trail.gd` + probe (the speed gate, the 18 px, the edge refusal, the pose's three phases).

## Grows from Diving plank (run 137 — the twentieth Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower you load with your own feet, and the first spot where SPACE means something other than the charge jump; deepen it before a twenty-first set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 137 — the pack's own next step) On the beat: a tap that lands while the tip is on its way up (`tip_dy` rising — the second half of the previous tap's 0.35 s) counts double, the diver's rhythm, so two well-timed taps fling on the third and a tap on the way down counts once; the creak's pitch climbs with the load (`creak_hz` × (1 + 0.15 × load)) so the beat is heard before it is seen; `probe_plank` gets a timed pair at 2.0 and 2.25 that flings on the third.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, run 137 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 26 open lines by keyword) A walk-off is a dive: a body that walks past the tip instead of tapping (today a plain fall) gets `bound`'s pike for the fall with no stretch — `stand` returns `off` with `dive` true when the last tick was in the tip zone and the board was pressed — and lands the usual way; a fall from anywhere else on the board stays a plain fall. One reactive variation on walking off; the plain outcome (a fall) is unchanged in every case, only the pose differs.
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, run 137 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 41 open lines by keyword) The cloud lift: on Cloud sea rest floors (25, 60, 95 …) a wicker gondola on a cable from the rest slab to the band's top ledge, wound by a resident liftkeeper at a crank (`grind` pose) who winds only when a body sits in the gondola and has paid the `prices.json` `lift` fare at the slab's post (`town_growth.can_pay`; the earning path is the floor's Climb prize) — the first ride in the tower that costs and the first tower job; the ladder, the rope and the hops beside it stay free; `probe_lift`.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty packs and every flight pose ends in the same `squash` landing the loader draws) The landing reads the height: a `bound` or `bounce` body that comes down more than 200 px (the loader's `apex − y`) brace-lands into a forward roll (`roll` pose: 0.4 s the shoulders tuck and the body turns once over along the hop, ending in a crouch facing the way it came) instead of the splat at 330 px; the splat threshold is untouched — this is the window between 200 and 330 — and the bot climbers, when they come, roll the same.
- [ ] (places + motions + relationships, 2026-10-10 — wider, and the motion-library rule's "a use in the town" for `bound`) The lake springboard: at the lakeside jetty (run 91's fishing spot) a plank clamped to the jetty's end over the water, built by `ClimbPlank` in town metres like the play-mushroom item — walk to the tip, three taps and the fourth throws the body into the lake (`bound`, then the existing `swim`, a splash ring and a `bound_splash` meta for the entry), out at the jetty ladder; two residents with a `mind.score` term "swim" use it on warm afternoons through `mind.befriend()` (one dives, one watches and says `mind.line("dive")`), and a dive within 2 m of a fishing resident draws `mind.line("scared_fish")`.

- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the run 137 check: a plank's fling always lands on the one far hop the plan picked) The plank aims: the fourth tap held with ←/→ for the last 0.35 s tilts the fling ±15° (`vx` 300 → 220..380, `vy` kept), the `bound` stretch leaning the arms the same way, so a good diver can reach the hop past the far hop when the floor has one within the arc; the plan records that second target only if no other platform cuts the longer arc; `probe_plank` flings left, centre and right and lands the right-hand one on the second hop.

## Grows from Hot-air balloon (run 138 — the twenty-first Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower the wind carries while you make it climb, and the first set-piece where the player's hand feeds a fire instead of pulling the body; deepen it before a twenty-second set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 138 — the pack's own next step) Ballast: a sandbag on a hook on the basket's rim (drawn at the gap side), and Down held 0.5 s in the basket tips it out — a `tug_tip` meta the pose reads as the rim hand swinging out and the body leaning over the side — so the balloon rises one `lift` on its own with no tug and the breeze carries it the same, saving one tug on the three-tug climbs; the bag drops like the icicles (gone 280 px down, nothing it hits), the balloon comes home with a fresh bag after its sink, and `probe_balloon` tips at 2.0 and reads +50 at 2.8 with no tug.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, run 138 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 28 open lines by keyword) The long burn: a tug that lands while the flame is still at full tongue (the first 0.3 s of a rise) is a long burn — lift 1.5 ×, hold 2 × hold_s, a 0.9 s whoosh from a second `_whoosh_wav` and the flame stays tall — while a tug after the hold has lapsed is the plain tug, so a rider who keeps the rhythm climbs in two long burns where a hesitant one needs three; one reactive variation on the tug, and any tug outside that 0.3 s window is the plain tug (plain stays the likelier outcome: the window is 0.3 of a 1.4 s cycle).
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, run 138 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 42 open lines by keyword) The tethered balloon in town: at the lookout terrace (run 73's second level) a moored balloon on a 12 m tether built by `ClimbBalloon` in town metres like the play-mushroom item — step in, tug with the same `tug` pose and it rises along the tether to a view over the square, the breeze leaning it 2 m toward the river; Down brings it home; two residents with a `mind.score` term "view" ride it on clear evenings through `mind.befriend()` (one tugs, one points over the rim and says `mind.line("view")`), and a child (run 102) is lifted in by a parent — the first vehicle in town that goes up; `probe_tether`.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-one packs and every ride pose starts from a snap at the end of a walk) Stepping in has its own motion (the `board` pose shipped on run 139 with the paternoster — step in, ride, step out, in `stick3d_board.gd`; this item is now the wiring of the other rides through it): the balloon's `mount_s` step, the dumbwaiter's crate, the rolling ladder's first rung and the cloud lift's gondola all cut from the walk straight to the ride pose — a `board` pose (0.25 s: one hand on the rim, the near knee lifts over it, the body pivots on the far foot and both feet settle in; the reverse on stepping out) that every ride pack calls through a shared `RidePoses.board(fig, k)` during its mount window, so the step reads the same into every vehicle in the tower; the bots, when they come, step the same.
- [ ] (places + weather + Climb, 2026-10-10 — wider: Cloud sea floors now hold five packs on hop gaps and nothing in the sky itself) Weather in the tower: Cloud sea floors above 60 get a passing cloudbank — a soft white slab drifting across the band at the floor's wind speed every 20 s that veils the hops behind it for 2 s (a material fade, no collision) and leans the moored balloon's envelope 10 px with it (`env.rotation.z`), and Starlit floors get a shooting star every 15 s that no mechanic reads; the first weather in the tower, read by the balloon so the sky and the set-piece agree; `probe_sky`.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-10 — deeper, from the run 138 check: the basket takes a body and a tug with no weight in it) The basket answers weight: stepping in dips the basket 6 px over 0.15 s and rocks the envelope 0.08 rad, settling over 0.8 s, with the mooring line going taut and a creak; each tug's whoosh kicks the basket 3 px down before the lift (the burner pushes the floor), and stepping out at the far hop lets it bob up 8 px empty; the `tug` pose's rim hand follows the dip so the hand stays on the rim; `probe_balloon` reads the dip 6.00 at mount + 0.15 and the rock under 0.01 rad at mount + 0.8.

## Grows from Paternoster (run 139 — the twenty-second Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that never stops and the first surface you walk into and out of instead of grabbing; deepen it before a twenty-third set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 139 — the pack's own next step) The library's returns ride the lift: every fourth cabin carries a book crate (a prop with its verbs — C picks it up, a two-hand `carry`, Down sets it down on any floor), and the highest serving hop gets a brass returns chute on the back wall; posting the crate (C with the crate within 20 px of the chute) is a thud, the crate reappears on an empty cabin after one lap, and the loader pays the chute's `prize` from `prices.json` once per floor per visit — the first paid errand in the tower under "Money is real" (entry free, coins for a job done); `probe_paternoster` reads the crate on cabins 0/4/8, the carry, the post and the single payment.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 139 — the quests axis, thinnest at 98 open lines by keyword) An odd-jobs card for the lift (run 115's board): "Library lift — ride a paternoster out onto a hop above the one you entered" with a 3-coin reward from `prices.json`; the loader counts a ride as `records.climb_lifts` (caught at serving hop A's level, stepped off at a higher serving hop of the same chain) and the card completes on the next town visit; the giver is the general store's keeper (run 114) until there is a librarian; `probe_notice` sees the card and its completion from a fake record.
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, run 139 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 102 open lines by keyword) The town's own paternoster: a two-cabin chain from the square's edge up to the lookout terrace (run 73's second level) built by `ClimbPaternoster` in town metres like the play-mushroom item — the same cabins, rails and sprockets, 1.2 m/s — that the player walks into with the `board` pose and out of at the terrace; residents with a `mind.score` term "view" ride up in the evening and down after, a child (run 102) rides only with a parent in the same cabin; the first lift in town and the first ride that never waits for you; `probe_lift` counts two residents up and down in a 60 s evening.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-11 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-two packs and the hop's edge is still a bare step) The sill: serving hops get a brass threshold plate on their lane edge (drawn, 6 px high) and `pn.sill(on, x)` is true within 14 px of it, so the loader sets `pose_request = "board"` one tick before the catch and the knee lifts over the sill before the floor arrives instead of after; on the way out `board_out` begins 6 px earlier when a serving hop's top is level with the floor (a step, not a fall); `probe_paternoster` reads `sill` true at 14 px from the edge and the out meta 0.3 at the plate.
- [ ] (jobs + places + Climb, 2026-10-11 — wider: the tower has twenty-two set-pieces and nobody works there) The engine room: under every chain's bottom sprocket a brass-and-iron engine on the back wall — a flywheel turning at the chain's speed, a governor whose balls lift with it, a steam pipe that puffs once a lap — and the tower's first worker, an engineer resident standing at the engine on the lowest serving hop's lane side who oils it with an `oil` pose (a can tipped at the flywheel's axle, the other hand on the hip, a wipe of the brow each lap) and says `mind.line("engine")` when the player steps out beside him; the first job in the tower and the first resident outside town; `probe_engine` reads the flywheel's angle against the chain's and the engineer's pose.

## Grows from Zip line (run 140 — the twenty-third Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that takes you down on purpose and the first ride whose speed is the slope's; deepen it before a twenty-fourth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 140 — the pack's own next step) A second rider: the trolley's bar is wide enough for two — a co-climber (run 139's `poz_net` shove partner) who presses Up under a *held* trolley within reach catches the bar's other half and rides the same slide; `ride` takes a `riders` count, the cable sags 16 px for two instead of 10, the brake zone begins 12 px earlier for the weight, the pose gets `zip_pair` ±1 (the hands shift to one half of the bar, the shoulder toward the partner drops) and both bodies are set down a body's width apart on the lower hop; `probe_zip` reads the deeper sag, the earlier brake and the two landing spots.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, run 140 — the quests axis, thinnest at 90 open lines by keyword) An odd-jobs card for the zip line (run 115's board): "Garden run — ride a zip line down and climb back past its upper post without a splat" with a 3-coin reward from `prices.json`; the loader counts `records.climb_zips` (a `ride` that ends `done`, then a landing on the upper hop or higher within 20 s with `hurt` never set), the card completes on the next town visit, the giver is the general store's keeper (run 114) until there is a gardener; `probe_notice` sees the card and its completion from a fake record.
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, run 140 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 102 open lines by keyword) The town's own zip line: from the lookout terrace (run 73's second level) down across the river to the meadow bank, built by `ClimbZip` in town metres like the play-mushroom item — the same posts, cable, trolley, bumper and winch, G × sin × 0.35 capped at 6 m/s — that the player catches with a jump at the terrace post (the `zip` pose) and is set down from on the bank; residents with a `mind.score` term "thrill" (curious, young) queue at the post in the afternoon and ride one at a time while the winch hauls the trolley back, a child (run 102) only after a parent has gone down; the first way off the terrace that is not the stairs; `probe_zipline` counts three residents down in a 90 s afternoon.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-11 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-three packs and the landing from every ride is still a snap to standing) The dismount: `ride` keeps the body's horizontal speed at the bumper (60 px/s along the slope) in the done tick's `vx` and the loader uses it, so the body steps off walking instead of stopping dead; `stick3d_zip.gd` gets a `zip_out` meta (0..1 over 0.3 s after the set-down: the hands drop from the bar one after the other and the body takes one short step the slide's way, the trailing arm swinging) that the loader drives from `cz.dismount(fig, dt)` until it returns true; `probe_zip` reads the kept `vx` and the staggered hand drop.
- [ ] (places + Climb, 2026-10-11 — wider: the tower has twenty-three set-pieces and the Hanging garden floors still have no garden) The garden floor: on every Hanging garden floor the widest still hop gets a planter box along its back edge (drawn, 12 px deep, never under the vine curtain's 56 px) with three crops that grow one stage per rest-floor visit (seedling → leaf → fruit, saved in `records.garden`), a watering can on a hook at the band's rest floor (C picks it up, a two-hand `carry`, C at a planter pours with the town's `water` pose and advances a stage), and ripe fruit (C picks, the town's `eat` pose, restores 0.3 stamina — the tower's first food under "Money is real": free, but only for a body that climbed with the can); `probe_garden` reads the planter placement against the curtain, the stage per visit, the pour and the stamina.

## Grows from Trapeze (run 141 — the twenty-fourth Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower whose beat the body sets: a still bar you catch, pump and leave when you choose; deepen it before a twenty-fifth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 141 — the pack's own next step) The catcher: a second bar hung from the same beam's far ring over the upper hop's far gap when that gap fits too (the plan takes a `pair` flag; two trapezes in one band only this way), still until the first bar has swung; a let-go within 0.15 s of the first bar's far end with Up held catches the second mid-flight (`grab` learns a moving bar: the hands within reach of the bar's current place, the body's velocity folded into the second swing at `catch_k`) — the flying return; `fly` gets a `fly_reach` meta (the arms stretch ahead and the legs trail straight for the flight between bars, no tuck); `probe_trapeze` reads the pair's plan, the window and the folded velocity.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-11 — the animals axis, thinnest at 50 open lines by keyword) The owl on the beam: every Starlit trapeze beam gets a small owl at its cliff end (`bird3d.gd` rig — bob, peck, flap, hop — plus a head turn of ±1.2 rad and a blink) that turns its head to follow a hanging body, hops to the far ring when the swing passes halfway, and flaps to the next floor's beam when a body lets go within 2 m of it (back after 20 s); co-climber ghosts get the same head-follow; `probe_owl` reads the head angle against the bar's angle, the hop and the flight.
- [ ] (relationships + Climb, 2026-10-11 — the relationships axis, second-thinnest at 90 open lines by keyword) The spotter: a co-climber (run 139's `poz_net` partner) standing on the lower hop under a held trapeze within 2 m gets a `spot` pose (both arms half up, the knees soft, the body tracking the bar's x), and if the hanging body drops (Down, or stamina out) with a spotter under it the fall is a `catch` into both bodies' arms — the dropper set down on the hop with no splat and `hurt` never set, both say a line; the loader sends `ev spot` so spectators see it; `mind.befriend()` for bots who spot; `probe_spot` reads the pose's tracking and the no-splat landing.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-11 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-four packs and the let-go is still the same hold jump from every bar) The timed release: letting go of the trapeze within 0.1 s of the far end (`fly_e` over 0.95) adds a `flip` — a 0.5 s forward somersault in the air (the tuck rolls the pelvis a full turn, the arms stay wrapped, the head leads) that lands as the plain landing if the turn has finished and as a `lurch` if it has not (a late release lands feet-first, an early one on the back: `hurt` 0.4, the pendulum's stumble, never a splat); the Climb record counts flips landed; `probe_trapeze` reads the window and both landings.
- [ ] (places + Climb, 2026-10-11 — wider: the tower has twenty-four set-pieces and the Starlit floors are still only a colour) The observatory floor: the rest floor at the top of every Starlit band (34, 69, 104 …) gets a brass telescope on a tripod at the rest hop's cliff end (C: the `peer` pose — one eye to the eyepiece, the other hand on the tube, 2 s; the camera pulls to the night sky and one of twelve named stars, seeded by the floor, glows and is written to `records.stars`), a star chart pinned to the cliff that fills in each star seen, and a resident-written line on the chart for the twelfth; the first thing in the tower that is looked at rather than climbed; `probe_observatory` reads the telescope placement, the twelve names and the record.

## Grows from Cuckoo perch (run 142 — the twenty-fifth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that is a platform only when the clock says so: a hatch that slides a perch out on the gears' beat, draws it back with you on it and lets you out next beat; deepen it before a twenty-sixth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 142 — the pack's own next step) The cuckoo itself: the bird on the plank's tip gets the small bird rig (`bird3d.gd` — bob, peck, flap, hop, a head turn of ±1.2 rad and a blink) and calls with the two notes (the beak opens on each), turns its head to a body that lands beside it, hops onto the body's shoulder when the perch draws the body in (a `perch_bird` meta so the pose's head tilts away from it) and back to the plank's tip at the next opening; co-climber ghosts get the same hop; `probe_perch` reads the beak, the head turn and the hop.
- [ ] (vehicles + town, 2026-10-11 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 86 open lines by keyword) The clockmaker's hand-cart: a two-wheeled hand-cart built in `props3d.gd` (two spoked wheels, a bed, two handles, a cuckoo clock in a crate) parked by the general store; a resident clockmaker (door job like the baker) pushes it (`push` pose, the cart rolls at walking speed on the pavement and the tower-road gravel — never in the main road's lanes z 0.2..3.8) to the newest finished house on the tower road and hangs the clock by the door (`hammer` pose, two taps); the player can take the handles (C — the cart follows at walking speed with the player's own `push` pose, and a resident in front steps aside) and the hung clock calls on the town hour with the perch pack's two-note wav, its doors opening on the same beat; `probe_cart` reads the path, the lane rule and the hung clock.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-11 — the quests axis, second-thinnest at 89 open lines by keyword) The clockkeeper's errand, as a pack (`climb_keeper.gd`, wired by the loader like the signs): a keeper resident stands by the lamp on every Clockwork rest floor (15, 50, 85 …) and asks "see if the cuckoo still keeps time" — ride a perch in and out once on the band above (the pack counts a full ride when `r["inside"]` flips back to false with the body still on and writes `records.perch_rides`); back on the rest floor C at the keeper pays the small prize from `prices.json` (`perch_errand`) once per band, the keeper says a line and winds a pocket watch (`wind` pose: one hand turning a crown at the chest, the head bent to listen); co-climber bots do the errand too; `probe_keeper` reads the count, the single payment and the pose.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-11 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-five packs and no two set-pieces keep time together) The clockwork pair: on a floor that has both a pendulum and a perch, the pendulum's `phase` is set from the perch's beat so the weight passes the end nearest the hatch exactly when the doors open (pendulum.json gets a `couple` flag; `cp.plan` takes the perch's plan as a hint and reads its hx); a body standing on the perch when the weight arrives can press ↑ straight onto the bar (`grab` learns a standing reach from the plank, hands at shoulder height — the `dangle` catch from a crouch, the body uncoiling over 0.2 s) — the first two set-pieces that hand a body to each other; `probe_pendulum` reads the coupled phase and the hand-off.
- [ ] (places + Climb, 2026-10-11 — wider: the Clockwork floors have a belt, a weight and a hatch and still not one room) The clock room: the rest floor of every Clockwork band (15, 50, 85 …) gets an enterable room behind the rest hop — a second door in the cliff beside the camp door (C: a 0.6 s step-in like the camp door's scene, the camera cuts to a 6 × 4 m interior) holding the tower's great escapement (a 2 m wheel, a swinging anchor, the rod of the floor's pendulum going down through the floor), a bench, and a wall of six small cuckoo hatches that all open on the beat; the first interior inside the tower, and the place the clockkeeper's errand (above) starts from; `probe_clockroom` reads the door, the interior node and the beat.

## Grows from Laundry line (run 143 — the twenty-sixth Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that is caught anywhere along its length and the first obstacle that is soft and comes along with you; deepen it before a twenty-seventh set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 143 — the pack's own next step) The laundress: a resident on every laundry-line floor's lower hop (seeded like the signs' keepers, `climb_laundry.gd` builds her from `Stick3D` beside the lower post) who re-pegs a dropped sheet herself — she walks to the heap within 2 s of it landing, lifts it (`stoop`), carries it to the post and pegs it with a `peg` pose (both hands at head height on the rope, a pinch, a step sideways, three times for three pegs) over 2.5 s, so `repeg_s` becomes her walk rather than a timer; while a body shimmies past her she shakes the next sheet out (the flap amplitude doubles for 1 s, `shimmy_sheet` rises to 1 even outside the sheet's 30 px) and says one of three `minds.json` "laundry" lines above her head ("Mind the whites."); the pose has its own beat so a town resident can use it too (see the places item below).
- [ ] (vehicles + town, 2026-10-12 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 91 open lines by keyword) The laundry cart: a two-wheeled basket cart in `props3d.gd` (two spoked wheels, a wicker tub, one push bar) that a resident launderer (door job like the baker, at the north lane's end house) pushes with the `push` pose from house to house on clear mornings (the town clock's 0.3..0.45), stopping at each door for 4 s while the resident there hands over a sheet (`pass` pose, a folded sheet item) and walks back with it full to the washing lines of the places item; the cart rolls at walking speed on the pavement only, never in the road band z −0.1..4.1, and a player who presses C on its bar takes over the round (the launderer walks behind, `mind.line("cart")`), earning 1 coin per door from `prices.json`'s new `laundry` wage; the player can also take one sheet out of the tub (carry, throw, wear over the head as the ghost of the hood pose) — the resident says a line and takes it back at the next door.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the animals axis, second-thinnest at 94 open lines by keyword) The sparrow on the line: every laundry line gets a sparrow on the rope at the upper post (`bird3d.gd` rig — bob, peck, flap, hop, a head turn of ±1.2 rad and a blink) that hops along the rope ahead of a hanging body, one 8 px hop every time the hands advance a stride, keeping 24 px ahead, lands on each peg as it passes and pecks it (a 0.2 s peck, the peg's peg_s gets 0.3 s shorter for that crossing — the bird loosens it), and flies off the post to the next floor's line when the body reaches the end or lets go; it comes back over 3 s when the line is empty; with a popped sheet on the body's head the sparrow lands on the hood and rides (a `shimmy_bird` meta so the pose's head tilts under the weight).
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-12 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-six packs and the sheets flap on their own clock, not the tower's wind) The wind on the line: `ride` takes the loader's `wind_of(n)` (the kite's and the vane's wind, already per floor); the sheets flap toward the wind's side (`flap_amp` × (1 + |wind|), the pivot's rest angle 0.3 × wind), a body under a sheet is halved only against the wind and pushed 20 px/s along with it (the first time the tower's weather helps instead of lifting or dragging), and a strong wind (|wind| > 0.6) pops a peg after 1.0 s instead of 1.5 — written as `peg_s × (1 − 0.33 × |wind|)` in the log line with the arithmetic; the probe runs the 38-tick crossing again at wind 0.8 and wants the plain crossing 0.5 s shorter.
- [ ] (places + town, 2026-10-12 — wider: the tower has a laundry line and the town has no washing) The town's own washing lines: every house on the north lane (runs 66–103) gets a line between two posts in its back strip (`ClimbLaundry` in town metres like the play-mushroom item, 3 m long, 1.9 m high, two sheets), the resident who lives there hangs washing on clear mornings (the `peg` pose from the laundress item, three pegs) and takes it in at dusk and when rain begins (`town_weather`'s first drops — the first thing in town that the rain makes a resident hurry for, with the `storm` dash); the player can shimmy along any line with a jump and Up (the same `shimmy`, the same pop — a popped sheet is the owner's `mind.line("laundry")` and a `mind.score` "annoyed" term for the day), take a sheet down (carry, give back at the door for a "thanks" and a friend point, or wear over the head for the hood pose's blind walk — the first costume), and a resident who passes a line with no washing on it says the deadpan "Dry day." line; the chimney smoke (run 77) and the washing together make a house readable from the street: someone is home, and it is a clear day.

## Grows from Drawbridge (run 144 — the twenty-seventh Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower you work from the hop before you use it, and the first bridge that takes itself away under your feet; deepen it before a twenty-eighth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 144 — the pack's own next step) The gatekeeper: a resident on every drawbridge floor's lower hop (seeded like the signs' keepers, `climb_drawbridge.gd` builds him from `Stick3D` behind the windlass) who cranks the plank down himself when a body stands at the hop's gap edge for 2 s looking across (`crank` with its own `crank_ph`, the same 1.5 s), says one of three `minds.json` "bridge" lines above his head ("Mind the counterweight.") and holds the drum while a body is on the deck — `hold_s` becomes his grip instead of a timer, let go 1 s after the deck empties; a body that cranks beside him gets the plank down in half the time (two pairs of hands on one handle — the first shared verb in the tower).
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the vehicles axis, thinnest by keyword over open lines) The windlass cart: on Old hall rest floors (5, 35, 70 …) a hand-cart with a spare drum and plank stands by the camp door (`props3d.gd`: two spoked wheels, a crate, one pull bar); C on its bar pulls it along the rest floor at walking speed with a `pull` pose (one hand behind on the bar, the body leaning away from it, short steps) and parking it at the rest floor's gap edge makes that edge a drawbridge for the lap (the pack's `plan` gains a `carted` entry the loader builds like the others) — the first tool you carry to put a set-piece where there was none; the cart rolls back to the door when the floor is unbuilt.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the animals axis, second-thinnest by keyword) The pigeon on the drum: every windlass gets a pigeon (`bird3d.gd` rig — bob, peck, flap, hop, a head turn and a blink) perched on the drum that walks the turning drum like a treadmill while a body cranks (one step per ratchet click, against the turn), flaps off when the plank thuds flat and lands on the plank's tip, rides the rising plank up and hops back to the drum when it stands; a body that cranks with the pigeon on the drum gets a `crank_bird` meta so the head glances at it between turns, and a sliding body passes under it as it lifts its wings.
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-12 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-seven packs, and the plank's fall and rise run on a clock, never on the tower's wind) The wind on the plank: `k_at` takes the loader's `wind_of(n)`; a wind blowing into the upright plank leans it 0.1 rad per unit of wind before any crank, lowers it 0.5 s faster when it blows with the crank and 0.5 s slower against it (lower_s × (1 ∓ 0.33 × |wind|), the arithmetic in the log line); a strong wind (|wind| > 0.6) rocks the flat deck so `stand` nudges a standing body 2 px/s along it with the wind and the pose reads `crank_slide` 0.3 for the sway; the probe runs the 24 crank ticks again at wind 0.8 and wants the flat tick eight ticks earlier.
- [ ] (places + town, 2026-10-12 — wider: the tower has a drawbridge and the town's river has only the stone bridge and the stepping stones) The river's drawbridge: a third crossing at the river's upstream bend (runs 68 and 84 — `town_build` gets `_drawbridge(at, yaw)` from the pack's builders in town metres: a 3 m plank hinged on the near bank, a windlass post with a stone counterweight, a bridgekeeper's hut), kept by a bridgekeeper resident (door job like the baker, `minds.json` "bridge" lines) who lowers it with `crank` when a resident waits on the bank (a `mind.score` "cross" term for residents whose routine lies over the river, not a fixed roll) and raises it when the punt (run 78) comes upstream so the punt passes under — the first thing in town that gives way to a boat; the player can crank it with C, a body on it when the keeper raises it slides back with the pack's slide mode (no hurt, the keeper's line), and residents who used the stepping stones take the plank in the rain (the shelter term).

## Grows from Scroll rack (run 145 — the twenty-eighth Climb content pack, the first bridge in the tower you make with your own hands and lose under your own feet, and the first paper that is a floor; deepen it before a twenty-ninth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 145 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the pull) The librarian: a resident on every scroll-rack floor's lower hop (seeded like the signs' keepers, `climb_scroll.gd` builds her from `Stick3D` beside the rack) who pulls the chart herself when a body stands at the hop's gap edge for 2 s looking across (`unroll` with the same `unroll_k` meta, the same 0.8 s), says a `minds.json` line as it flops ("Mind the paper."), and after a tear tuts, picks the torn roll off the rack (`shelve`, run 99) and sets a fresh one — the rack is ready at once instead of after `roll_s` when she is there; she never pulls for a body that is already on the paper, and the plain jump across the gap stays the body's own.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the quests axis, thinnest by keyword over open lines) The chart errand: the torn far piece does not only vanish — it lands as a `scrap` item on whatever caught the body (pick up, carry, give, bin; `stoop`, run 107), and the librarian above asks for it back ("Bring me the piece you took with you.") as an odd-jobs card (run 115) paid at the ledger table (run 112) with the `prices.json` prize; handing it over lets her tape it (`sew`, run 82's stitch pose over paper) and that chart tears 1 s later than new ones for the rest of the session — the first quest the tower itself sets.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the interactions axis, second-thinnest) Reading the chart: C while standing on the open chart (`scan`, run 100) draws the floor on the paper for 3 s — the next two hops as ink rectangles to scale, the taken pieces as small icons (a ladder, a crate, a case, a chain), the camp door as a square — the first thing in the tower that tells you where to go; the ink marks the pack already draws become that drawing's frame; the librarian reads it too when she has nothing to pull.
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-12 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-eight packs and every one of them runs on its own clock — the plank's hold, the chart's idle, the door's spin — and none of them on the tower's weather) Paper and wind: `hold_s` and `idle_s` read the loader's `wind_of(n)` and rain — on a rain floor the wet chart tears after 1 s and leaves no scrap (pulp), on a windy floor the open chart flutters (page.rotation.z ±0.05 on the wind's beat) and its far end lifts 4 px so the step onto it is a step up; the drawbridge's wind item (run 144, above) is the same thought for the plank — build both in one run on one `weather_of(n)` read so the two packs never disagree about the weather.

## Grows from Seesaw planter (run 146 — the twenty-ninth Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that lifts you with someone else's weight, and the first bridge whose slope is yours to choose by where you stand; deepen it before a thirtieth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 146 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the lift) The gardener: a resident on every planter floor's lower hop (seeded like the signs' keepers, `climb_planter.gd` builds her from `Stick3D` beside the near end) who snips the vine herself when a body has stood on the near end for 2 s without pressing C (`sew`'s scissor hand, run 82, over her head — or a new `snip` pose: a reach up, two closing fingers, the arm dropping with the cut), says a `minds.json` line as the pot lands ("Mind the begonias."), and after the pot slides off walks to the vine with a fresh pot from a crate at her feet (`carry` + `shelve`, run 99) so the regrowth is 3 s when she is there instead of 8; she never snips for a body on the far side of the pivot, and the plain jump across the gap stays the body's own.
- [ ] (jobs + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the jobs axis, thinnest by keyword over open lines with two) The tower gardener's round: the gardener above is a town job with a door (`town_jobs.gd`, like the baker's) — at 09:00 she leaves the greenhouse-to-be (a lean-to against the Climb tower's plaza wall, two panes and a bench, built by `town_growth.gd` as a stage of the tower road) carrying a crate of three pots (`carry`, two-hand), walks the tower road, enters the door, and the Climb loader seeds her on the first planter floor; at 17:00 she comes back out with the empty crate; wages from `prices.json` (`wage.gardener`, paid at the ledger table of run 112); a second gardener is hired by the odd-jobs board (run 115) when a day passes with a planter floor unstaffed — the first town job whose workplace is inside a mini-game.
- [ ] (economy + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the economy axis, second-thinnest) Pots are stock: the vine's new pot is not free — each regrowth takes one pot from the gardener's crate, the crate holds three, and an empty crate means the trough works only by weight until the general store (run 114) sells her a refill at `prices.json` `buy.pot` (the store restocks pots like bread, run 72); a pot that slid off lands as a `pot` item on whatever hop is below (pick up, carry, give to her, sell at any staffed counter at `sell.pot` — "Money is real": the first thing in the tower that is worth coins in the town), so a player who climbs down for the pot keeps the lift running and earns the difference.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-12 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: twenty-nine packs and in every one of them the body uses the thing, never takes from it) Picking the flowers: C at the five flowers on the trough's back half (`pluck`, a new pose: a stoop to the stem, two fingers closing, the hand coming up with the bloom and tucking it behind the ear — the recovery) takes one bloom, a wearable with the hat-band socket of `wear.gd` (run 114), worn by the player and by the gardener on her days off; the trough with fewer flowers nods less (nod × flowers/5) and the gardener replants one bloom per visit; five blooms worn at once is the `garland` unlock (reputation with the gardener, `minds.json`) — the first thing taken out of the tower into the town.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-12 — deeper, from the check of run 146: the trough is soil and flowers and nobody ever waters it) Watering the trough: a watering can hangs on the vine hook's bracket (C on the near end while holding nothing takes it — `water`, run 46, the same pose the town's beds use); C along the trough pours for 1.5 s and the soil darkens from the near end (the deck's top material lerps to wet over the pour), and wet soil is heavier on its own side — for 20 s the trough with no pot settles back near-end down in tip_s × 0.6 and a body needs to stand mid_w + 12 px past the pivot to tip it; the flowers stop nodding while wet (nod × 0.3) and stand straighter; the gardener above waters it on her round when it is dry, and a rain floor (`wind_of(n)` rain) keeps it wet without a can; probe: the pour clock, the lerp, the shifted tip threshold, the dry return at 20 s

## Grows from Candle stair (run 147 — the thirtieth Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that wears under your weight and remembers it for a while: three candles a tap jump apart whose wax sinks under a standing body and sets back twenty seconds after; deepen it before a thirty-first set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 147 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the stair) The lamplighter's boy: a resident seeded on every candle floor's lower hop (like the signs' keepers, `climb_candle.gd` builds him from `Stick3D` beside the bracket) who, when all three flames are out, climbs the stair himself with a taper (`carry`, one hand) and relights each wick from the bottom (`light`, a new pose: the taper held out at arm's length, a stoop to the wick, the hand drawing back as the flame takes — a windup, a hold, a recovery), and a candle he reaches sets back at once instead of after 20 s; he sinks the wax like anyone (the same `stand`), so after his round the stair is lower by his own dents, and he says a `minds.json` line as the last wick takes ("Mind the wax.") — the first resident in the tower who undoes what the player did and costs the player something by it; the plain jump across the gap stays the body's own.
- [ ] (jobs + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the jobs axis, thinnest by keyword over open lines with three) The chandler: a town job with a door (`town_jobs.gd`, like the baker's) in a lean-to on the tower road, built by `town_growth.gd` as a stage of the tower road — at 09:00 the chandler dips a frame of wicks in a wax pot (`dip`, a new pose: both hands lowering the frame into the pot and lifting it, a shake, the lift again — three dips a candle), stacks the day's candles in a crate, carries it up the road (`carry`, two hands) and in at the tower door, and the Climb loader seeds the crate on the first Starlit floor's rest; wages from `prices.json` (`wage.chandler`, paid at the ledger table of run 112); a candle floor whose stair took more than 40 px of dents in a day is "worn" and the chandler replaces it the next morning (the stair rebuilt fresh, `hs` from the same `rises`) — the first town job whose product is tower content.
- [ ] (economy + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the economy axis, second-thinnest) Candles are stock: a candle stood on for 10 s in all (its dents add up over the day in the saved `records`) burns down for good — its top 8 px lower until the chandler above replaces it — and its stub can be carried out of the tower (C at a burnt-down candle with empty hands picks the stub up, `stoop`, run 107) and sold at any staffed counter at `sell.stub` or bought back by the chandler at `buy.stub` to melt down; `prices.json` entries for both; "Money is real": the second thing in the tower worth coins in the town, after the planter's pot.
- [ ] (motions + Climb, 2026-10-12 — the motion-library rule's town use for `sink`) Soft ground in the town: the park lawn for 60 s after rain (`town_systems.gd`'s weather clock) and the lake shore's mud band (`town_critters.gd`'s shore) are `sink` ground — a body standing still there for 2 s takes the `sink` pose with `sink_k` from the time stood and `sink_v` 1 for the first second, and leaves a pair of footprints (two dark ovals, 20 s) when it walks off; residents on their routines through the park do the same and a resident who sinks says `mind.line("mud")`; no slowdown, no refusal — the plain walk is untouched.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-12 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirty packs, and in every one of them the thing is as it was the first time; this one is the first that is not, so the tower should start to remember) Wear everywhere: the hammock net (run 135) sags 4 px per ten landings, the rope bridge (run 121) loses a plank after fifty crossings (a 24 px hole the body steps over, `step` in `stand`), the rolling ladder (run 127) loses a rung after a hundred climbs (a gap the hands reach past, 0.2 s slower); each pack reads a per-floor wear counter from the saved `records`, draws the worn state, and the chandler's and gardener's rounds reset it; a probe per pack for the worn state.

## Grows from Tulip lift (run 148 — the thirty-first Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that carries the body folded inside it: a bud that closes round you, a stem that stretches, petals that open at the top; deepen it before a thirty-second set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 148 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the lift) The gardener's round: a resident seeded on every tulip floor's lower hop (`climb_tulip.gd` builds her from `Stick3D` beside the pot, like the candle floors' boy in the item above) who, when the flower has wilted twice in a row without a watering, fetches a can from the pot's bracket (`carry`, one hand), waters the pot (`water`, run 70's pose, three tilts) so the next stretch is 10 px taller (max_lift still honoured) and the wilt 2 s later, and rides the lift herself once a tower-minute with `curl` like anyone (the same `enter`/`stand`) — the first resident in the tower who rides the player's set-piece; she says a `minds.json` line as the petals close ("Mind your elbows.") and will not step in while the player stands inside.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the quests axis, thinnest by keyword over open lines) A tower errand with a return trip: the odd-jobs board (run 115) posts "Bring a tulip petal from the garden floors" — C at an open bud with empty hands picks one petal (`stoop`, run 107; the flower keeps five until the next bloom regrows the sixth), the petal is a held item that droops in the hand after 3 town-minutes (its `sell.petal` price halves, the mesh tilts 0.6 rad), and the board pays `prize.petal` from `prices.json` at the ledger table (run 112) only while it is fresh; the first quest whose item comes out of Climb and the first with a timer the player can see on the thing itself.
- [ ] (vehicles + jobs, 2026-10-13 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest) The gardener's barrow: a wheelbarrow prop (`props3d.gd`: two handles, one wheel, a tray) parked at the tower road's lean-to that the chandler of the item above pushes up the road at 08:30 with the day's pots (`push`, the existing pose, hands on the handles and the torso leaning) and a player can take by C at the handles — pushing is `push` at 0.8 walk speed, a turn faster than 1.2 rad/s tips the load (`stumble`, the pots roll 2 m and can be picked up), and a pot delivered in it to the tower door pays `wage.barrow` at the ledger; residents give way to a barrow on the road like they do to a car; the second thing in the town that moves goods on a wheel, after the bus.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: three packs in a row carry the body — the planter tips it, the candle lowers it, the tulip lifts it — and none of them feels the body's own move) The body in the bud: during `rise` holding ← or → leans the stem 6 px that way (the floor arrives 6 px nearer the hop or the gap; a `curl_lean` meta the `curl` pose reads as a tilt of the whole ball), and SPACE while the petals are still closing cancels the ride — the petals part again from the fold's own fraction (`close` run backward) and the body steps out where it stood, no fall, no cost; both halves for the gardener above too; the plain jump stays 100%.
