- [ ] (weather + Climb, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the check of run 148: the tulip opens and wilts on its own clock and the tower's weather never touches it) Rain on the garden floors: while the town's weather clock (`town_systems.gd`) says rain, every tulip's `stay` is 8.0 instead of 5.0 and its `wilt` slows to half (the bud drinks and holds the body longer — `tulip.json` gains `rain_stay` and `rain_wilt_k`, read by `climb_tulip.gd` from the weather the owner session's loader already passes in), the open petals carry four drip beads that fall on each bloom (`Water3D`'s drop), and the `curl` pose under rain tucks the head 0.1 rad lower; a resident sheltering on the lower hop steps under the open bloom like under the bus shelter (`shade`, run 63); the plain ride stays the plain ride in dry weather.

## Grows from Bellows lift (run 149 — the thirty-second Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower the body's own foot lifts: a pedal that pumps a piston across the gap, a valve that lets it sink when the stamping stops; deepen it before a thirty-third set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 149 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the lift) The stoker's shift: a resident seeded on every bellows floor's lower hop (`climb_bellows.gd` builds him from `Stick3D` beside the bellows, like the gardener in the tulip item) who, when nobody has stamped for a tower-minute, steps onto the board and stamps eight times at one a second through the same `stamp` with `stomp`, walks off the board's end onto the raised plate, hops to the upper hop and climbs back down to his own over 20 s (`rail`, the ladder's pose, on a fixed cliff ladder drawn beside the cylinder) — the first resident who works the player's machine on the player's schedule; while he stamps the board is his: C on him asks him aside (`part`) and he steps off for 10 s; his `mind.line("stoke")` when the clank comes.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the quests axis, thinnest by keyword over open lines) The gauge reading: the odd-jobs board (run 115) posts "Read the gauge on the Clockwork bellows at full" — `bellows.json` gains `mark_k` 0.9 and the cylinder's gauge a red mark there, the player must bring the needle past it (eight steady stamps) and press C at the gauge while it is there (`scan`, run 100's pose, 0.8 s) to be handed a stamped slip (a held item, `sell.slip` 0), and the board pays `prize.gauge` from `prices.json` at the ledger table (run 112); the slip is void if the needle fell below the mark before the scan ended — the first quest whose proof is a reading the player had to make happen with their feet.
- [ ] (relationships + children, 2026-10-13 — the relationships axis, second-thinnest) Piggyback: a child resident (run 102) within 1.5 m of anyone who has stamped, jumped or kicked three times in 10 s asks to be carried (`ask`, a line from `minds.json` `child`); C on her hoists her onto the back — `piggy` pose for the carrier: leaning forward 0.3 with the hands hooked under the knees behind, a 1.4 Hz bounce in the walk and a heave on the hoist; the child's `cling` pose on the carrier's back socket with her arms round the neck — for up to 30 s or until the carrier sits, jumps or is hit (she slides off with `lurch`); residents carry their own children home at 18:00 this way and a carried child's mind ticks `befriend` toward the carrier; the first time one body rides another on purpose.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: three packs in a row lift the body with a force that is not its own — the pot's weight, the stem, now the player's foot through a pipe — and the pump has no sense of rhythm) Keeping time: `bellows.json` gains `beat_s` 1.0 and `window_s` 0.15 — a stamp landing within the window of one beat after the last lifts the piston 28 px instead of 24, three kept beats in a row pitch the wheeze up a fifth and make the `stomp` knee rise 0.2 higher with the arms over the head (meta stomp_beat 0..3), a beat missed by more than 0.6 s drops the bonus; the gauge's needle quivers 0.05 rad at 8 Hz while a beat is kept so the rhythm reads from across the gap; the stoker above keeps it perfectly, which is how the player learns it.
- [ ] (Climb + economy, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the check of run 149: the bellows pumps forever and nothing in the tower remembers how hard it was worked) Worn leather: each bellows counts its stamps for the climb (`bellows.json` gains `wear_at` 60); past it the leather shows a pale split along one pleat and every stamp lifts 20 px instead of 24 with a hiss through the split, until the player presses C at the bellows holding a cloth patch bought at the Climb base counter (`prices.json` `buy.patch`, earned back by `prize` — mini-game entry stays free) — `sew` (the tailor's existing pose) for 1.5 s, the split gets a darker square and the count resets.

## Grows from Dandelion clock (run 150 — the thirty-third Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower the body's own breath sets flying: a seed head blown bare, tufts that ride the wind and carry whoever jumps into their line; deepen it before a thirty-fourth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 150 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the clock) The seed-gatherer: a resident seeded on every dandelion floor's upper hop (`climb_dandelion.gd` builds her from `Stick3D` beside the hop's gap edge, like the gardener in the tulip item) who, when nobody has blown for a tower-minute, hops down to the lower hop, walks to the stalk and blows with the same `blow` and `puff`, jumps after the first tuft through the same `catch`/`ride` with `kite`, and lands back on her own hop — the first resident who plays the player's set-piece on the player's schedule; C on her asks for a turn (`part`) and she steps back 10 s; her `mind.line("wish")` when the last tuft pops (three dry lines in `minds.json` `default` — a wish made on a dandelion clock, in municipal tone).
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the quests axis, thinnest at 108 open lines by keyword) The clock's hour: the odd-jobs board (run 115) posts "Tell the time by the dandelion on floor 20" — `dandelion.json` gains `tell` true: C at the stalk a second time within 2 s of a blow, while the tufts are still leaving, is `count` (the existing `scan` pose, 0.8 s) and the number of tufts that had left before the C (1..8) is written on a held slip (`sell.slip` 0 in `prices.json`); the board pays `prize.clock` at the ledger table (run 112) if the slip's number matches the hour the board posted, which it draws fresh each tower-day from the seed — the first quest whose answer the player has to time with a second key press.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the interactions axis, second-thinnest at 142) Catch and keep: a riding body that presses C while the tuft carries it pockets the seed (`stoop` for 0.4 s; the tuft pops early and the body falls with its speed) — a held item `seed` with the verbs of the depth rule: give it to a resident (he `plant`s it in the nearest garden plot, run 70, where a small dandelion grows over a town-day and can be blown there with `puff` — the pose's first use in the town, by residents too), throw it (it drifts on the town's weather wind at the wind's speed for 3 s then pops; with no wind it drops), eat it (nothing happens and a resident nearby says so, `mind.line("seed")`).
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirty-three packs and only the kite, the vane and now the dandelion read the wind, and the wind is one number per floor) Gusts for the seeds: on windy floors the pack reads `gust(t)` from the "Weather in the tower" item (the Kite line section) once it exists, until then its own 7 s swell (±40%), so the tufts speed and slow in flight and the line a body jumps into bunches and spreads; a tuft caught at a swell's peak carries 10 px/s faster; the stalk sways with the swell so it reads before the blow; `probe_dandelion` gains the swell's numbers.

- [ ] (Climb + animals, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the check of run 150: the dandelion regrows over 12 s and nothing alive notices the bare stalk) The finch at the clock: a `bird3d.gd` finch perches on the bare stalk while the head regrows (`grow` 0..1), pecks a seed every 2 s (each peck sets `grow` back 0.05, never below 0, so a watched clock ripens slower), and flushes with its existing flap when a body lands on the lower hop or a tuft passes within 40 px; C beside it while it pecks is `stoop` held still for 1.5 s and it hops onto the player's hand (the existing `palm` pose) until the player moves — residents on the floor do the same when they idle there; `probe_dandelion` gains peck/flush/palm checks.

## Grows from Lighthouse lens (run 152 — the thirty-fifth Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that is a platform only while light is on it and the first clock a player speeds up by hand: three cloud puffs lit in turn by a lens sweeping the gap, a hand-wheel that doubles the sweep; deepen it before a thirty-sixth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 152 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the lens) The keeper: a resident seeded on every lens floor's lower hop (`climb_lens.gd` builds them from `Stick3D` at the hop's far end, like the archivist in the catalogue item) who, when nobody has spun for a tower-minute, walks to the wheel, spins it with the same `spin` (1.8 s, the same clock) and crosses the three puffs on the pack's own windows with the loader's jump arc — jumping at each puff's fade, landing on the next while it is lit — then stands on the upper hop looking back down the beam for a tower-minute before taking the band's rest route back; the first resident who uses the player's timed crossing on the player's clock; a body landing within 40 px of them on a puff gets one `mind.line("keeper")` ("Mind the dark half."); `probe_lens` gains the keeper's spin, crossing and line.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the quests axis, thinnest at 110 open lines by keyword) The keeper's errand: when the player reaches a lens floor's lower hop while the keeper (the item above) is on the upper hop, the keeper calls down one ask — "Spin it for me, I missed the beam." — listed as that floor's task on the sign board (`climb_signs.gd` reads it from the pack); spinning the wheel while they wait completes it: the keeper crosses on the fast windows and hands over `prize.lens_errand` (2 coins, `prices.json`) at the upper hop with the existing `pass` pose; one errand per floor per tower-day, never a gate — the puffs light on their own either way; `probe_lens` gains the ask, the completion and the prize.
- [ ] (relationships + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the relationships axis, second-thinnest at 134) Crossed together: a resident standing on a puff while the player stands on the next puff of the same lens (both lit, inside one window) calls `mind.befriend()` once per tower-day — the shared moment is the crossing, like a meal — and from then on greets the player at the wheel by name and spins it for them when the player stands at the wheel for 3 s without pressing C; a player who lands on a resident's puff and knocks them off it (the existing hit) is remembered the other way: the keeper refuses to spin for a tower-day and says `mind.line("knocked")`; `probe_mind` gains the two memories.
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirty-five packs and the lens is the tower's first light source, yet the tower has no dark) Fog bank: on a lens floor a fog rolls in for a tower-minute every tower-hour on the loader's clock — a row of translucent boxes 40 px wide (alpha 0.6, drifting 10 px/s on the floor's wind) in front of the walk line across the floor's width; the column the beam currently points at and the lit puff's column are hidden, so the beam cuts a clear lane through the fog and the crossing reads by the light; the plain jump is untouched (platforms stay solid, only the fog is drawn; nothing refuses) and the keeper (the item above) spins the wheel when the fog comes so the light comes round sooner; `probe_lens` gains the fog clock and the clear lane.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-14 — deeper, from the run 152 check: the lens lights puffs but nothing living uses them) Gulls on the beam: one gull per lens floor (the bird rig's bob/peck/flap/hop, built in `climb_lens.gd` like the drum) rides whichever puff is lit — it hops onto a puff 0.2 s after it hardens and flaps off to the next as the glow fades, so a player can read the window from the bird; a body landing on its puff makes it flap up 20 px and settle on the next lit puff (never blocks the landing, no hurt), and the keeper (the item above) tosses it a crumb with the `put` pose on each crossing; probe: the gull's puff matches `lit_at(t)` at 1.3/2.8/4.3 and is airborne at 5.7.

## Grows from Catalogue cabinet (run 151 — the thirty-fourth Climb content pack and the first stair in the tower you build one drawer at a time and lose one drawer at a time: a card catalogue in the cliff whose drawers come out for three seconds and carry you back when they go home; deepen it before a thirty-fifth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 151 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the cabinet) The archivist: a resident seeded on every catalogue floor's upper hop (`climb_catalogue.gd` builds her from `Stick3D` beside the hop's gap edge, like the gardener in the tulip item) who, when nobody has yanked for a tower-minute, hops down to the lower hop, yanks drawer one with the same `yank` (face toward the cabinet, meta yank_side from her facing), jumps onto it, yanks the next, and rides the first drawer home standing (the same `stand` z-carry) to land on the lower hop — the first resident who climbs the player's stair on the player's clock; a card she shakes loose she picks up with `stoop` and refiles in the top drawer (C-less: `take` by the pack); C on her asks for a turn (`part`) and she steps back 10 s; her `mind.line("file")` on a refile (three dry lines in `minds.json` `default` — a catalogue entry read aloud, in municipal tone).
- [ ] (economy + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the economy axis, thinnest at 146 open lines by keyword) Cards for coin: a picked-up catalogue card is a held item `card` (the loader keeps `holding` as it does the hand-axe) that any staffed counter in the town buys for `sell.card` in `prices.json` (1 coin — a scrap of paper) and the letter room (run 99) buys for `sell.card_filed` (3 coins) because it files it: the clerk's `shelve` pose, the card gone; six cards from six floors filed in one tower-day pay `prize.catalogue` at the ledger table (run 112) — an earning path beside the drawers, never a gate; `probe_catalogue` gains the held card and `probe_money` the two prices.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-13 — the animals axis, second-thinnest at 157) The finch in the top drawer: a `bird3d.gd` finch perches on the top drawer's pull when every drawer has been shut for a tower-minute (its existing bob and peck); a yank anywhere on the cabinet flushes it with its existing flap and it lands on the upper hop's gap edge, where it hops to a lying card and pecks it — each peck tears a corner, after three pecks the card is gone early (reshelved by the bird, the pack says so); it flushes again when a body lands within 40 px; residents on the floor shoo it with the existing `lwave` when they idle there; `probe_catalogue` gains perch/flush/peck checks.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirty-four packs and the drawers are the first platform with a depth of its own, yet nothing is ever put in one) Drawers hold things: a body standing on the lower hop that presses C with a held item (the hand-axe, a card, a seed from the dandelion item) while drawer one is open puts it in (`put`, the existing pose, 0.4 s) and the drawer slides home at once with it; the next yank of that drawer brings it back out on top as a pick-up — a stash that survives the floor (`records.climb_stash` by floor and drawer, saved like the camp); a resident archivist (the item above) empties a stash older than a tower-day into the top drawer; `probe_catalogue` gains stash/return checks.
- [ ] (polish, motions — from the check of run 151, measured in the real rig with a headless probe) The arm spread is mirrored in the pack poses: the rig's pivots apply z before x (Godot's YXZ order), so on a hanging arm a positive `rotation.z` moves the hand toward the body's local +x — and `sh.rotation.z = -s * θ`, which `unroll`, `puff`, `shunt`, `heave`, `sky` and most of the Climb poses use to "spread the arms", actually crosses them: at `unroll_k` 1 the s = +1 elbow sits at x −0.040 against a shoulder at +0.115 (a probe in run 151 printed it). Go through `stick3d_poses.gd` and the `stick3d_*.gd` pack poses, decide per pose whether the comment or the sign is right (the crossed arms may be what the owner has been seeing and approving), flip the sign where the comment is right, and pin the direction in each pose's probe with a shoulder-to-elbow dx like `probe_catalogue`'s; ship nothing else that run.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-13 — deeper, from the check of run 151: the card is read once and gone, and the drawers clack home on their own with nothing heard by anyone) The overdue stamp: a card picked up and not put back (in the drawer-stash item or dropped on the lower hop with C) for a tower-minute turns overdue — its text gains "OVERDUE" and the next yank on that floor's cabinet makes the drawer stick for 0.6 s (a `yank` that stalls at `pull` and shakes, then opens; still opens every time, plain outcome 100%) until the card is returned with `put`; the archivist resident (the item above) stamps a returned card with the existing `pin` pose and says one `mind.line("overdue")` line (add the category to `minds.json` `default`); probe the stick, the return and that a shut cabinet with no card out never sticks.

## Grows from Tapestry (run 153 — the thirty-sixth Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that carries you sideways while you climb it with your own hands, and the first cloth: a long tapestry on a ring rail whose rings slide toward the upper hop as the body climbs; deepen it before a thirty-seventh set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 153 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the tapestry) The weaver: a resident seeded on every tapestry floor's lower hop (`climb_tapestry.gd` builds them from `Stick3D` at the hop's far end, like the keeper in the lens item) who, when nobody has gripped the cloth for a tower-minute, walks to the gap edge, grips and climbs with the same `scale` (the same 40 px/s and 30 px/s drift on the pack's own clock), steps across, and sits on the upper hop's edge mending the hem with the existing `sew` for a tower-minute before taking the band's rest route back; one body per cloth — a player who presses C while the weaver is on it gets nothing and no line; `probe_tapestry` gains the weaver's timeline and the one-body rule.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the animals axis, thinnest at 107 open lines by keyword) A swallow on the rail: one bird per tapestry floor (the bird rig of `bird3d.gd` — bob, peck, flap, hop — built in `climb_tapestry.gd` like the rings) that sits on the far bracket and pecks at the tassels whenever the cloth is home; when the rings slide within 20 px of it the bird flaps up, circles the gap once in 2 s and settles on the near bracket, and a body stepping across under it makes it flap once without leaving; the weaver (the item above) shoos it off the hem with the existing `lwave` before sewing; never blocks a hand or a landing, no hurt; probe: the bird's bracket flips with the rings' position and it is airborne 0..2 s after the slide reaches it.
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 133) The cloth van: once a tower-day a resident drives the existing `Car3D` van from the general store (run 114) up the tower road to the plaza bay with a rolled cloth on its roof (a new `make_item` roll, two-hand carry with the existing `haul` grip when lifted), unloads it at the tower door and drives back; from the next climb on, every tapestry floor's cloth shows the next of three ink motifs (the roll is the delivery you can see in the tower); a player who takes the roll off the roof (the existing pick up) and carries it to the door gets `prize.cloth_delivery` (2 coins, `prices.json`) from the driver with the existing `pass` — never a gate, the cloths hang either way; `probe_cars` gains the van's route with 0 crashes and `probe_tapestry` the motif index.
- [ ] (Climb + economy, 2026-10-14 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirty-six packs and only the candle stair (run 147) wears, and nothing in the tower is ever mended) Worn hems: each tapestry remembers the crossings made on it (user://climb_cloth.json, per floor id): after 5 crossings its tassels shorten to half (the fringe boxes), after 15 the motif fades to half alpha and the hem band frays (a second thin band with alpha 0.5 under it), and the weaver (the first item above) restores it to new on their next mending visit, saying `mind.line("mended")` — the first thing in the tower that wears by the player's use and is repaired by a resident, not a timer; probe: the counts persist across a reload and reset only by the weaver.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-14 — deeper, from the check of run 153: the cloth rustles once when gripped and then nothing in the hall answers it) The dust shake: the first grip on a tapestry each tower-visit shakes a dust puff off the cloth (six small grey boxes in `climb_tapestry.gd` falling 40 px over 1.2 s and fading, alpha 0.6 → 0), and a body standing on the lower hop under it at that moment plays the existing `sigh` once and says nothing; later grips on the same visit shake nothing (the cloth is clean), so the puff reads as "nobody has climbed this lately"; probe: puff only on the first grip, gone at 1.2 s, the `sigh` only for a body within the hop's width.
- [ ] (polish, traffic — from the check of run 153, after the valley rewrite of cd54bbb1) `probe_traffic` still reports 0 crashes, but five of its eight cars (taxi, sedan, delivery, truck, hatchback at x −330/−245/−160/180/265, z ≈ ±5) print `still=90.0s` and end where they started, and every car reports `waypoints=0`; check whether these spawns now sit off the valley's road graph (cars route by `valley.gd` nodes/branches) and either respawn them on a road node or teach the probe the new graph so it measures driving again.

## Grows from Hurdles (run 155 — the thirty-eighth Climb content pack, the first set-piece on a hop top that the body's own speed decides: a row of low rail hurdles on Wind cliffs floors that a walk vaults and a slow step bumps, counted once a tower-day on the camp boards; deepen it before a thirty-ninth set-piece)
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 91 open lines by whole-word keyword) The rail cart: a rail bumped three times in one tower-day comes off its posts and lies flat on the hop (a third `hits` entry within `day_s` → the rail box drops to the hop top, nothing solid, the vault over that rail no longer counts); once a tower-day a resident pushes a two-wheeled handcart of spare rails (a prop built in `props3d.gd` — two spoked wheels that turn with the walk, pushed with the existing `push` pose) up the tower road to the Wind cliffs camp, and a player who C's at the cart takes one rail (`carry`), walks it to the row and C's at the fallen rail's posts to seat it (`plonk`), which the hurdler pays 2 coins for — an earning path, never a gate; a rail the player does not replace is replaced by the hurdler the next tower-day; probe: the third bump, the fallen rail, the carry and the seating.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the animals axis, second-thinnest at 101) The cliff jackdaw: a bird on the small bird rig (bob, peck, flap, hop — the standard of 2026-09-28) perched on the middle rail of every row, who hops to the next rail when a body comes within 40 px, flaps off the row when the body vaults (one circle at the cliff face, 4 s, back to a rail), flaps in place and scolds (a two-note caw, 0.2 s) when a rail is bumped, and pecks at the post feet between visitors; the hurdler's run (the item above) sends it up too; no new file over 120 lines, the rig reused from the town's birds.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-14 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirty-eight packs and the camp boards have only now started reading a pack) The board's day: `climb_signs.notes` becomes the camp board's "TODAY" block — the telescope (floors peered), the bell (rings), the catalogue (drawers opened), the hurdles (rails vaulted) each append one line through the same hook, the board's title gains "TODAY" when any line is non-empty, and the lines reset with the tower-day (600 s, the hurdles' `day_s` moved to a shared `data/climb/day.json` the packs read); never a prize, only the record — a climber reads what they did on the way up.
- [ ] (interactions + motions, check of run 155 — the `hurdle` pose leaves the tower) The summit hurdles: a row of three low wooden hurdles (built in `props3d.gd`, rail 0.55 m high, 1.7 m apart) on the flat beside the outdoor gym at Mt. Pell's summit (`town_mountain.gd`'s `_gym`, off the stairs' line), a `gym` spot with `ex: "hurdle"`; C at the near end runs the row with the same `hurdle` pose driven by the body's own x through the existing `HurdlePoses` (meta hurdle_k), a rail clipped while walking (no C) wobbles 0.6 s and stops the body with `teeter`, and residents who pick `gym` (the existing `mind.score` term) run it too, once per visit, then say `mind.line("hurdle")` (add the category to `minds.json`'s `default`); the odd-jobs board gains "Run the summit hurdles" (done on one clean run); probe: the row's placement clear of the stair path, a run of three, a resident run.
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 74 open lines by whole-word keyword) The hurdler commutes: two bicycles (a `Bike3D` builder in `props3d.gd` — two spoked wheels that turn with the ground speed, a frame, bars, a saddle; the cycling family from the owner's board as a `pedal` pose: legs circling at the wheel's rate, the torso low over the bars, one hand lifting off the bar for 0.6 s before a turn, never a still frame) in a rack at the tower road's foot (x 15.5, z −13); once a tower-day the floor-20 hurdler's town twin (a resident with the `hurdler` tag, seeded in `residents.json`) rides from the rack to the Mt. Pell outdoor gym for the summit hurdles item above and back, 6 m/s, steering with the cars' raycasts and never on the main road's lanes; the player takes the second bicycle with C at the rack and rides the same road with the same pose and speed, Z brakes, C at any rack or 30 s abandoned returns it; nothing costs. Probe: the rack, the route clear of the lanes, the pose at speed, the return.
- [ ] (relationships + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the relationships axis, tied second-thinnest at 100) The hurdler remembers: the hurdler gets a mind (`resident_mind.gd` — the first mind in the tower, saved with the others in user://minds.json under the key `hurdler.<floor>`) that counts the player's vaults it saw within 60 px; from the third, when the player steps onto the row hop the hurdler walks to the margin and runs the row in the front lane in lockstep (started by the player's first `in_row` tick, the same `hurdle` pose on its own x, 0.5 s behind) and says `mind.line("hurdle_pal")` at the far end (new category in `minds.json`'s `default`); a player who bumps a rail while the hurdler watches gets `mind.line("hurdle_ouch")` and a sympathy `teeter` from the hurdler (the aftermath family of system 21); a player who shoves the hurdler off its home end (the existing climb shove) is remembered too — the hurdler faces away (`avert`, the estranged pairs' slice 1) for a tower-day and runs only on its own minute. No coins, no gate, no numbers shown. Probe: the count, the lockstep start and offset, the two lines, the avert day.
- [ ] (motions + interactions, 2026-10-14 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: the `wheel` about-turn is the first turnaround motion and lives only on the hurdle floors) The town turns on its heel: `wheel` leaves the tower — a resident whose walk hits a dead end (the quay's end, a fence, a wall within 0.6 m on the pathfinder's next step) stops and turns with `wheel` before walking back instead of pivoting on the yaw lerp alone (`resident_life.gd`'s step-back — the pose runs its 0.5 s with `move_dir` zero, then the walk resumes); the player too: tapping the opposite arrow while standing still (speed < 0.2 m/s) in `town_player.gd` plays `wheel` once (the body turns during it, 10 rad/s as now — no slower), never while moving (a running reversal stays the existing lean); the hurdler's `climb_hurdler.gd` keeps its own use; `probe_mind` gains the dead-end turn. No new pose needed — but add `wheel`'s mirror: a left-foot plant for a body turning the other way (`WheelPoses` reads `f.get_meta("wheel_dir", -1.0)`), so two residents turning toward each other do not plant the same foot.
- [ ] (motions + Climb, check of run 156 — the hurdler runs cold) The warm-up: before each of its minute runs the hurdler spends 1.6 s at the home end with a `limber` pose (one heel up on the end rail's height in the air, the torso folding over the raised leg, a bounce on a 0.4 s beat, then a swap to the other leg and two loose leg swings — never a still frame) through `stick3d_limber.gd` (`LimberPoses`), and the player standing still at the row's home end within 18 px gets the same pose on C (1.6 s, cancelled by any move; nothing counts, nothing gates, the plain run is untouched); the poke-run after a player vault skips the warm-up (it is a reaction, not a session); probe: the 1.6 s before the minute run, none before the poke-run, the player's C and its cancel, the pose bounce/swap/swing values. Land it after the `stick3d_poses.gd` split item above (the poses file is at 490 lines).

## Grows from Telescope (run 154 — the thirty-seventh Climb content pack, the first thing in the tower that adds no platform and only lets the eye go further: a tripod telescope that draws the floor above as star outlines on the cliff for four seconds; deepen it before a thirty-eighth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb, run 154 — the pack's own next step; the resident half of the telescope) The stargazer: a resident seeded on every telescope floor's lower hop (`climb_telescope.gd` builds them from `Stick3D` at the hop's far end, like the weaver and the keeper items) who, when nobody has peered for a tower-minute, walks to the tripod, peers with the same `peer` (the same 4.0 s, the pan swept once end to end through `pan`), says one of three `minds.json` "stars" lines about the floor above ("Four hops. One moves. Noted."), and walks back; one body per tripod — a player who presses C while the stargazer is on it gets nothing and no line; `probe_telescope` gains the stargazer's timeline and the one-body rule.
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 100 open lines by keyword) The lens cart: once a tower-day a resident pushes a two-wheeled handcart (a prop built in `props3d.gd`, two spoked wheels that turn with the walk, pushed with the existing `push` pose) from the general store up the tower road to the plaza bay carrying a brass lens case; while the case sits at the tower door the next climb's telescopes draw the floor above at a third instead of a quarter (`scale` 0.33 read from the case's presence, saved as `climb_lens_case` in records until the next tower-day); a player who pushes the cart the last 20 m (the existing push with the cart as the pushed thing) earns `prize.lens_cart` (2 coins, `prices.json`) from the resident with the existing `pass` — never a gate, the telescopes work either way; `probe_cars` gains the cart's walk with no car crash and `probe_telescope` the scale switch.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-14 — the quests axis, second-thinnest at 121) The star chart card: the odd-jobs board (run 115) gains a card "Count the hops above floor n" posted by the stargazer (the item above) once a tower-day for one Starlit floor; it is completed by peering through that floor's telescope (the pack records `peered_n` in records when `peer` succeeds) and then standing on the floor above's last hop, pays the card's coin at the board and adds a hand-drawn star chart of that floor (the same outline frames, inked on paper, in `climb_signs.gd`'s board) to the tower door's sign — the first quest that sends a player up the tower for a reason other than height; `probe_telescope` checks `peered_n` and the board probe the card's completion.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-14 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: thirty-seven packs and the tower never shows what the last climber did with one) The eyepiece memory: a telescope remembers the last pan a body left it at (per floor id in user://climb_scope.json) and the tube stays turned there (`tube.rotation.y`) so a later climber sees where the last one looked; peering again starts from that pan instead of 0; the stargazer resets it to 0 when they peer; probe: the pan persists across a rebuild of the band and resets by the stargazer.
- [ ] (interactions + motions, check of run 154 — the `peer` pose leaves the tower) The lookout telescope: a brass telescope on a tripod at the town's existing `lookout` spot (built in `props3d.gd` from the same tube/tripod proportions as `climb_telescope.gd`, kept out of the road band z −0.1..4.1), used with the existing `peer` pose by the player (C, the pan on ←/→ as in the pack) and by residents whose mind picks `lookout` at night (a `mind.score` term "stars" raised by `curious` after `is_night()`, not a fixed roll); what the eye finds is the Climb tower's lit Starlit floors on the horizon (the tube's yaw toward the tower decides a toast "Floor 32 is lit. Someone is up there." from the saved `climb_scope`/records), a resident says `mind.line("stars")` (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`) and the task "Look at the tower from the lookout" in the odd-jobs board sends a player there; `probe_mind` gains the night pick.

## Grows from Sundial (run 157 — the thirty-ninth Climb content pack and the first clock in the tower: a stone dial on Hanging garden floors whose shadow turns once a tower-day, read with `squint` for the time and for which vent above is blowing; deepen it before a fortieth set-piece)
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-15 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: the hurdles write to the camp board and the sundial now knows the hour) The board's hour: `climb_sundial.note(n)` joins `climb_signs.notes` so every camp board opens with "Tower time hh:mm." under its title, read from the same `day_s` clock the hurdles count by, and the hurdles' line gains the hour of the last vault ("last at 09:40"); `day_s` moves into the shared `data/climb/day.json` of the board's-day item (Grows from Hurdles) in the same run, since two packs now read it.
- [ ] (interactions + motions, check of run 157 — the `squint` pose leaves the tower) The square sundial: a stone dial on a waist-high plinth in the square's garden corner (built in `props3d.gd` from the slab, marks, gnomon and shadow of `climb_sundial.gd`, out of the road band z −0.1..4.1 and off the hub's walk lines), its shadow driven by the town's own day clock; C reads it with the same `squint` and the time is said over the head with the next closing hour ("Half past two. The bakery closes at six." — the real counters' hours), and residents whose next routine stop has an opening hour read it on the way with the same pose (a `mind.score` term, not a `randf()`), so the square shows people checking the time.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the quests axis, second-thinnest at 92 open lines by whole-word keyword) The noon card: the odd-jobs board (run 115) gains a card "Read the garden dial at tower noon" posted by the dial-keeper (the item above) once a tower-day for one Hanging garden floor; it is completed by a `read` on that floor's dial between 11:30 and 12:30 of the shared tower clock (the pack records `read_n` and the hour in records when `read` succeeds), pays the card's 3 coins at the board, and the keeper's line that day becomes "You were here at noon." — never a gate, the dial reads for free as before.
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 83 open lines by whole-word keyword) The soil barrow: on every dial floor a one-wheeled hand barrow of soil (a `Barrow3D` builder in `props3d.gd` — a spoked wheel that turns with the ground speed, two handles, a heaped tray; the wheelbarrow family from the owner's board as a `barrow` pose: both hands low on the handles, the back flat, the weight forward, short steps, the tray tipping forward for 0.6 s at the end — never a still frame) stands at the hop's far end; C at its handles pushes it at 60 px/s along the hop top to the slab's edge, where the tray tips and the soil fills a small bed beside the slab in which a flower grows over the next tower-day (three stages from primitives) and wilts if no barrow comes for two days; the barrow rolls back to its spot at tower dawn (06:00) and the dial-keeper pushes it at dawn on their own with the same pose; never solid, nothing costs, the plain run past it untouched.
- [ ] (weather + Climb, check of run 157 — deeper: the dial has a shadow but the tower has no sky) The overcast hour: when the town's weather is cloudy or raining (the existing weather state, read once per tower-minute), every dial's shadow fades to 0.15 alpha and a `read` says "Tower time unclear. Vents: floor 7, 2 up." — the vent arrows still draw, since they read the wind, not the sun; the dial-keeper squints, shakes the head once (a 0.4 s yaw beat in `squint`, not a new pose) and says `mind.line("overcast")` (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`); `probe_sundial` gains an OVERCAST line (alpha, the text, the arrows unchanged).
- [ ] (vehicles + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 69 open lines by whole-word keyword) The funicular to the Climb door: two open wooden cars on a rail pair up the tower road's slope (a `Funicular3D` builder in `props3d.gd` — a cable over a wheel at the top, one car rising while the other descends, the cars 1.6 m long with a rail to hold), running on the shared tower clock between the tower road's foot (x 15.5, z −13) and the plaza bay at the tower door, leaving at :00 and :30 of every tower-hour (`data/climb/day.json` once the board's-day item lands, else the sundial's `day_s`); C at a stopped car boards it and the body rides standing with the existing `board` pose (hands on the rail), C or the end stop gets off; the dial-keepers' town twins (residents with a `keeper` tag seeded in `residents.json`) ride it up at tower dawn (06:00) and down at dusk with the same pose; nothing costs, the road stays walkable beside the rail, the cars never cross the main road's lanes z 0.2..3.8 and the AI cars' raycasts see them as a wall only at the foot stop; probe: the counter motion, the timetable, the boarding and alighting, no lane crossing.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the animals axis, second-thinnest at 72) The dial lizard: a lizard on the quad rig (`quad3d.gd` — a low body, legs splayed, a long two-segment tail; the standard of 2026-09-28 in full: idle breath and blink, four-beat walk, bound, sit, lie, stretch, look at the player, flee, tail curl, and two species moves — bask: the body flattens on the stone in a 0.5 s pulse; dart: a 0.3 s bound to the slab's rim) that basks on the slab's sunny half — the half the shadow is not on, so once a tower-hour it crosses the slab ahead of the shadow — and darts to the rim when a body comes within 30 px (back after 4 s); the keeper's noon wipe sends it off too and it climbs back after the keeper leaves; never solid, never on the walking line in front of the slab; probe: the sunny-half pick at four hours, the dart and return, the noon exit.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, 2026-10-15 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: the keeper now says the hour but only a body on that hop sees it) The hour's call: at each tower-hour the keeper's read also rings the dial's `chime` (one note, two at noon, the existing `_snd` player at −10 dB and `max_distance` 60 so the floor below hears it), the camp board's "Tower time" line (the board's-hour item above) gains "last called by the keeper on floor n", and the hurdler (`climb_hurdler.gd`) runs its row once at tower noon on the same shared clock regardless of its minute — the first thing two packs do together; never a gate, nothing counts; `probe_sundial` gains the chime at :00 and the double at noon, `probe_hurdle` the noon run.
- [ ] (jobs + motions, check of run 158 — the `wipe` pose leaves the tower) The shopfront wiper: the town's shop windows (the bakery, café and counters that already have glass in `props3d.gd`) get a dust film (a `Label3D`-free quad over the pane whose alpha rises 0.05 per town-hour to 0.6), a resident whose job is `cleaner` (seeded in `residents.json`, a bucket held on the left-hand socket) walks the shop row each morning and wipes each pane with the existing `wipe` pose (the full 2.0 s; alpha falls to 0 over the rub phase), and C at a dusty pane with empty hands does the same (the pane clears, the shop's keeper says `mind.line("clean_glass")` — three deadpan lines in `minds.json` — and the odd-jobs board posts "Wipe the bakery window" for 2 coins when a pane passes 0.4); never solid, out of the road band z −0.1..4.1.

## Grows from The Climb pose split (run 159 — the first hygiene run in the tower: the forty-one tower poses left `stick3d_poses.gd` for `stick3d_climb.gd`, so the next pose has room; keep the pose library honest before it grows again)
- [ ] (hygiene + motions, 2026-10-15 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: forty-one tower poses now route through one file and nothing checks that each one still moves) The pose net: `game/tools/probe_poses.gd` (headless, `-s`) puts one `Stick3D` in an empty scene and, for every name in stick3d.gd `CI_POSES`, sets `pose_request`, steps 3 s at 60 Hz and reports two things per pose — "still" when no joint rotation changes between 0.5 s and 1.5 s (a still frame is not a pose; the owner's rule) and "sunk" when, 0.5 s after the pose is cleared, `pelvis.position.y` is not back within 0.01 of `StickRig.HIP_Y` (a pose that forgets to give the pelvis back); every pose must print "ok", and the probe is named in the GROW.md probe list of the Fit checklist in the same run; no pose edited unless it fails, and then only its recovery.
- [ ] (hygiene + motions, 2026-10-15 — deeper, from run 159: `CI_POSES` in stick3d.gd is still a hand-kept copy of `ClimbPoses.POSES` plus the town pack names) One declaration per pose: `StickPoses` gets `const POSES` (the town names water…plonk in their current order) and stick3d.gd builds `CI_POSES` as `StickPoses.POSES + ClimbPoses.POSES` (a `static var` filled once in `_static_init()`, since a `const` cannot concatenate two class constants in 4.3 — write the why in the comment); `pose_request in CI_POSES` reads the same; a new pose then adds its name in one place; `probe_hurdle`, `probe_sundial` and the pose net above at their wants.
- [ ] (vehicles + jobs, 2026-10-15 — the vehicles axis, thinnest at 78 open lines by whole-word keyword) The milk float: a `Car3D` spec `float` (the van's glb, top 2.5, painted cream, an open crate rack of twelve bottles built in `props3d.gd` on its bed) driven by a resident with a `milk` job (seeded in `residents.json`) from town 06:00 to 07:30 along the north lane and the tower road's houses, stopping at every door with a home on it: the driver steps down, carries one bottle to the step with the existing carry and sets it with `plonk`, the household's resident takes it in with `stoop` when they leave for work, and an empty bottle stands on the step from dusk for the next dawn's round; the player may take a full bottle from a step (drinkable with `drink`, 3 sips) — the household's mind gets a "milk" term −1 for two town-days and the driver says `mind.line("milk_gone")` (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`) — or sell it at a counter for its `prices.json` price; the float crosses the main road only at the U-turns x ±80..90 and never stands in the lanes z 0.2..3.8; probe: the stop count, a bottle on every step at 07:30, the empties at dusk, the taken bottle's memory.
- [ ] (relationships + motions, 2026-10-15 — the relationships axis, second-thinnest at 66 open lines by whole-word keyword) Neighbours walk in: two residents whose homes share a lane (`residents.json` `home`) and who leave within 60 s of each other at town morning meet at the lane mouth and walk the first 20 m into the hub as a pair (`resident_pair.gd` of run 95, the existing `walk_pair`), parting at the first turn with the existing `wave`; each shared walk adds +1 to a "lane" term on both minds, and from 3 they greet by name at any later passing (`mind.line("lane_pal")`, three deadpan lines in `minds.json`, no stop) and one picks a bench next to the other at lunch through `mind.score` rather than a `randf()`; a player who knocks one over within 5 m of the other is remembered on both minds (the existing hit memory, no new state — the Estranged-pairs section above owns the falling-out); probe: the pair forms on two mornings, the term's count, the greeting from 3, no pair while either is in `drive` or `chase`.

## Grows from Wind chime (run 160 — the fortieth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower made for the ear: five bamboo tubes hung from an iron bracket over a Hanging garden gap, swung by the floor's wind, rung and tangled by a body jumping through them with `brush`; deepen it before a forty-first set-piece)
- [ ] (interactions + motions, run 160 — deeper: the player can ring the chime but cannot quiet it) Hush: standing on the upper hop within 18 px of the lip while the tubes tangle (`tangle(c, t) > 0`), C plays `hush` through `stick3d_hush.gd` (`HushPoses`, 1.1 s: both arms rise out and up to cup the tubes with the palms in, a hold of 0.6 s in which the hands close an inch on a 0.3 s beat and the head tilts to listen, a step back with the arms lowering — never a still frame) and the pack damps the tangle four times faster for the hold (`hush_t0` on the chime; `tangle()` reads it); the chime jumper (the item above) hushes its own ringing after the drop; nothing counts, nothing gates, a C anywhere else on the hop stays the loader's.
- [ ] (weather + Climb, run 160 — deeper, from the last twenty log lines: `wind_of(n)` is one number per floor, so the chime, the kite and the vane never gust together) Gusts: `data/climb/wind.json` (gust_every 7..13 s seeded by floor with the loader's `rng`, gust_s 1.5, gust_k 2.0) read by a small `ClimbWind` helper (`scripts/games/climb_wind.gd`, `gust(n, t) -> float` 0..1); the chime's `sway()` and tink rate multiply by 1 + gust_k × gust, two tubes ring at a gust's start; the kite (run 131) and vane (run 136) read the same `gust()` for their pull and turn, so the three agree on every floor; `probe_chime`, `probe_kite` and `probe_vane` print the gust at two times from the same seed.
- [ ] (Climb + interactions, run 160 — deeper: the hurdles and the sundial write to the camp board; the chime, the tower's first sound, is nowhere on paper) The wind line: `climb_chime.note(n)` joins `climb_signs.notes` so the camp board under a Hanging garden pair lists the floors above with wind by their chimes ("Wind at 40 and 42; still at 41, 43, 44." from `wind_k` — the vent floors' chimes too) and, once a tower-day per chime, "Chimes rung today: k" counted when `brush()` fires (a player's knock only — the jumper's rings are not the player's record), the shared `day_s` of the hurdles and the sundial; never a gate.
- [ ] (motions + interactions, check of run 160 — the `brush` pose leaves the tower) The bead curtain: the general store's door (run 114) gets a bead curtain built in `props3d.gd` from the chime's hanging-tube code (nine strands, the same `tube_angle` swing and the same 2 s tangle, no sound but a soft rattle through the store's existing `_snd`); every body that walks through the door — the player, the storekeeper opening up, a resident coming to buy — plays `brush` for its first 0.5 s on the way through (the pose's air phase at walking speed, no landing phase: the loader of the town sets `brush_land` to the entry time so the shake comes at once), and the strands part and tangle around them; the door's `use` is untouched, nothing refuses.
- [ ] (relationships + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the relationships axis, thinnest at 85 open lines by whole-word keyword) Who rang it: the chime jumper (the first item above) gets a mind (`resident_mind.gd`, saved under `jumper.<floor>` like the hurdler's) that counts the rings it heard the player make within 120 px; from the third ring it waits at the lip for the player to land and jumps right after them through the tubes they just rang — a duet, two rings 0.8 s apart — and says `mind.line("chime_pal")` (three deadpan lines in `minds.json`'s `default`); a player who hushes the chime (the hush item above) while the jumper is mid-air is remembered as a spoiler for a tower-day: the jumper turns away (`avert`, the estranged pairs' slice 1) and jumps only on its own minute; no coins, no gate, no numbers shown; `probe_chime` gains the count, the duet offset, the lines and the avert day.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the animals axis, second-thinnest at 88 open lines by whole-word keyword) The chime wren: a wren on the bird rig (`bird3d.gd` — bob, peck, flap, hop; the 2026-09-28 standard in full: idle breath and blink, a hop along the bracket's arm, a look at the player, a flee, and two species moves — a tail flick on a 0.7 s beat and a song: three rising tinks through the chime's own `_tone_wav` at 2.2 kHz) that perches on the bracket's arm of every chime whose `wind_k` is under 0.15 (the still floors 5..9), sings once a tower-minute, and flies off to the cliff face when the tubes tangle (`tangle(c, t) > 0`) or a body comes within 40 px, back after 6 s; on the windy floors the arm is empty — the wren is the ear's way of reading "no wind here" before the tubes do; `probe_chime` gains the perch on a still chime, the empty arm at wind 120, the flee and the return.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 37 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 161: the jumper and the player ring the tubes but nothing ever comes of a hard knock) The slipped tube: a chime knocked twice within 3 s (`tangle(c, t) > 0.7` at the second `brush()` — a player and the jumper, or a quick drop-and-rejump) lets its longest tube slip its string and lie on the upper hop at the lip (`climb_chime.slip(c)`: the pivot hidden, a tube mesh laid on the hop, `c["slipped"]` true, the chord rebuilt from the four hz left — a thinner ring that says something is missing); C with empty hands within reach picks it up (the loader's held item, the carry verb — it can be carried off the floor, thrown, dropped, sold), and C at the bracket within 24 px with the tube in hand re-hangs it with a `knot` pose (`stick3d_knot.gd`: both hands up to the arm, three tugs of the string on a 0.35 s beat, a step back with the head tipped to check it hangs straight, never a still frame) and the five-note chord returns; the jumper re-hangs its own slipped tube on its next minute instead of jumping (walks to the lip, `knot`, back) and says nothing; never a gate, a four-tube chime still rings and still tangles; `probe_chime` gains the slip, the carry, the knot and the jumper's repair.
- [ ] (economy + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the economy axis, second-thinnest at 48 open lines by whole-word keyword; grows from the slipped tube above) Bamboo is stock: a slipped tube nobody re-hangs by the next tower-day is gone when the floor reloads (swept — the chime stays four-note until someone hangs a new one), and the camp board under a Hanging garden pair lists "bamboo tube — 3 coins" from `data/prices.json` (`buy.bamboo_tube`), bought at the board like the sundial's lantern oil with `town_growth.can_pay` and refused with the board's own line when the wallet is short; C at a bracket with an empty string and a tube in hand hangs it with the same `knot`; a found tube sold at any staffed counter pays `sell.bamboo_tube` 1 coin; the earning path is the counters and the Climb prize — never a gate, mini-game entry stays free; `probe_money` gains the price pair and `probe_chime` the bought tube on the string.

## Grows from Long-jump pit (run 162 — the forty-first Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that measures a jump: a raked sand strip on a Cloud sea hop with a takeoff board at its near end; a full-charge jump that leaves the board and comes down in the sand lands with `plant` and plants a peg at the day's best; deepen it before a forty-second set-piece)
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 38 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 162: the sand takes a body and shows nothing of it) The scar and the rake: every landing in the sand leaves a scar (a darker 12 px box at the landing x, `p["scars"]`, up to three, the oldest fading after a tower-minute) and the rake lines under it vanish; a rake leans on the peg's side of the board — C with empty hands within 20 px of the strip takes it and walks the strip with a `rake` pose through `stick3d_rake.gd` (`RakePoses`: both hands low on the handle, a pull on a 0.4 s beat, the torso bowed, the head down — never a still frame), each pull within 14 px of a scar erases it and redraws the rake line; the rake goes back to the board on C there or when the body jumps; the leaper (the item above) rakes its own scar before it walks back; no coins, nothing gates, the plain run across the strip is untouched; `probe_pit` gains the scars, the pull and the leaper's rake.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-15 — the animals axis, tied second-thinnest at 49 open lines by whole-word keyword with economy, which run 161 served) The sand sparrow: a sparrow on the bird rig (`bird3d.gd` — bob, peck, flap, hop; the 2026-09-28 standard in full: idle breath and blink, a look at the player, a flee, and two species moves — a dust bath at the sand's far end, wings shivering on a 0.3 s beat for 2 s, and a tail flick) that lands on the sand of every pit under floor 60 when no body has landed there for a tower-minute, baths once a tower-minute, hops off at the thump of a landing or when a body comes within 40 px and returns after 6 s; from floor 60 the strips stay empty — the sparrow is the eye's way of reading "nobody has jumped here lately"; `probe_pit` gains the bath, the flight at the thump and the return.
- [ ] (Climb + quests, run 162 — deeper: the peg is pulled at the end of every tower-day and nothing remembers it) The mark that is kept: the best per pit is saved under `records.climb_marks[pit_id]` like `climb_camp` (the pack exposes `marks()` and takes `restore(marks)`; the owner session wires the save on `land()` as it wires the loader), the camp board's line gains "your best: 3.9 m (floor 27)" from the saved marks of the floors above, and a saved mark beyond the day's peg draws a second, paler peg (`e9dcc3` wood, no accent) behind the day's so the player sees the gap to beat; no coins, no gate; `probe_pit` gains the saved mark surviving a day change and the two pegs.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-16 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 52 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 163: the leaper jumps beside you but never answers you) The leaper's applause: when the player's counted `land()` in the sand beats the leaper's `last_dist`, an idle leaper within 150 px plays a `clap` pose (`stick3d_clap.gd`: both hands rise to the chest, three claps on a 0.3 s beat with the elbows opening and closing, the head nodding once, the hands dropped — never a still frame) and says "Yours." above the head for 2 s; a mark short of it gets "Mine." and no clap; C with empty hands within 30 px of an idle leaper asks it to jump now (the REACT path without a mark — `pit["ask_t"]`), once per tower-minute, refused with "Just did." inside the minute; the player can clap too — C with empty hands while the leaper is in `plant` within 150 px plays the same `clap` and the leaper's next `_leap` aims one AIMS step further; `probe_pit` gains the clap on a beating mark, the ask, the refusal and the further aim. Plain outcome untouched: nothing on the hop refuses or costs, the plain run across stays 100%.
- [ ] (places + town, 2026-10-16 — wider, from run 163: the long jump lives only in the tower, and the town is the engine) The park's long-jump pit: the pit brought down to the ground as a component — a raked sand strip 4 m long with a takeoff board at the park's gravel edge (`props3d.gd` builder `pit(len, dir)` reused by `town_places.gd`; off the road band, flat hub ground), a resident PE routine at mid-morning where two residents take turns from the board with the same `limber` → crouch → jump arc as the leaper (the town's jump is `jump3d.gd`, the aim the same sand fraction) and land with `plant`, the day's best as the same accent peg; the player with SPACE charged on the board jumps it like any town jump and lands in the sand with `plant` when the arc comes down inside the strip; a task "jump the park pit"; one `mind.line` category "pit"; `probe_plots`-style probe `probe_pit_park.gd`: the builder, the two residents' turns, the peg. Structural under the strict definition? No — a place on an existing map; the cadence note stands.
- [ ] (vehicles + places, 2026-10-16 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 57 open lines by whole-word keyword; grows from the park pit above) The sand barrow: a wheelbarrow (`props3d.gd` builder `barrow()` — one wheel, two handles, a heaped load that empties) a groundsman resident pushes from the river bank at "Mist Quay" to the park pit once a tower-day after the morning routine, with a `barrow` pose from the owner's board (both hands low on the handles, the back bowed, short steps on a 0.45 s beat, the barrow tipped forward over the strip to empty with a lift of the handles — never a still frame; `stick3d_barrow.gd`), the load re-raking the strip (the rake lines redrawn, the scars of the Climb item's rule cleared); the player can take a parked barrow with C and push it with the same pose at 0.7 × walk speed, tip it with Z, and gets one coin from the groundsman for bringing a full one to the pit (`prices.json` `prize.sand_barrow`); residents step out of its line like the cars' raycast rule; a task "bring a barrow of sand to the pit"; probe: the route, the tip, the re-rake, the coin.

## Grows from Tightrope (run 164 — the forty-second Climb content pack and the first surface in the tower that the body's own rhythm keeps or loses: a rope from one hop's edge up to the next on Starlit floors, walked on the toes with the arms out; hold a direction and the sway passes 3 px at 0.37 s, walk in bursts and it never does; deepen it before a forty-third set-piece)
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-16 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 16 open lines by whole-word keyword `interaction|interact`; deeper, from run 164: the rope is walked bare-handed, and a ropewalker never is) The balance pole: a bamboo pole leaning on the lower ring's hop (`props3d.gd` builder reused from the chime's tubes), C with empty hands within 20 px takes it in the existing two-hand carry across the body — on the rope it halves `speed_amp` and `wind_amp` (that is what the pole is for) but the body walks at 0.6 × WALK and cannot jump from the rope while holding it (SPACE lays the pole down instead); C on the far hop leans it on the far ring; `stand()` takes an optional `pole: bool`; a pole dropped by a fall lies on the hop below for 20 s, then is back at a ring; no coins, nothing gates — the rope without the pole is exactly as run 164 left it; probe: the halving, the slower walk, the no-jump, the lay-down, the return.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-16 — the quests axis, second-thinnest at 69 open lines by `quest|task`; deeper, from run 164: a crossing leaves nothing behind) The crossing counted: `stand()` and `land()` read the loader's `on` changes as a crossing (stepped on from one ring's hop, stepped off onto the other's, no fall between) and keep a per-rope day count like the pit's `day_s`; the camp board's `note(n)` for the floors above gains "Tightrope, floor 32: crossed 3 times today, 1 fall"; the first fall-free crossing of a tower-day is saved under `records.climb_ropes[rope_id]` like `climb_camp` (the pack exposes `crossed()` and `restore()`; the owner session wires the save as it wires the loader), and the floor-30 camp sign says "A rope above. Walk it slowly." until the first saved crossing; no coins, no gate; probe: the count, the fall counted, the day reset, the restore, the sign's change.
- [ ] (Climb + weather, run 164 — wider, from the last twenty log lines: the chime (160), the sundial (157) and now the rope all read `wind_of(n)` and not one shows it before the body is on it) The wind pennant: a small cloth pennant on a short post by each rope's upper ring that streams with the floor's wind — the angle atan(wind / 120) from hanging to level, a 2 Hz flutter whose amplitude is the wind's, the Starlit accent — so the player reads the wind from the lower hop; on a windless floor it hangs; the same `props3d.gd` pennant goes on the chime's bracket and the sundial's gnomon post in the same run (one builder, three places); probe: the angle at 0 / 60 / 120, the flutter, the three places.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-16 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 18 open lines by whole-word keyword `interaction|interact`; deeper, from run 165: the ropewalker crosses beside you but nothing passes between you) The walker's cue: C within 20 px of an idle ropewalker at either ring asks for a crossing — it goes at once if the rope is empty (the same `_start_cross`, no new wait), and when the player's own crossing ends fall-free with the walker within 60 px the walker's next `flourish` is turned to the player (`look_yaw` toward the player's x during the dip, the pose reading meta "flourish_at" like `hark_side`), a second `flourish` from the player on C at the far ring answered by the walker's in turn — the first bow in the tower that is exchanged; nothing gates, nothing costs; probe: the cue, the turned bow, the exchange, the refusal while the player stands on the rope.
- [ ] (animals + Climb, 2026-10-16 — the animals axis, second-thinnest at 20 open lines by whole-word keyword `animal`; wider, from run 165: the rope has stars under it and nothing alive on it) The nightjar: a bird on the bird rig (`bird3d.gd` — bob, peck, flap, hop; the 2026-09-28 standard in full: idle breath and blink, a look at the player, a flee, and two species moves — a silent gape held 0.4 s, and the rope-hop: three hops along the slack rope from the lower ring when nobody has stood on it for 10 s, back to the ring post at the first `hum`) perched on the upper ring's hop by the ring, its eyes two accent points that catch the Starlit light; the `twang` of a fall sends it off with a flap into the dark and it is back on the post a tower-minute later; one per rope, built by `climb_tightrope.gd` under the rope's node; probe: the perch, the rope-hop and its interruption, the flap at the twang, the return.
- [ ] (relationships + Climb, 2026-10-16 — wider, from the last twenty log lines: the hurdler (156), the dial-keeper (158), the chime jumper (161), the leaper (163) and now the ropewalker are five tower residents with a label each and no name, no face and no mind) The tower's roster: each tower resident takes a handle, a face and a `resident_mind` from `residents.json`'s roster entries not placed in the town (one each, picked by floor number so the same floor always has the same person; the label shows the handle), says one `mind.line("tower")` category line when the player first lands within 40 px of it in a session ("Floor 32. I walk this one twice an hour."), and keeps `mind.memory` of the player's best on its piece — the pack exposes `people()` for the camp board (the owner session wires the save); no coins, no gate; probe: five handles, five distinct, the line once, the memory.

## Grows from Rolling log (run 166 — the forty-third Climb content pack and the first floor in the tower that rolls under your own walk: a loose log on the widest Old hall hop that carries you at 0.6 × your speed to a peg, spills when you stop, and drifts home when left; deepen it before a forty-fourth set-piece)
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-16 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 57 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 166: the log only ever moves under a body) The shove: C beside a stopped log on its hop (within 20 px, not riding, hands empty) gives it a two-handed push — a new `shove` pose (0.3 s windup: knees bend and both arms reach forward low, a 0.2 s drive, a 0.3 s recovery straightening up — never a still frame) — and the log rolls `kick` px away from the body over `kick_s` with the spill's own animation and the clonk; so a log that sits at the far peg can be sent back to the middle before a crossing instead of waiting the 6 s drift; the roller shoves it home the same way when it finds the log at its peg; probe: the push distance, the direction, the refusal while riding, the roller's shove.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, run 166 — deeper: the same `roll()` through data) The barrel: `kinds.barrel` in `log.json` for Old hall floors from 35 where no hop reaches 150 px — a hooped barrel (radius 12, length 24, `min_w` 120, `slow` 0.5, `stop_s` 0.1, two iron hoops in the accent, a `bung` on the side that turns with it) read by the same `plan()`/`roll()`/`build()` with the kind name as a parameter; the pose is `log` with meta log_v, the faster surface is the difference; the camp note names both ("1 ride on the log, 2 on the barrel"); probe: the plan on a 120 px hop, the quicker spill, the hoops.
- [ ] (Climb + weather, run 166 — wider, from the last twenty log lines: the pit's sand (162), the sundial's dial (157) and now the worn track are three surfaces that never change with the floor) The worn track: every ride darkens the track strip a step (albedo toward the ink scale, 12 steps, saved with the camp records by the owner session through an exposed `wear()`/`restore()`), and a rainy tower-day (the loader's rain flag, when it exists; until then `wind_of(n)` ≥ 100 stands in) wets the log — `slow` 0.5 and `stop_s` 0.1 for that day, the knock lower — so the Old hall floors read differently from one visit to the next; probe: the step per ride, the cap, the wet day's numbers.
- [ ] (vehicles + jobs, 2026-10-16 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 94 open lines by whole-word keyword; wider: the town's logs (woodcutting, run 90) move only in hands) The timber dray: a two-wheeled flatbed cart (`Car3D` spec "dray" — no cab, a low bed with four stakes, the slowest car in the town) parked by the woodcutter's pile; a builder resident (the road-1 builder when it exists, else the woodcutter) loads three logs with the existing two-hand carry and `heave`, drives it down the main road to the tower-road construction lot and unloads them onto the plank stack — the first vehicle that carries the town's own produce, and a visible supply chain for the house that is being built; the player may ride on the bed as a passenger (existing passenger code) and may load a log they carry (C at the bed) for a coin from the builder (`prices.json` "log_delivery"); residents on the road step aside for it as for cars; probe: the three logs loaded, the trip, the stack's count, the coin.
- [ ] (interactions + relationships, run 167 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 56 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 167: the roller only ever rides on its own clock) The roller's cue: C beside the idle log-roller at its home (within 20 px, hands empty, the log not in its spill grace) asks for a ride — a dry line above its head from a three-line pool in the pack's json ("After you.", "Mind the pegs.", "It rolls either way.") and the ride starts 0.8 s later whatever the minute says; if the player then mounts the log first, the roller stops at its 12 px wait as now and says a second line once ("Go on, then."); a cue while the roller is already walking does nothing; probe: the ask, the line, the start time, the wait line, the refusal mid-walk.
- [ ] (vehicles + motions + jobs, run 167 — the vehicles axis, second-thinnest at 59 open lines by whole-word keyword; wider, from the owner's board: the wheelbarrow sheet) The wheelbarrow: a one-wheel barrow (`Car3D` spec "barrow": one spoked wheel, two handles, a tray, no engine — it moves only while a body holds the handles) parked at the tower-road construction lot; the builder wheels three planks at a time from the stack to the frame in a new `barrow` pose (hands low on the handles, back bent, short steps, the tray bobbing with each step — 0.3 s lift, the push, 0.3 s set-down; never a still frame), the player takes the handles with C (hands empty) and wheels it anywhere on the lot at WALK × 0.8, planks the player carries drop into the tray with C at the tray (three fit), and a resident who finds the barrow outside the lot wheels it back; probe: the lift, the pose, the three planks, the player's steering, the return.
- [ ] (Climb + quests + motions, run 167 — wider, from the last twenty log lines: every resident half so far only mirrors the player, none asks anything of them) The roller's challenge: C at the roller's home with the log settled asks for a match — the roller rides first (its normal ride), the camp board then carries a note for one tower-minute ("Beat the roller: 72 px without a spill, floor n."), and a spill-free player ride at least as long as the roller's within that minute earns a new `salute` pose from the roller (two fingers to the brow: 0.2 s up, 0.4 s hold with a nod, 0.2 s down) and a line; a spill ends the match with a different line; no coins, no prize gate, the plain ride unchanged; probe: the ask, the note and its minute, the win, the spill, the salute's timing.

## Grows from Pin wheel (run 168 — the forty-fourth Climb content pack and the first platform in the tower that moves in steps instead of flowing: a brass escape wheel in the widest Clockwork gap whose eight rim pins jolt one 45° step every 0.7 s, carrying a body from the lower hop's level up over the top to a tap-jump under the upper hop and round again if it stays; deepen it before a forty-fifth set-piece)
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-17 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 44 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 168: the wheel never waits for anyone) The pawl: C from the upper hop within 24 px of the anchor (hands empty, not riding) pinches the anchor still — a new `pinch` pose (both hands up to the pallet, the shoulders hunched, a 0.3 s reach, the grip, a shiver each missed tick — never a still frame) — and the wheel holds its step for up to 3 s (`steps()` reads a per-wheel `held_from`/`held_s` and freezes the beat; the ticks fall silent; it resumes with a double tick), so a second climber below (poz_net) can board the bottom pin without the beat; letting go, walking or jumping ends it; the rider never needs it; the plain ride is untouched; probe: the hold length, the frozen pin, the silent ticks, the double tick, the refusal while riding.
- [ ] (Climb + motions, run 168 — deeper: the same `plan()`/`ride()` through data) The chime wheel: `kinds.chime` in `escape.json` for Clockwork floors from 85 — twelve pins (30° steps, tick_s 0.5, step_s 0.12), each pin a small bell (a short cone under the cap) struck by a hammer on the anchor as it passes the top, so the wheel plays the floor's twelve-note round once a turn (the chime pack's `_tone_wav` reused through a tiny shared `ClimbTones` static); read by the same `plan()`/`ride()`/`build()` with the kind name as a parameter; the pose is `jolt`, the faster beat is the difference; the camp note names both ("2 rides over the top, 1 on the chime wheel"); probe: the plan on floor 85, the twelve notes in order, the beat.
- [ ] (economy + Climb, 2026-10-17 — the economy axis, second thinnest at 50 open lines; wider, from the last twenty log lines: the board counts — hurdles, dial, chime, pit, log and now the wheel — six tallies nobody pays for) The clockkeeper's bounty: the Clockwork camp board (floors 15, 50, 85 …) posts one coin prize a tower-day for the first climber who rides the wheel over the top on every wheel of that theme span (`today()` per wheel already knows; a `claimed_day` on the board, the prize through `town_growth.gd`'s existing Climb prize path, never a gate — the wheels work the same unpaid), the board's line names the floors still owed ("Pin wheels ridden: 16, 18 — 17 owed"), the rider never claims it; probe: the tally per wheel, the claim once, the next day's reset.
- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-17 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 16 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 169: the rider and the player never meet on the wheel) Boarding behind the rider: when the player lands on a pin while the rider is on the wheel (`rider.phase` ride or poise), the rider looks back over its shoulder (`look_yaw` toward the player's pin for 1 s, then forward) and at the top gives way — holds `poise` one extra beat (`yield_t`, the pin steps past the top and the rider rides it round once more) so the player's pin reaches the top first; if the player jumps off before the top the rider goes on its own beat; the plain ride is untouched (100%); `probe_escape` gains the look-back, the extra beat and the no-yield when the player leaves.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-17 — the quests axis, second thinnest at 37 open lines; the tower has tallies and no asks) The rider's errand: the Clockwork camp board (floors 15, 50, 85 …) posts one line a tower-day, "The rider on floor N has had no company today — ride the pin wheel over the top", for the nearest wheel above the board whose `me_t` is older than a tower-day; the player's ride over the top marks it "done" on the board line (`climb_signs.notes`, through `today()` per wheel) and the rider follows within 1 s as now; no coins (the clockkeeper's bounty is the paid line); probe: the line, the done mark, the reset at the next tower-day, never two errands at once.
- [ ] (Climb + hygiene, 2026-10-17 — from runs 163–169: three tower residents now each carry their own copy of the loader's jump — the leaper's `land_x` push, the ropewalker's `_leap`, the rider's `_fit` with the integrator bias) `ClimbJump`: one static `fit(ox, oy, tx, ty, dt)` → {vx, vy, fly_t, charge_s} in `scripts/games/climb_jump.gd` (JUMP_MIN..JUMP_V by charge, RUN cap, the G·dt/2 of the semi-implicit step handled once) — **landed in run 171 with `over(apex, dy, dt)` beside it; the rider calls `fit` (identical arithmetic, `probe_escape` unchanged) and the high jumper calls `over`.** Still open: the ropewalker's `_leap` and the leaper's `_leap` move onto it (the leaper keeps its push-back but reads the same flight time); `probe_pit`/`probe_tightrope` keep their values (the ropewalker's landing may move by a tick — write the new numbers).

## Grows from High-jump bar (run 170 — the forty-fifth Climb content pack and the first thing in the tower that measures height: two iron uprights with three pegs on a Wind cliffs hop, a light crossbar on the floor's notch (1.6, 2.0 or 2.4 m) and a mat past it, cleared by a charged jump whose feet pass over the bar, knocked onto the mat by one that does not; deepen it before a forty-sixth set-piece)
- [x] (Climb + motions, run 170 — the resident half; the owner's rule: every interaction you give players, the residents must also do on their own; due by the alternation of runs 149–170) The jumper: one per bar (`scripts/games/climb_jumper.gd` — a Stick3D under the bar's node, built and stepped by `climb_bar.gd` like the hurdler and the rider), standing at the entry edge; once a tower-minute, and 1 s after the player's own flight at that bar ends (`flight()` writes `me_t` when `count` is true), it charges the jump its notch needs (`ClimbJump.fit` from the hygiene item under Pin wheel — land it first or in the same run), runs the `run` px, flies through the same `flight()` with `count false` in the same `arch`, lands on the mat with the loader's squash and lies there a moment — a new `sprawl` pose (on the back on the mat: a 0.3 s flop, 1.2 s of breathing with one knee up and an arm over the eyes, a 0.4 s roll up to sitting — never a still frame) — then walks back round the uprights to the entry edge and turns with `wheel`; one jump in five is short on purpose and knocks the bar, and the jumper then sets it back by hand (the bar's self-reset waits while a jumper is on its way, so it only serves bars without one); the player's clear is counted, the jumper's never; `probe_bar` gains the jumper's trail, the sprawl's timing, the knock-and-set-back and the wait while the player is in flight. — **done 2026-10-17 (run 171):** `scripts/games/climb_highjumper.gd` (`ClimbHighJumper` — `climb_jumper.gd`/`ClimbJumper` is the chime jumper of run 161, so the name differs from this sketch) built and stepped by `climb_bar.gd`, one per bar at the entry edge; once a tower-minute and 1 s after the player's flight (`flight()` now writes `me_t` when `count`) it charges exactly the notch's height through the new `ClimbJump.over()` (`scripts/games/climb_jump.gd` — the hygiene static, the rider ported to `ClimbJump.fit()` with identical arithmetic), walks the run-up at WALK (ice accel), flies through the same `flight()` with `count false` in `arch`, lands on the mat top with the loader's squash facing the bar and lies in the new `sprawl` (`stick3d_sprawl.gd`: 0.3 s flop, breathing to 1.5 s with one knee up and an arm over the eyes, 0.4 s roll up to sitting), then walks out to the lane in front of the uprights and back to the entry edge and turns with `wheel`; every fifth jump is short (apex 10 px under the bar — the head knocks it), the bar's self-reset waits while `held`, and the jumper sets it back by hand in the new `reset` (`stick3d_reset.gd`: 0.2 s reach, 0.6 s lift = the bar's lift_s with the pack's own lift animation, 0.2 s hands off) — a bar the player knocked and left is set back the same way (walk under the uprights, `wheel`, `reset`); its clears are never counted; `probe_bar` gains the JUMPER lines (the trail, the 64 px apex, the 1.9 s sprawl, the lane round the uprights, the short jump's 46 px and the set-back, the player's knock fetched, the 1 s follow, the 0.1 s wait and the 25 s held wait).
