- [ ] (interactions + Climb, 2026-10-17 — the interactions axis, thinnest at 58 open lines by whole-word keyword; deeper, from run 170: the bar's height is the floor's, never yours) Raising the bar: C at either upright (within 16 px, standing, hands empty, the bar up) lifts the crossbar to the next peg — the `reset` pose of run 171 (`stick3d_reset.gd`: hands to the bar, a 0.6 s lift with the knees straightening, hands off; the jumper's set-back uses it — give it a `reset_down` meta or a second phase so the top peg brings the bar down to the lowest with the same motion reversed; the jumper then jumps the new height, since it reads `h` at charge time), the bar's `h` changes for everyone on that floor (poz_net sends the peg index; the jumper jumps the new height), the camp note names the heights cleared today instead of one best ("1 of 2 bars cleared today — 2.4 m on 20"), and the floor's own notch returns at the next tower-day; the plain jump across the hop is untouched (100%); probe: the three pegs round, the note, the reset at day's end, the refusal while the bar is down or a body is in flight.
- [ ] (quests + Climb, 2026-10-17 — the quests axis; the tower has tallies and still no asks: the Wind cliffs board counts hurdles and bars and asks for neither) The meet: the Wind cliffs camp board (floors 20, 55, 90 …) posts one line a tower-day, "Meet today: vault every rail and clear every bar on floors N–N+4", drawn from `ClimbHurdle.vaulted_today()` and `ClimbBar.cleared_today()`; when both read complete the line turns to "Meet done — N rails, N bars" with the hour, the hurdler and the jumper on those floors each run once in salute, and nothing is paid (the clockkeeper's bounty is the paid line; a meet is for the board); probe: the line, the done mark, the salute runs, the reset at the next tower-day, never two meets at once.
- [ ] (Climb + weather, 2026-10-17 — deeper, from run 170: the bar trembles in the wind but the jump does not know it) Wind on the bar: on windy floors (from 55 — `wind_of`) the camp note adds the wind ("the highest 2.4 m, into a 90 wind"), a clear made against the floor's wind reads as the next notch up for the note only (never for a prize), and the bar's quiver grows into a rattle on its pegs above 150 px/s (a soft tick every 0.37 s from the existing `thump` wave at −24 dB); probe: the note's wind, the against-wind credit, the rattle's onset, nothing below 55.
- [ ] (Climb + jobs, 2026-10-17 — deeper, from run 171: the mat takes every landing and never changes) Mat wear and the mat-beater: every landing on a mat (the player's through `leave(fig)` after a flight that was `in_flight`, the jumper's through `_land`) adds one to the bar's `dents`; the mat box sinks 1 px per five landings to −4 px and its top stripe wrinkles (a 0.3 s sag in `_place` of the fallen bar's lie height follows the dent, so a knocked bar lies lower on a worn mat); at the next tower-day a mat-beater (one per Wind cliffs camp floor — 20, 55, 90 …, a Stick3D that walks up from the camp board to each bar on the five floors above, 1 floor a tower-minute) stands at the mat's far end and beats it — a new `beat` pose (a carpet beater in the right hand: 0.3 s raise over the shoulder, four 0.25 s strokes with the torso following and a dust puff per stroke from the existing `Jump3D.dust`, 0.3 s lower — never a still frame) — and the dents go to 0 and the mat rises; the player can take the beater from the camp board hook (C, hands empty) and beat a mat first, which the camp note names ("mats beaten today: 3 of 5, one by you"); the beater and the jumper never share the mat (the jumper waits in `idle` while `beating` is set on its bar); probe: the dent count, the sink, the beater's round, the pose timing, the player's beat, the note, the jumper's wait.

## Wishes from the town (the town writes these itself, one per patrol)
- [ ] (owner, 2026-09-30) Resident minds exist (`game/scripts/resident_mind.gd`, `game/data/minds.json`): traits, four needs, mood, memory of the player (saved to user://minds.json), friend/rival ties, gossip. Next slices: minds for the other 116 roster handles (voice + likes), residents remembering each other's hits, moods visible in gait/posture (slump when tired, brisk when cheerful), and residents seeking out a friend by name instead of only meeting at spots.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-03) Add a small unmanned 'community pantry' stall on the street (near the bakery) with a pantry volunteer who quietly refills shelf items and lets any passing resident take one food token when low.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-03) Add a small boatsmith booth at the park edge (near the river entrance) where a boatsmith resident will accept one damaged oar or wet boot, sit to mend it with a short timer, and return a repaired item with a repair tag.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-06) Add a public compost heap in the park that accepts one food-scrap item per resident and, after a short timer, returns a compost token usable to fertilize a town garden plot.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-06) Add a shared broom-rack on the square so residents can take a broom, perform a short sweeping action that clears nearby litter tokens, and return the broom to earn a small community token.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-06) Add a Lost & Found ledger booth interaction so players/residents can deposit a single found item (fountain or ground) into the ledger and reclaim it by name after a short delay.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-07) A boules circle on the park gravel where idle residents take turns with an underhand toss towards a wooden jack, letting a player take a turn from the ring to contest the nearest marker. (moved here on run 122 from the owner 'Money is real' section where the town wrote it; in `game/` this is the 3D town's first turn-based game — the web's bocce under 'Turn-based games' never crossed over — so it stands as its own item: a gravel ring in the park, a wooden jack, three boules each, an underhand `toss` pose, residents play in pairs through `mind.befriend()`, the player takes a turn with C and the nearest boule wins; no coins.)
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-08) When two walking residents pass within two metres of each other, trigger a short reciprocal overhead murmur from a shared pool of dry greetings or weather complaints, putting both on a thirty-second chatter cooldown. (moved here on run 122 from the owner section; this is the web-era 'Passing words' system asked for in `game/` — one slice: a `mind.line("pass")` pool in `minds.json`, the 30 s cooldown on each mind, both lines above the heads for 1.5 s, no stop in the walk.)
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-08) When a resident interacts with a notice podium, nearby idle residents move within two metres to form a small audience and display a brief agreement bubble. (moved here on run 126 from the owner "Money is real" section where the town wrote it; in `game/` this is the noticeboard of run 100 — one slice: a `pin` or `scan` at the board pulls up to three idle residents within 8 m to a half-ring 2 m off through a `mind.score` term "audience", each says `mind.line("agree")` once, three deadpan lines in `minds.json`, no stop longer than 4 s; the player pinning a note draws the same ring.)
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-13) Residents buying from the bakery or café carry a visible cup or parcel in one hand, pause every half-minute for a bite or sip animation, and discard the crumpled wrapper when passing within two metres of a bin. (moved here on run 153 from the owner "Money is real" section where the town wrote it; in `game/` this is the takeaway of runs 72–86 carried out of the shop — one slice: a resident leaving the bakery or café counter with a bought item keeps it in `carrying` (the existing make_item bun/cup), pauses every 30 s of walking for the existing `eat`/`drink` with `mind.ate()` on the last bite, and the emptied item becomes a wrapper that the first bin within 2 m takes with the existing `put`; `probe_meals` gains the carried-out item and the binned wrapper.)
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-16) Allow residents carrying a piece of sidewalk chalk to draw a temporary hopscotch grid on paving, where passing idle residents execute a three-step hop-and-turn animation until the chalk washes away in two minutes. (moved here on run 168 from the owner "Money is real" section where the town wrote it; a temporary chalk grid is the walk-up variant of "teach 2"'s permanent one under "Elders teaching children" — build it as that slice's second grid, drawn by a child with a `chalk` item in a `scrawl` pose, not as a separate system)

## Later — phone / app game (owner 2026-10-01: "모바일 최적화는 나중에(앱 게임)") — do not pick these until the owner says so
- [ ] (owner, mobile 1) Touch controls for the Godot game (web export on phones): a new `game/scripts/touch_ui.gd` CanvasLayer shown only when `DisplayServer.is_touchscreen_available()` — a left-thumb virtual joystick (drag anywhere on the left 45% of the screen; feeds `move_left/right/up/down` with analog strength via `Input.action_press(action, strength)`) and four round buttons on the right (SPACE jump, X punch, Z kick, C use) that press/release the same input actions, so town_player, cars (throttle/steer) and Climb all work unchanged. Multi-touch (joystick + button at once). Works in phone landscape and portrait, respects safe-area insets (`DisplayServer.get_display_safe_area()`), buttons ≥ 64 px. Hide the key legend line on touch devices. Verify headless (no errors) and with a probe that injects `InputEventScreenTouch`/`InputEventScreenDrag` and checks the player moves.
- [ ] (owner, mobile 2) Mobile quality preset: when `OS.has_feature("web_android") or OS.has_feature("web_ios") or DisplayServer.is_touchscreen_available()`: directional shadows off, `WorldGen.RADIUS` 2 instead of 3, residents 20 instead of 32, MSAA off, render scale 0.75 (`get_viewport().scaling_3d_scale`). One place (`town_base` const + a `quality_low` var) so later systems read it.
- [ ] (owner, mobile 3) HUD and labels readable on a phone: the help line, toasts, name tags and speech labels scale with `get_viewport().get_visible_rect().size` (min 14 px text); Climb HUD the same.

## Owner priority (2026-10-06): Climb first, the road to it, houses that get built — pick these before anything else, in order
Owner: "climb를 먼저 잘 만들고 미니게임을 만들게끔 되야하고 마을 확장도 계속 진행되야하고 climb쪽까지 가는 길에도 컨텐츠들이 생겨야겠지 집들도 건축하고 막". The session works on Climb itself; CI builds the road and the construction loop. One slice per run.
- [ ] (owner, road 1) The tower road gets a construction site: on the gravel path from the lane (x 15.5, z −13) to the tower (16, −76), at z ≈ −30 on the east side, a fenced lot with stakes and string, a stack of planks and a sawhorse. A new job "builder" (one resident, door job like baker): by day the builder carries a plank from the stack (two-hand carry pose) to the frame and hammers (existing `hammer` pose). Each finished shift adds one stage to the lot (0 stakes → 1 floor → 2 walls frame → 3 roof frame → 4 finished house via `_house`, a new door). Stage persists in user:// like records. When finished, the next roster handle moves in (resident appears at the door, says a line). The player can help: C at the frame with a plank in hand adds progress. Residents stop to watch (spot kind "watch").
- [ ] (owner, road 2) A rest stop halfway up the road (z ≈ −50): a stone well (C: drink — a cup, `drink` pose), a bench, a signpost with arrows ("CLIMB", "TOWN", "LAKE", "CABIN") that point at the real places, two lamps. Residents walking to the tower stop here.
- [ ] (owner, road 3) The road itself becomes a street: three more lots along it (alternating sides, z −38, −58, −66) that start as empty fenced lots with a "Plot for sale" sign; the builder works the lowest-stage lot after the first house is done, so the street fills over days. No money involved (owner: no currency for building).
- [ ] (owner, road 4) Trees, flower verges and a small orchard along the road (apple trees you can shake, existing `_tree`), a hedge where the road meets the tower plaza, and a bird or two on the fence posts.
- [ ] (owner, growth) Generalise lots: `town_build` gets `_lot(at, yaw)`; any district can declare lots. The weekly vision run proposes new lots in the open world (near the cabin, by the lake) so the town keeps widening by construction instead of by fiat.

## Owner priority (2026-10-06, later the same day): the roads to Climb and Race must be busy, then Racing 2.0 — pick these right after the "road" items above
Owner: "Climb, 레이싱까지 가는 길이라던지 이런 곳에 마을 엄청 활성화 되어있어야 해" and "레이싱은 지형이 험악해야하고 카트라이더를 생각하면 돼 맵도 여러개여야겠지". The Climb mini-game itself is now the tower-world (owner session) — do not edit `scripts/games/climb.gd`.
- [ ] (owner, busy 1) People on the roads: residents' routines include the open-world sites as real destinations — each day some residents walk the road to the tower to watch the door (spot "watch" at the plaza), some walk to the lakeside for fishing/benches, some to the cabin fire. Add `mind.score` terms so curious/social residents go further; at least 6 residents are on the tower road and 6 on the lake road at any daytime moment (probe that counts them).
- [ ] (owner, busy 2) A bus: a resident-driven bus (`Car3D` kind "van" painted as a bus, or a new spec) runs a loop town square → tower road stop → lakeside stop → cabin stop → square, with bus-stop shelters (bench + sign + timetable label) at each stop. Residents wait at stops and ride (seated inside, visible through the glass shader), the player can ride as a passenger (existing passenger code) and get off at a stop.
- [ ] (owner, busy 3) Roadside life between the town and the lake: a farm stand at the junction (crates of apples you can take), a small café terrace with two tables, a fuel/garage pump outside the race garage where resident cars stop, street lamps every 15 m along both site roads, a few dog walkers and joggers (existing animals/run poses).
- [ ] (owner, race 1) Racing 2.0 framework: tracks become data (`game/data/tracks/*.json`: centre-line points with elevation, width, banking per point, surface kind, props). The garage shows a map board (C cycles maps, SPACE starts); each map keeps its own best lap on the garage sign. Port the current loop as map 1 "Lakeside Loop".
- [ ] (owner, race 2) Rugged terrain like KartRider: map 2 "Canyon Rush" — steep climbs and drops (±6 m), two jumps with real airtime (ramps), banked hairpins, narrow cliff edges with no rails over a drop (fall = respawn at last checkpoint after 1.5 s), loose-gravel patches that lower grip. Cars must handle air and landings well (Car3D `_vy`, landing squash, no tunnelling).
- [ ] (owner, race 3) Map 3 "Forest Switchback": tight S-bends between trees, bumpy root sections (small random bumps in the road mesh), a log-bridge over a creek, a shortcut through a muddy trail (slow but shorter).
- [ ] (owner, race 4) KartRider feel: a drift gauge that fills while drifting and grants a boost charge (N2O-style, up to 2 stored, SPACE+↑ to fire), boost pads on the track, a start countdown with a "start boost" if you press ↑ on GO, and rivals that also drift and boost. Lap/position HUD, minimap.
- [ ] (owner, race 5) Map 4 "Snow Peak" (icy low-grip corners, a tunnel, a ski-jump) and map 5 "Night Harbour" (lit by lamps, cargo containers as walls, a ferry ramp jump).

## Owner priority (2026-10-06, night): Money is real — then more and better Climb and Racing content
Owner: "진짜 화폐로 키우자", "Climb는 컨텐츠 자체의 질과 양을 늘리는 거고 레이싱도 마찬가지". Core is done (owner session): `data/prices.json`, saved wallet, counters charge and refuse, selling held items at any staffed counter, Climb/Race prizes (`town_growth.gd`). The Climb game itself is edited by the owner session — CI adds Climb *content* only through data/extra files listed below.
- [ ] (owner, climb content) Climb content packs as data: `game/data/climb/*.json` describes extra platform kinds and themed set-pieces the tower-world reads (e.g. "rope bridge", "conveyor gears", "ghost platforms that appear on beat", "updraft vents", "bouncy mushrooms", "rotating windmill blades", "falling icicles"), one pack per run, each with its own visuals and a probe; the owner session wires the loader. Shipped: run 118 `updraft` (`data/climb/updraft.json`, `scripts/games/climb_updraft.gd`, `stick3d_glide.gd`, `tools/probe_updraft.gd`; loader call sequence in the script header). Run 119 `mushroom` (`data/climb/mushroom.json`, `scripts/games/climb_mushroom.gd`, `stick3d_bounce.gd`, `tools/probe_mushroom.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `step_vy` goes before the landing check and sets `bounced`). Run 120 `ghost` (`data/climb/ghost.json`, `scripts/games/climb_ghost.gd`, `stick3d_lurch.gd`, `tools/probe_ghost.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick, `land(x, y, ny, z)` after the loader's own landing test comes up empty, `holds(on)` while standing on a `ghost` kind (false → `on = {}` and `lurch`), `fading(on)` → `teeter`). Run 121 `bridge` (`data/climb/bridge.json`, `scripts/games/climb_bridge.gd`, `stick3d_sway.gd`, `tools/probe_bridge.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick, `land(x, y, ny, z)` after the loader's own landing test comes up empty (the returned dict's `y` is the deck height at that x; `step` true means a step down from a hop, so vx may be kept), `y = stand(on, x, dt, fig)` every tick while `on["kind"] == "bridge"` plus `pose_request = "sway"`, cleared on leaving). Run 122 `windmill` (`data/climb/windmill.json`, `scripts/games/climb_windmill.gd`, `stick3d_ride.gd`, `tools/probe_windmill.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick, `var r := ride(on, x, dt, fig)` at the 'move'-platform spot before `ny` is computed while `on["kind"] == "windmill"` (x and y come from the cup; `off` true → `on = {}`), `land(x, y, ny, z)` after the loader's own landing test comes up empty (`arm` is which sail, `step` a walk-down from a hop), `sweep(x, y, z)` while airborne → vx/vy, hurt 0.4). Run 123 `icicle` (`data/climb/icicle.json`, `scripts/games/climb_icicle.gd`, `stick3d_duck.gd`, `tools/probe_icicle.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick, `var hit := strike(x, y, z, on.is_empty())` after the landing/standing step (`vy = minf(vy, hit["vy"])`, `hurt = hit["hurt"]`, `lurch` when airborne — cleared by `LurchPoses.done`), while standing and not lurching `near(x, y, z)` → `pose_request = "duck"` plus `DuckPoses.hold(fig)`, else `DuckPoses.done(fig)` clears it after its 0.3 s release). Run 124 `gears` (`data/climb/gears.json`, `scripts/games/climb_gears.gd`, `stick3d_tread.gd`, `tools/probe_gears.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick, at the 'move'-platform drift spot `elif cg.belt_of(on): x += cg.carry(on, dt, fig, dir); fig.pose_request = "tread"` (the loader's own off-the-edge check empties `on`; clear the pose when `on` is empty or has no belt), optionally `vx = dir * RUN + cg.boost(on)` when a jump is released, and while airborne `sweep(x, y, z)` → vx/vy, hurt 0.4). Run 125 `pendulum` (`data/climb/pendulum.json`, `scripts/games/climb_pendulum.gd`, `stick3d_dangle.gd`, `tools/probe_pendulum.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick; airborne with Up and no hold caught, `var g := cp.grab(x, y, z)` fills a new `swinging` dict like `holding`; every hanging tick `var r := cp.hang_on(swinging, fig)` sets x/y and `pose_request = "dangle"` with the holds' stamina drain; on release `cp.let_go(swinging)` adds the bar's vx/vy to the 0.9 jump or the drop; while airborne and not hanging `sweep(x, y, z)` → vx/vy, hurt 0.4). Run 126 `raft` (`data/climb/raft.json`, `scripts/games/climb_raft.gd`, `stick3d_wade.gd`, `tools/probe_raft.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick, `land(x, y, ny, z)` after the loader's own landing test comes up empty (the returned dict's x/w are the raft's span now and y its top now), at the 'move'-platform drift spot while `on["kind"] == "raft"`: `var r := cr.stand(on, x, dt, fig, dir); x = r["x"]; y = r["y"]; fig.pose_request = "wade"; if r["off"]: on = {}` — the loader's own off-the-edge check is not used for a raft because it moves; clear the pose when `on` is empty). Run 127 `ladder` (`data/climb/ladder.json`, `scripts/games/climb_ladder.gd`, `stick3d_rail.gd`, `tools/probe_ladder.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick; in `_after_step`'s C chain after the sign and the camp door: `elif not locked and Input.is_action_just_pressed("act") and not on.is_empty(): var g := cl.grab(on, x, z); if not g.is_empty(): riding = g; on = {}; vx = 0.0; vy = 0.0; charge = 0.0` (an empty dict means the ladder was called over from the far end, or C was out of reach); every riding tick at the top of the step like `holding`: `var r := cl.ride_on(riding, fig); x = r["x"]; y = r["y"]; apex = y; face = r["face"]; fig.pose_request = "rail"; if r["done"] or r["lost"]: riding = {}` — the done tick's x/y stand on the far hop so the loader's own landing test takes over; SPACE released: `var v := cl.let_go(riding)` adds `v["vx"]` to the 0.9 hold jump; Down: `vx = v["vx"]; vy = 0.0; riding = {}`). Run 128 `bell` (`data/climb/bell.json`, `scripts/games/climb_bell.gd`, `stick3d_haul.gd`, `tools/probe_bell.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick; airborne with Up after the loader's own `_try_grab` comes up empty, `var g := cb.grab(x, y, z)` fills a new `hauling` dict like `holding` (empty while the rope is mid-cycle or within 0.4 s of being let go); every hanging tick `var r := cb.hang_on(hauling, fig)` sets x/y/face and `pose_request = "haul"` with the holds' 0.09/s stamina drain; on release `var v := cb.let_go(hauling)` — SPACE adds `maxf(0.0, v["vy"])` to the 0.9 hold jump, Down or no stamina keeps `minf(0.0, v["vy"])`; the rope finishes its cycle empty). Run 129 `vine` (`data/climb/vine.json`, `scripts/games/climb_vine.gd`, `stick3d_part.gd`, `tools/probe_vine.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick; on a standing or airborne tick after vx is set and before `x += vx * dt`: `var r := cv.part(x, y, z, fig); if not on.is_empty(): vx *= float(r["f"])`, then `if r["in"]: fig.pose_request = "part"; PartPoses.hold(fig)` / `elif fig.pose_request == "part" and PartPoses.done(fig): fig.pose_request = ""`; airborne with vy < 0 after the loader's own landing test comes up empty: `var c := cv.catch(x, y, ny, z, vy); if not c.is_empty(): clinging = c; on = {}; vx = 0.0; vy = 0.0; charge = 0.0; apex = y` (`clinging` is a new dict like `holding`); every clinging tick at the top of the step: `var r := cv.cling(clinging, fig); x = r["x"]; y = r["y"]; apex = y; fig.pose_request = "part"; if r["done"] or r["lost"]: clinging = {}; vy = 0.0` — the done tick's y is the hop's top so the loader's own landing test takes over; SPACE released: `var v := cv.let_go(clinging)` then the 0.9 hold jump; Down: `vy = v["vy"]; clinging = {}`). Run 130 `dumbwaiter` (`data/climb/dumbwaiter.json`, `scripts/games/climb_dumbwaiter.gd`, `stick3d_heave.gd`, `tools/probe_dumbwaiter.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `sync(_t)` each tick; in `_after_step`'s C chain after the sign and the camp door: `elif not locked and Input.is_action_just_pressed("act") and not on.is_empty(): var g := cd.grab(on, x, z); if not g.is_empty(): lifting = g; on = {}; vx = 0.0; vy = 0.0; charge = 0.0` (an empty dict means the crate was called over from the far end, or C was out of reach); every lifting tick at the top of the step like `holding`: `if Input.is_action_just_pressed("act"): cd.heave(lifting)` then `var r := cd.ride_on(lifting, fig); x = r["x"]; y = r["y"]; apex = y; fig.pose_request = "heave"; if r["done"] or r["lost"]: lifting = {}; fig.pose_request = ""` — the done tick's x/y stand on the far hop so the loader's own landing test takes over; SPACE released: `var v := cd.let_go(lifting)` adds `maxf(0.0, v["vy"])` to the 0.9 hold jump; Down: `vy = minf(0.0, v["vy"]); lifting = {}`). Run 131 `kite` (`data/climb/kite.json`, `scripts/games/climb_kite.gd`, `stick3d_kite.gd`, `tools/probe_kite.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `ck.build(root, ck.plan(n, plats, wind_of(n)))` when a band is built (the plan needs the floor's wind), `sync(_t)` each tick; airborne with Up after the loader's own `_try_grab` and the other packs' grabs come up empty, `var g := ck.grab(x, y, z)` fills a new `kiting` dict like `holding` (empty while someone hangs or the kite is returning) and sets `face = float(g["dir"])`; every hanging tick `var r := ck.hang_on(kiting, fig)` sets x/y and `pose_request = "kite"` with the holds' 0.09/s stamina drain, `r["done"]` (the tick's y is the far hop's top — the loader's own landing test takes over) or `r["lost"]` (the tether ran out — fall from there) empties it; on release `var v := ck.let_go(kiting)` — SPACE adds `v["vx"]` to the 0.9 hold jump, Down or no stamina keeps `vx = v["vx"]; vy = 0.0`). Run 132 `bookcase` (`data/climb/bookcase.json`, `scripts/games/climb_bookcase.gd`, `stick3d_shunt.gd`, `tools/probe_bookcase.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `cs.build(root, cs.plan(n, plats, cl.plan(n, plats) + cd.plan(n, plats)))` when a band is built (the ladder's and the dumbwaiter's plans go in as `taken` so the three Library packs never share a spot), `sync(_t)` each tick; in the grounded walk branch before `target`: `var r := cs.push(on, x, z, dir, Input.is_action_pressed("act"), dt, fig); if r["pushing"]: x = r["x"]; vx = 0.0; fig.pose_request = "shunt"; elif fig.pose_request == "shunt": fig.pose_request = ""`; `landed = cs.land(x, y, ny, z)` after the loader's own landing test comes up empty (kind `shelf`, the case top); while `on["kind"] == "shelf"` at the 'move'-platform spot `var r := cs.stand(on, x, dt); x = r["x"]; y = r["y"]; if r["off"]: on = {}; apex = y`, and the loader's own leave test skips `shelf`). Run 133 `lantern` (`data/climb/lantern.json`, `scripts/games/climb_lantern.gd`, `stick3d_cling.gd`, `tools/probe_lantern.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `cn.build(root, cn.plan(n, plats, ci.plan(n, plats)))` when a band is built (the icicles' plan goes in as `taken` so the two Starlit packs never share a spot), `sync(_t)` each tick; airborne with Up after the loader's own `_try_grab` and the other packs' grabs come up empty, `var g := cn.grab(x, y, z)` fills a new `clinging` dict like `holding` and sets `face = float(g["dir"])`; every clinging tick `var r := cn.hang_on(clinging, fig)` sets x/y and `pose_request = "cling"` with the holds' 0.09/s stamina drain, `r["done"]` (the tick's y is the far hop's top — the loader's own landing test takes over) or `r["lost"]` empties it; on release `var v := cn.let_go(clinging)` — SPACE adds `maxf(0, v["vy"])` to the 0.9 hold jump and `v["vx"]` to the run, Down or no stamina keeps `vx = v["vx"]; vy = v["vy"]`). Run 134 `door` (`data/climb/door.json`, `scripts/games/climb_door.gd`, `stick3d_turn.gd`, `tools/probe_door.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `cr.build(root, cr.plan(n, plats, cb.plan(n, plats)))` when a band is built (the bell's plan goes in as `taken` so the two Old hall packs never share a spot), `sync(_t)` each tick; in the grounded walk branch before `target`: `var r := cr.push(on, x, z, dir, Input.is_action_pressed("act"), dt, fig); if r["riding"]: x = r["x"]; z = r["z"]; vx = 0.0; fig.pose_request = "turn"; elif fig.pose_request == "turn": fig.pose_request = ""` with the `z = PLAYER_Z` line skipped on a riding tick (the arc's z is that tick's place); `landed = cr.land(x, y, ny, z)` after the loader's own landing test comes up empty (kind `canopy`, the door's roof — a still step the loader's own leave test handles)). Run 135 `hammock` (`data/climb/hammock.json`, `scripts/games/climb_hammock.gd`, `stick3d_loll.gd`, `tools/probe_hammock.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `ch.build(root, ch.plan(n, plats, wind_of(n), cb.plan(n, plats) + cg.plan(n, plats) + cm.plan(n, plats)))` when a band is built (the rope bridge's, the ghost slab's and the mushroom's plans go in as `taken` so no gap is shared), `sync(_t)` each tick; in the air after the loader's own landing test comes up empty and vy < 0: `var c := ch.catch(x, y, ny, z, vy); if not c.is_empty(): clinging = c; on = {}; vx = 0.0; vy = 0.0; charge = 0.0; apex = y`; every lying tick at the top of the `_hold` spot: `var r := ch.cling(clinging, fig); x = r["x"]; y = r["y"]; apex = y; fig.pose_request = "loll"; if r["lost"]: clinging = {}; fig.pose_request = ""`; SPACE released: `var v := ch.let_go(clinging, true); vy = v["vy"]; vx = dir * RUN * 0.9; clinging = {}` and the pose cleared (the net throws — the jump charge is not mixed in); Down: `var v := ch.let_go(clinging, false); vy = v["vy"]; vx = v["vx"]; clinging = {}`). C within 44 px of the gap edge blows (puff): the tufts leave one by one from 0.5 s after the C, one every 0.2 s, drifting at the wind's speed and rising 20 px/s for 3 s, then pop; an airborne body at its apex or falling whose hands (feet + 58) come within 26 px of a live tuft hangs from it (snap, the `kite` pose) and is carried for 1.5 s — 30 px higher than its own arc — then the tuft pops and lets it go with its speed (vx the drift, vy 20); `landed = cc.land(x, y, ny, z)` after the loader's own landing test comes up empty (kind "drawer"); while `on["kind"] == "drawer"` at the 'move'-platform spot: `var r := cc.stand(on, x, z, dt); y = r["y"]; z = r["z"]; if r["off"]: on = {}; apex = y` — on every Library floor, at the widest 70..220 px gap between two consecutive still hops 40..150 px apart in height whose box no other platform cuts and no ladder, dumbwaiter, bookcase, paternoster or scroll rack meets, a card catalogue set into the cliff 12 px past the lower hop's gap edge, 48 px wide, with drawers at 24 px steps from the lower hop's top up to 20 px under the upper hop (at most six; Run 169 took the wheel's rider: the resident half (`scripts/games/climb_rider.gd` — a Stick3D under the wheel's node, built and stepped by `climb_escape.gd`; `ride()` writes `me_t` when `count` is true, the loader's call sequence is unchanged) and the `poise` pose (`stick3d_poise.gd`). The next climb-content run keeps the choice: the next "Grows from …" slice (the wheel's pawl, chime wheel, boarding behind the rider, the rider's errand and the `ClimbJump` hygiene; the log's shove, barrel, worn track, the roller's cue and the roller's challenge; the rope's balance pole, crossing count, wind pennant, walker's cue, nightjar and the tower's roster; the pit's scar and rake, sand sparrow, kept mark, applause; the hurdles' rail cart, jackdaw and board's day; the telescope's stargazer; the sundial's board's hour, overcast hour and hour's call; the chime's hush, gusts, wind line, bead curtain, who-rang-it and wren), or a new set-piece on Wind cliffs (dandelion, hurdles, kite, vane, icicles — five, the only theme without a resident half yet) or Clockwork again (five, all with their resident halves now) — a set-piece is due by the alternation of runs 149–169. Run 170 took the high-jump bar: pack 45 (`data/climb/bar.json`, `scripts/games/climb_bar.gd`, `stick3d_arch.gd`, `tools/probe_bar.gd`; call sequence in the script header — `var kp := ck.plan(n, plats, wind_of(n)); var hop_top := cu.plan(n, plats) + ci.plan(n, plats) + kp; cb.build(root, cb.plan(n, plats, wind_of(n), hop_top + ch.plan(n, plats, hop_top) + cd.plan(n, plats, wind_of(n), kp + cv.plan(n, plats, wind_of(n), kp))))` with the vents, icicles, kite, hurdle row and dandelion as `taken`, `sync(_t)` each tick, airborne after the move `var r := cb.flight(ox, x, y, z, fig); if r["in_flight"]: fig.pose_request = "arch" elif fig.pose_request == "arch": fig.pose_request = ""`, on landing `if fig.pose_request == "arch": fig.pose_request = ""; cb.leave(fig)`; `signs.notes.append(cb.note)` for the camp board; Wind cliffs was chosen over Clockwork because it was the only theme without a resident half and its five packs are all gap or lip pieces — the bar is the theme's second hop-top piece after the hurdles and takes the widest hop the row did not). Run 171 took the bar's jumper: the resident half (`scripts/games/climb_highjumper.gd` — `ClimbHighJumper`, built and stepped by `climb_bar.gd`, the loader has nothing to add; `flight()` now writes `me_t` when `count` so the jumper waits 0.1 s and follows 1 s after; the bar dict gains `held`/`me_t`/`jumper`), the `sprawl` and `reset` poses, and `scripts/games/climb_jump.gd` (`ClimbJump.fit`/`over` — the rider ported, the ropewalker and leaper still to come). The next climb-content run keeps the choice: the next "Grows from …" slice (the bar's raising, meet, wind and mat wear; the wheel's pawl, chime wheel, boarding behind the rider, the rider's errand and the rest of the `ClimbJump` hygiene; the log's shove, barrel, worn track, the roller's cue and the roller's challenge; the rope's balance pole, crossing count, wind pennant, walker's cue, nightjar and the tower's roster; the pit's scar and rake, sand sparrow, kept mark, applause; the hurdles' rail cart, jackdaw and board's day; the telescope's stargazer; the sundial's board's hour, overcast hour and hour's call; the chime's hush, gusts, wind line, bead curtain, who-rang-it and wren), or a new set-piece on a theme with few (Wind cliffs now six, Clockwork five, the Library and the Cloud sea four) — a set-piece is due by the alternation of runs 149–171.
- [ ] (owner, race content) Racing content: more tracks and features per the Racing 2.0 items above, plus cosmetic car paints bought with coins at the garage and per-track coin prizes.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-17) Allow residents with empty pockets to inspect a loose cobblestone or gutter drain, triggering a brief search pose with a small chance to pull out a dropped copper token or a bent washer.
- [ ] (town wish, 2026-10-17) Residents short on coins can check the job slate at the cooperage, pick up a timber crate, and carry it to a target market stall to earn a single copper coin.
